===== main.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\main.cpp 
#include <time.h>

#include "Engine/Core/framework.h"

#define APPLICATION_NAME L"X3DGP"

LRESULT CALLBACK window_procedure(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	framework* p{ reinterpret_cast<framework*>(GetWindowLongPtr(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA)) };
	return p ? p->handle_message(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam) : DefWindowProc(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
}

int WINAPI WinMain(_In_ HINSTANCE instance, _In_opt_  HINSTANCE prev_instance, _In_ LPSTR cmd_line, _In_ int cmd_show)
{
	srand(static_cast<unsigned int>(time(nullptr)));

#if defined(DEBUG) | defined(_DEBUG)
	_CrtSetDbgFlag(_CRTDBG_ALLOC_MEM_DF | _CRTDBG_LEAK_CHECK_DF);
	//_CrtSetBreakAlloc(####);
#endif

	WNDCLASSEXW wcex{};
	wcex.cbSize = sizeof(WNDCLASSEX);
	wcex.style = CS_HREDRAW | CS_VREDRAW;
	wcex.lpfnWndProc = window_procedure;
	wcex.cbClsExtra = 0;
	wcex.cbWndExtra = 0;
	wcex.hInstance = instance;
	wcex.hIcon = 0;
	wcex.hCursor = LoadCursor(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
	wcex.hbrBackground = (HBRUSH)(COLOR_WINDOW + 1);
	wcex.lpszMenuName = NULL;
	wcex.lpszClassName = APPLICATION_NAME;
	wcex.hIconSm = 0;
	RegisterClassExW(&wcex);

	RECT rc{ 0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT };
	AdjustWindowRect(&rc, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, FALSE);

	//HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, APPLICATION_NAME, L"",
	//	WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW ^ WS_MAXIMIZEBOX ^ WS_THICKFRAME | WS_VISIBLE,
	//	CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
	//	rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top, NULL, NULL, instance, NULL);

	HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, APPLICATION_NAME, L"",
		WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE,
		CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
		rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top, NULL, NULL, instance, NULL);

	ShowWindow(hwnd, cmd_show);

	framework framework(hwnd);
	SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA, reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(&framework));
	return framework.run();
}

#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>

class Camera
{
public:
    Camera() = default;
    ~Camera() = default;

    // ‰Šú‰»
    void Initialize(float screenWidth, float screenHeight);

    // ’‹“_İ’è
    void SetLookAt(
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& eye,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& target,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& up
    );

    // ƒp[ƒXƒyƒNƒeƒBƒuİ’è
    void SetPerspectiveFov(
        float fovY, float aspect,
        float nearZ, float farZ
    );

    // ƒQƒbƒ^[
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& GetView()        const { return view; }
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& GetProjection()  const { return projection; }
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& GetEye()           const { return eye; }
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& GetTarget()        const { return target; }
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& GetFront()         const { return front; }
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& GetRight()         const { return right; }
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& GetUp()            const { return up; }
    float                    GetViewingAngle()  const { return viewingAngle; }
    void                     SetViewingAngle(float v) { viewingAngle = v; }

private:
    DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 view = {};
    DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 projection = {};
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3   eye = {};
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3   target = {};
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3   up = {};
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3   front = {};
    DirectX::XMFLOAT3   right = {};
    float               viewingAngle = 40.0f;
};

===== JsonFileCache.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\JsonFileCache.h 
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <functional>
#include <fstream>
#include <filesystem>
#include <json.hpp>

template<typename T>
class JsonFileCache
{
public:
	T& GetOrLoad(const std::string& path, const std::function<void(T&, const nlohmann::json&)>& loadFunc)
	{
		std::error_code ec;
		auto writeTime = std::filesystem::last_write_time(path, ec);

		auto it = cache.find(path);
		if (it != cache.end())
		{
			// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹©‘Ì‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚éê‡‚ÍA¡‚ÌƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü•Ô‚·
			if (!ec && writeTime == it->second.lastWriteTime)
			{
				return it->second.data;
			}
			// XV‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½ê‡‚ÍAŠù‘¶‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒgƒŠ‚ğˆê’UÁ‚µ‚ÄA‰º‚ÌV‹K“Ç‚İ‚İ‚Öi‚Ş
			cache.erase(it);
		}

		// ƒ}ƒbƒv‚Ì’†‚É’¼Ú\’z‚µ‚Ä‚©‚ç“Ç‚İ‚Ş
		auto result = cache.try_emplace(path);
		Entry& entry = result.first->second;
		entry.lastWriteTime = writeTime;

		std::ifstream ifs(path);
		if (ifs.is_open())
		{
			try
			{
				nlohmann::json j;
				ifs >> j;
				loadFunc(entry.data, j);
			}
			catch (const std::exception&) {}
		}

		return entry.data;
	}

private:
	struct Entry
	{
		T data;
		std::filesystem::file_time_type lastWriteTime;
	};

	std::unordered_map<std::string, Entry> cache;
}; 
 
===== PhysicsDestroyQueue.h =====

#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include <PxPhysicsAPI.h>

// íœ‘Ò‚¿•¨—Actor‚Ìˆê•ÛƒLƒ…[
namespace PhysicsDestroyQueue
{
    inline std::vector<physx::PxActor*>& GetQueue()
    {
        static std::vector<physx::PxActor*> queue;
        return queue;
    }

    // ‰ğ•ú—\–ñ‚·‚é
    inline void Push(physx::PxActor* actor)
    {
        if (actor)
        {
            GetQueue().push_back(actor);
        }
    }


    // íœ‘Ò‚¿Controller‚Ìˆê•ÛƒLƒ…[
    inline std::vector<physx::PxController*>& GetControllerQueue()
    {
        static std::vector<physx::PxController*> queue;
        return queue;
    }

    // ‰ğ•ú—\–ñ‚·‚éiControllerj
    inline void Push(physx::PxController* controller)
    {
        if (controller)
        {
            GetControllerQueue().push_back(controller);
        }
    }
} 
 
===== PhysicsLayer.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\PhysicsLayer.h 
#pragma once
#include <PxPhysicsAPI.h>


// ƒJƒeƒSƒ‰ƒCƒY
namespace PhysicsLayer
{
    constexpr physx::PxU32 Static    = 1 << 0; // Ã“I„‘Ì
    constexpr physx::PxU32 Dynamic   = 1 << 1; // “®“I„‘Ì
    constexpr physx::PxU32 Character = 1 << 2; // ƒLƒƒƒ‰ƒNƒ^[ƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[

    // PxFilterDataì¬
    inline physx::PxFilterData MakeFilterData(physx::PxU32 layer)
    {
        return physx::PxFilterData(layer, 0, 0, 0);
    }
}

// ƒtƒBƒ‹ƒ^ƒŠƒ“ƒO
// ƒtƒBƒ‹ƒ^ƒŠƒ“ƒO
class PhysicsQueryFilter : public physx::PxQueryFilterCallback
{
public:
    explicit PhysicsQueryFilter(physx::PxU32 wantedLayers) : wantedLayers(wantedLayers) 
    {
    }

    physx::PxQueryHitType::Enum preFilter(
        const physx::PxFilterData& filterData,
        const physx::PxShape* shape,
        const physx::PxRigidActor* actor,
        physx::PxHitFlags& queryFlags) override
    {
        if (shape->getQueryFilterData().word0 & wantedLayers)
        {
            return physx::PxQueryHitType::eBLOCK;
        }
        return physx::PxQueryHitType::eNONE;
    }

    physx::PxQueryHitType::Enum postFilter(
        const physx::PxFilterData&, const physx::PxQueryHit&,
        const physx::PxShape*, const physx::PxRigidActor*) override
    {
        return physx::PxQueryHitType::eBLOCK;
    }

private:
    physx::PxU32 wantedLayers;
};

class PhysX
{
public:
	PhysX() = default;
	~PhysX() = default;

	PhysX(const PhysX&) = delete;                // ƒRƒs[ƒRƒ“ƒXƒgƒ‰ƒNƒ^‹Ö~

	PhysX(PhysX&&) noexcept = delete;            // ƒ€[ƒuƒRƒ“ƒXƒgƒ‰ƒNƒ^‹Ö~
	PhysX& operator=(PhysX&&) noexcept = delete; // ƒ€[ƒu‘ã“ü‹Ö~

	// ‰Šú‰»
	void Initialize();
	// I—¹ˆ—
	void Cleanup();

	// PxPhysicsæ“¾
	physx::PxPhysics* GetPhysics() { return physics; }
	// CPUƒfƒBƒXƒpƒbƒ`ƒƒæ“¾
	physx::PxDefaultCpuDispatcher* GetDispatcher() { return dispatcher; }
	// ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgƒ}ƒeƒŠƒAƒ‹æ“¾
	physx::PxMaterial* GetDefaultMaterial() { return defaultMaterial; }

	// ƒƒbƒVƒ…ƒf[ƒ^‚©‚çPxTriangleMesh‚ğ¶¬
	physx::PxTriangleMesh* CreateTriangleMesh(
		const void* vertexData, uint32_t vertexCount, uint32_t vertexStride,
		const uint32_t* indices, uint32_t triangleCount);

private:
	physx::PxDefaultAllocator      allocator;        // ƒAƒƒP[ƒ^
	physx::PxDefaultErrorCallback  errorCallback;    // ƒGƒ‰[ƒR[ƒ‹ƒoƒbƒN

	physx::PxPvd* pvd = nullptr;                     // PhysXVisualDebuggerÚ‘±
	physx::PxPvdTransport* pvdTransport = nullptr;   // PhysXVisualDebuggerÚ‘±—pƒgƒ‰ƒ“ƒXƒ|[ƒg

	physx::PxFoundation* foundation = nullptr;
	physx::PxPhysics* physics = nullptr;
	physx::PxDefaultCpuDispatcher* dispatcher = nullptr;
	physx::PxMaterial* defaultMaterial = nullptr;

};

===== Camera.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\Camera.cpp 
#include "Camera.h"

// ‰Šú‰»
void Camera::Initialize(float screenWidth, float screenHeight)
{
    SetLookAt(
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0, 5, 10),
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0, 0, 0),
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0, 1, 0)
    );
    SetPerspectiveFov(
        DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(viewingAngle),
        screenWidth / screenHeight,
        0.1f,
        1000.0f
    );
}

// ’‹“_İ’è
void Camera::SetLookAt(
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& eye,
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& target,
    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& up)
{
    DirectX::XMVECTOR Eye = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&eye);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR Target = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&target);
    DirectX::XMVECTOR Up = DirectX::XMLoadFloat3(&up);
    DirectX::XMMATRIX View = DirectX::XMMatrixLookAtLH(Eye, Target, Up);
    DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&this->view, View);

    // •ûŒüƒxƒNƒgƒ‹æ“¾
    this->right.x = view._11;
    this->right.y = view._21;
    this->right.z = view._31;

    this->up.x = view._12;
    this->up.y = view._22;
    this->up.z = view._32;

    this->front.x = view._13;
    this->front.y = view._23;
    this->front.z = view._33;

    this->eye = eye;
    this->target = target;
}

// ƒp[ƒXƒyƒNƒeƒBƒuİ’è
void Camera::SetPerspectiveFov(
    float fovY, float aspect,
    float nearZ, float farZ)
{
    DirectX::XMMATRIX Projection =
        DirectX::XMMatrixPerspectiveFovLH(fovY, aspect, nearZ, farZ);
    DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&projection, Projection);
}

void framework::update(float elapsedTime)
{
	ZoneScoped;

#ifdef _DEBUG
	// F1‚ÅRelease‚Æ“¯‚¶Œ©‚½–ÚiImGui–³‚µj‚ğƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
	static bool prevF1 = false;
	bool currF1 = (GetAsyncKeyState(VK_F1) & 0x8000) != 0;
	if (currF1 && !prevF1)
	{
		previewRelease = !previewRelease;
	}
	prevF1 = currF1;
#endif

	// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚½uŠÔAGame‚Ö‚Ì“ü—Í‚ª—LŒø‚Å‚ ‚ê‚ÎDebugƒ‚[ƒh‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	static bool prevRButton = false;
	bool currRButton = (GetAsyncKeyState(VK_RBUTTON) & 0x8000) != 0;
	if (currRButton && !prevRButton && IsGameInputActive())
	{
		editorContext.cameraMode = CameraMode::Debug;
	}
	prevRButton = currRButton;


	// SceneContext XV
	updateSceneContext();

	// ƒV[ƒ“XV
	sceneManager.Update(elapsedTime, sceneContext);

	// StudioXV
	for (auto& win : studioWindows)
	{
		win->Update(elapsedTime);
	}

	// ƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌŒ‹‰Ê‚ğcontext‚É”½‰f
	sceneContext.view = gameWindow.GetCamera().GetView();
	sceneContext.projection = gameWindow.GetCamera().GetProjection();
}

void framework::updateImGui()
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// Editor–³Œø‚ÍImGui©‘Ì‚ğˆêØg‚í‚È‚¢
	if (!IsEditorActive()) return;

	// ImGuiƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŠJn
	imguiManager.BeginFrame();

	// ƒV[ƒ“ImGui•`‰æ
	sceneManager.RenderImGui();

	// GameƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEiƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒO/ƒ^ƒu‰»‘ÎÛj
	gameWindow.DrawWindow("Game", vsync);

#ifdef _DEBUG
	if (auto* scene = sceneManager.GetCurrentScene())
	{
		projectWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
		hierarchyWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
		inspectorWindow.Draw(scene->GetRegistry(), editorContext);
	}

	// StudioOpenRequest‚ğˆ—
	if (editorContext.studioRequest.pending)
	{
		openStudioWindow(editorContext.studioRequest.assetPath, editorContext.studioRequest.graphPath);
		editorContext.studioRequest.pending = false;
		editorContext.studioRequest.assetPath = "";
		editorContext.studioRequest.graphPath = "";
	}
	for (auto& win : studioWindows)
	{
		std::string title = "Studio##" + std::to_string(win->GetWindowId());
		win->DrawWindow(title.c_str());
	}
	// •Â‚¶‚éƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg‚ªo‚½ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğíœ
	studioWindows.erase(
		std::remove_if(studioWindows.begin(), studioWindows.end(),
			[](const std::unique_ptr<StudioWindow>& w)
			{
				return w->IsCloseRequested();
			}),
		studioWindows.end()
	);
#endif
}

void framework::render(float elapsedTime)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// ‰æ–ÊƒNƒŠƒA
	graphics.Clear(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f);
	graphics.SetRenderTargets();

	// Game•`‰ææ‚ğGameWindow—pƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦‚Ä•`‰æ
	gameWindow.Begin(graphics.GetDeviceContext());
	sceneManager.Render(sceneContext);
	gameWindow.End(graphics.GetDeviceContext());


	if (IsEditorActive())
	{
		// Studio•`‰æ
		for (auto& win : studioWindows)
		{
			win->Render();
		}

		// ƒƒCƒ“RTV‚ğÄƒZƒbƒg
		graphics.SetRenderTargets();

		// ImGui•`‰æiGameƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚à‚±‚±‚ÅImGui::ImageŒo—R‚Å•\¦‚³‚ê‚éj
		imguiManager.EndFrame();
	}
	else
	{
		// Editor–³ŒøFGame‚ÌƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ğƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“‚ÅƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚Ö
		graphics.SetRenderTargets();
		graphics.PresentTexture(gameWindow.GetSRV());
	}

	// ‰æ–Ê”½‰f
	graphics.Present(vsync);
	
	FrameMark;
}

// ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŒvZ
void framework::calculate_frame_stats()
{
	static int frames = 0;
	static float time_tlapsed = 0.0f;

	frames++;

	if ((timer.time_stamp() - time_tlapsed) >= 1.0f)
	{
		float fps = static_cast<float>(frames);
		float mspf = 1000.0f / fps;

		updateCount = updateCountBuf;
		updateCountBuf = 0;

		std::ostringstream outs;
		outs.precision(6);
		//outs << "FPS : " << fps << " / " << "Frame Time : " << mspf << " (ms)";
		outs << "FPS : " << fps
			<< " / Frame Time : " << mspf << " ms"
			<< " / Update : " << updateCount << " fps";
		SetWindowTextA(hwnd, outs.str().c_str());

		frames = 0;
		time_tlapsed += 1.0f;
	}
}

//ƒAƒvƒŠƒP[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒ‹[ƒv
int framework::run()
{
	MSG msg{};

	// ŒÅ’èƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒXƒeƒbƒvİ’èi60fpsj
	const double fixedTimestep = 1.0 / 144.0;
	double accumulator = 0.0;

	while (WM_QUIT != msg.message)
	{
		if (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE))
		{
			TranslateMessage(&msg);
			DispatchMessage(&msg);
		}
		else
		{
			timer.tick();
			calculate_frame_stats();

			// Œo‰ßŠÔ‚ğ’~ÏiÅ‘å0.25•b‚ÅƒXƒpƒCƒN–h~j
			double frameTime = (double)timer.time_interval();
			if (frameTime > 0.25) frameTime = 0.25;
			accumulator += frameTime;

			// ŒÅ’èƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒXƒeƒbƒv‚Åupdate‚ğÀs
			while (accumulator >= fixedTimestep)
			{
				update((float)fixedTimestep);
				accumulator -= fixedTimestep;
				updateCountBuf++;
			}

			// ImGui‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€1‰ñ‚¾‚¯
			updateImGui();

			// render‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Às
			render((float)timer.time_interval());
		}
	}
	return static_cast<int>(msg.wParam);
}

//ƒƒbƒZ[ƒWƒnƒ“ƒhƒ‰
LRESULT framework::handle_message(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	// ImGuiƒƒbƒZ[ƒWˆ—
	if (imguiManager.HandleMessage(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam)) { return true; }

	switch (msg)
	{
	case WM_PAINT:
	{
		PAINTSTRUCT ps{};
		BeginPaint(hwnd, &ps);

		EndPaint(hwnd, &ps);
	}
	break;

	case WM_DESTROY:
		PostQuitMessage(0);
		break;
	case WM_CREATE:
		break;
	case WM_KEYDOWN:
		if (wparam == VK_ESCAPE)
		{
			PostMessage(hwnd, WM_CLOSE, 0, 0);
		}
		break;
	case WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE:
		timer.stop();
		break;
	case WM_SIZE:
		if (wparam == SIZE_MINIMIZED)
		{
			// Å¬‰»‚Íƒ^ƒCƒ}[’â~‚Ì‚İiƒŠƒTƒCƒY•s—vj
			timer.stop();
		}
		else
		{
			// Å‘å‰»/•œŒ³/’ÊíƒŠƒTƒCƒY
			timer.start();
			graphics.ResizeBuffers();
			updateSceneContext();
		}
		break;
	case WM_EXITSIZEMOVE:
		timer.start();
		// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒTƒCƒY•ÏX‚Éƒoƒbƒtƒ@Ä¶¬
		graphics.ResizeBuffers();
		updateSceneContext();
		break;
	default:
		return DefWindowProc(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
	}
	return 0;
}

// SceneContext ‚ğÅVó‘Ô‚ÉXV‚·‚é
void framework::updateSceneContext()
{
	sceneContext.device = graphics.GetDevice();
	sceneContext.deviceContext = graphics.GetDeviceContext();
	sceneContext.resourceManager = &resourceManager;
	sceneContext.physX = &physX;

	// GameWindow‚ª‚Âê—pƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ğ•`‰ææ‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“n‚·
	sceneContext.renderTargetView = gameWindow.GetRTV();
	sceneContext.depthStencilView = gameWindow.GetDSV();
	sceneContext.camera = &gameWindow.GetCamera();
	sceneContext.view = gameWindow.GetCamera().GetView();
	sceneContext.projection = gameWindow.GetCamera().GetProjection();
	sceneContext.screenWidth = gameWindow.GetRenderWidth();
	sceneContext.screenHeight = gameWindow.GetRenderHeight();
	sceneContext.cameraMode = editorContext.cameraMode;
	sceneContext.editorContext = &editorContext;
	sceneContext.cameraMode = editorContext.cameraMode;
	sceneContext.editorContext = &editorContext;
	sceneContext.gameInputActive = IsGameInputActive();
}

// Studio‚ğŠJ‚­
void framework::openStudioWindow(const std::string& assetPath, const std::string& graphPath)
{
	auto win = std::make_unique<StudioWindow>();
	win->Initialize(graphics.GetDevice(), &studioShared);
	win->GetContext().deviceContext = graphics.GetDeviceContext();
	win->SetWindowId(nextStudioWindowId++);

	// ƒpƒX‚ªw’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ğƒ[ƒh
	if (!assetPath.empty())
	{
		win->LoadAsset(assetPath);
	}

	// ƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÌƒpƒX‚ªw’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚ç•R•t‚¯ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	if (!graphPath.empty())
	{
		win->GetContext().attachedGraphPath = graphPath;
	}

	studioWindows.push_back(std::move(win));
}

bool framework::IsEditorActive() const
{
#ifdef _DEBUG
	return !previewRelease;
#else
	return false;
#endif
}

bool framework::IsGameInputActive() const
{
	return IsEditorActive() ? gameWindow.IsViewportHovered() : true;
}
 
 
===== GameWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\GameWindow.cpp 
#include "GameWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>
#include <cassert>

// ‰Šú‰»
void GameWindow::Initialize(ID3D11Device* dev, UINT width, UINT height)
{
    device = dev;

    // ƒJƒƒ‰‰Šú‰»
    camera.Initialize(static_cast<float>(width), static_cast<float>(height));

    ResizeRenderTarget(width, height);
}

// •`‰ææ‚ÌØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
void GameWindow::Begin(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
    if (!dc) return;

    // Œ»İ‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğ•Û‘¶
    UINT numVP = 1;
    dc->RSGetViewports(&numVP, &savedViewport);

    // ©‘O‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğƒZƒbƒg
    D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{};
    vp.Width = static_cast<float>(renderWidth);
    vp.Height = static_cast<float>(renderHeight);
    vp.MinDepth = 0.0f;
    vp.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
    dc->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);

    // •`‰ææ‚ğ©‘ORTV‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
    dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, rtv.GetAddressOf(), dsv.Get());

    // ƒNƒŠƒA
    float clear[4] = { 0.2f, 0.2f, 0.5f, 1.0f };
    dc->ClearRenderTargetView(rtv.Get(), clear);
    dc->ClearDepthStencilView(dsv.Get(), D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH | D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL, 1.0f, 0);
}
void GameWindow::End(ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
    if (!dc) return;

    // ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğŒ³‚É–ß‚·
    dc->RSSetViewports(1, &savedViewport);

    // •`‰ææ‚ğƒƒCƒ“‚É–ß‚·
    ID3D11RenderTargetView* nullRTV = nullptr;
    dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &nullRTV, nullptr);
}

// ImGui•`‰æ
void GameWindow::DrawWindow(const char* windowName, bool& vsync)
{
    ImGui::Begin(windowName, nullptr, ImGuiWindowFlags_MenuBar);

    // ƒƒjƒ…[ƒo[
    if (ImGui::BeginMenuBar())
    {
        ImGui::Checkbox("VSync", &vsync);

        ImGui::EndMenuBar();
    }

    // Gameê—p‚ÌDockSpace
    ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID("GameDockSpace");
    ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);

    // ‰‰ñƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgƒŒƒCƒAƒEƒg‚ğ‘g‚Ş
    if (firstDockSetup)
    {
        firstDockSetup = false;

        ImGui::DockBuilderRemoveNode(dockSpaceId);
        ImGui::DockBuilderAddNode(dockSpaceId, ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);
        ImGui::DockBuilderSetNodeSize(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(1280, 720));

        ImGuiID dockMain = dockSpaceId;
        ImGuiID dockBottom = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockMain, ImGuiDir_Down, dockRatioBottom, nullptr, &dockMain);
        ImGuiID dockLeft = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockMain, ImGuiDir_Left, dockRatioLeft, nullptr, &dockMain);
        ImGuiID dockRight = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
            dockMain, ImGuiDir_Right, dockRatioRight, nullptr, &dockMain);

        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Viewport", dockMain);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Hierarchy", dockLeft);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Inspector", dockRight);
        ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow("Project", dockBottom);

        ImGui::DockBuilderFinish(dockSpaceId);
    }

    // Viewportƒpƒlƒ‹
    ImGui::Begin("Viewport");

    ImVec2 region = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
    if (region.x > 1 && region.y > 1)
    {
        camera.SetPerspectiveFov(
            DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(camera.GetViewingAngle()),
            region.x / region.y,
            0.1f,
            1000.0f
        );
    }

    ImGui::Image(reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(srv.Get()), region);

    // ƒJƒƒ‰‘€ì‚ÌŠJn”»’è‚ÍViewport’P‘Ì‚Ìƒzƒo[‚¾‚¯‚ğŒ©‚é
    viewportHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered();

    ImGui::End(); // Viewport

    ImGui::End(); // Game
}

// ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ÌÄ¶¬
void GameWindow::ResizeRenderTarget(UINT width, UINT height)
{
    if (!device) return;
    if (width == 0 || height == 0) return;

    renderWidth = width;
    renderHeight = height;

    // ŒÃ‚¢ƒŠƒ\[ƒX‚ğ‰ğ•ú
    srv.Reset();
    rtv.Reset();
    renderTexture.Reset();
    dsv.Reset();
    depthTexture.Reset();

    HRESULT hr;

    // ƒJƒ‰[ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒÄ¶¬
    {
        D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
        desc.Width = renderWidth;
        desc.Height = renderHeight;
        desc.MipLevels = 1;
        desc.ArraySize = 1;
        desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT;
        desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
        desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_RENDER_TARGET | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;

        hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, renderTexture.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, rtv.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, srv.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
    }

    // [“xƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒÄ¶¬
    {
        D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
        desc.Width = renderWidth;
        desc.Height = renderHeight;
        desc.MipLevels = 1;
        desc.ArraySize = 1;
        desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT;
        desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
        desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL;

        hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, depthTexture.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(depthTexture.Get(), nullptr, dsv.GetAddressOf());
        assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
    }
}

void Graphics::Initialize(HWND hWnd)
{
	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒnƒ“ƒhƒ‹•Û‘¶
	this->hWnd = hWnd;

	// ƒNƒ‰ƒCƒAƒ“ƒg—ÌˆæƒTƒCƒYæ“¾

	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE“à•”ƒTƒCƒYæ“¾

	// ƒAƒ_ƒvƒ^[‘I‘ğ
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<IDXGIFactory1> factory;
	CreateDXGIFactory1(__uuidof(IDXGIFactory1), (void**)factory.GetAddressOf());

	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<IDXGIAdapter1> selectedAdapter;
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<IDXGIAdapter1> adapter;

	for (UINT i = 0; factory->EnumAdapters1(i, adapter.GetAddressOf()) != DXGI_ERROR_NOT_FOUND; i++)
	{
		DXGI_ADAPTER_DESC1 desc;
		adapter->GetDesc1(&desc);

		// ƒ\ƒtƒgƒEƒFƒAƒAƒ_ƒvƒ^[‚ÍœŠO

			adapter.Reset();

		}

		if (desc.VendorId == 0x10DE || desc.VendorId == 0x1002)

	hr = device->CreateTexture2D(
		&dsDesc,

// ‰æ–ÊƒNƒŠƒA
void Graphics::Clear(float r, float g, float b, float a)
{
	// ƒNƒŠƒAƒJƒ‰[ì¬
	float color[4]{ r, g, b, a };

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚ğw’èF‚ÅƒNƒŠƒA
	immediateContext->ClearRenderTargetView(renderTargetView.Get(), color);
	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹’l‰Šú‰»
	immediateContext->ClearDepthStencilView(
		depthStencilView.Get(),
		D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH |
		D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL,
		1.0f, // [“x‰Šú’l(1.0 = Å‰“)
		0     // ƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹‰Šú’l
	);
}

// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgİ’è
void Graphics::SetRenderTargets()
{
	// ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgİ’è
	immediateContext->RSSetViewports(1, &viewport);
	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg‚Æ[“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹‚ğOMƒXƒe[ƒW‚Éİ’è
	immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(1, renderTargetView.GetAddressOf(), depthStencilView.Get());
}

// w’èƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒ‚ğƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“‚ÅƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚Ö•`‰æ
void Graphics::PresentTexture(ID3D11ShaderResourceView* srv)
{
	if (!srv) return;

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@‚Ö•`‰æ
	immediateContext->RSSetViewports(1, &viewport);
	immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(1, renderTargetView.GetAddressOf(), nullptr);

	// ƒuƒŒƒ“ƒhE[“xƒXƒe[ƒg‚ğŠù’è‚É–ß‚·
	immediateContext->OMSetBlendState(nullptr, nullptr, 0xffffffff);
	immediateContext->OMSetDepthStencilState(nullptr, 1);

	// ƒVƒF[ƒ_[İ’è
	immediateContext->VSSetShader(presentVS.Get(), nullptr, 0);
	immediateContext->PSSetShader(presentPS.Get(), nullptr, 0);

	// “ü—ÍƒŒƒCƒAƒEƒg‚È‚µEƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“OŠpŒ`ƒXƒgƒŠƒbƒv
	immediateContext->IASetInputLayout(nullptr);
	immediateContext->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLESTRIP);
	immediateContext->RSSetState(presentRasterizer.Get());

	// ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒEƒTƒ“ƒvƒ‰[İ’è
	immediateContext->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &srv);
	immediateContext->PSSetSamplers(0, 1, presentSampler.GetAddressOf());

	// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“•`‰æ
	immediateContext->Draw(4, 0);

	// SRV‚Ì‘©”›‰ğœ
	ID3D11ShaderResourceView* nullSrv = nullptr;
	immediateContext->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &nullSrv);
}

// ‰æ–ÊƒvƒŒƒ[ƒ“ƒg
void Graphics::Present(bool vsync)
{
	//swapChain->Present(0, DXGI_PRESENT_ALLOW_TEARING); // í‚ÉƒeƒBƒAƒŠƒ“ƒO‹–‰Â

	if (vsync)
	{
		// VSync ONFƒ‚ƒjƒ^[‚ÌƒŠƒtƒŒƒbƒVƒ…ƒŒ[ƒg‚É“¯Šú
		swapChain->Present(1, 0);
	}
	else
	{
		// VSync OFFF‰Â”\‚ÈŒÀ‚è‘¬‚­•`‰æ
		swapChain->Present(0, DXGI_PRESENT_ALLOW_TEARING);
	}
}

// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
void Graphics::ToggleFullscreen()
{
	// ƒŠƒTƒCƒY’†
	isResizing = true;

	// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
	if (!isFullscreen)
	{
		// Œ»İƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEˆÊ’u•Û‘¶
		GetWindowRect(hWnd, &windowedRect);
		// Œ»İƒ‚ƒjƒ^[æ“¾
		HMONITOR monitor = MonitorFromWindow(hWnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
		// ƒ‚ƒjƒ^[î•ñæ“¾
		MONITORINFO monitorInfo{ sizeof(MONITORINFO) };
		GetMonitorInfo(monitor, &monitorInfo);
		// ƒ‚ƒjƒ^[‹éŒ`
		RECT& mr = monitorInfo.rcMonitor;

		// Š®‘Sƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“—p
		SetWindowLong(hWnd, GWL_STYLE, WS_POPUP | WS_VISIBLE); // ƒ^ƒCƒgƒ‹ƒo[‚â˜g‚È‚µ‚ÌŠ®‘Sƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“—pƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹

		// ƒ‚ƒjƒ^[‘S‘ÌƒTƒCƒY‚Ö•ÏX
		SetWindowPos(
			hWnd,
			HWND_TOP,
			mr.left, mr.top,
			mr.right - mr.left, mr.bottom - mr.top,
			SWP_FRAMECHANGED
		);

		// ƒtƒ‹ƒXƒNƒŠ[ƒ“’†
		isFullscreen = true;
	}
	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒ‚[ƒh‚Ö–ß‚·
	else
	{
		// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹‚É–ß‚·
		SetWindowLong(hWnd, GWL_STYLE, WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE);
		// •Û‘¶‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½ˆÊ’u‚Ö–ß‚·
		SetWindowPos(hWnd, HWND_TOP,
			windowedRect.left, windowedRect.top,
			windowedRect.right - windowedRect.left,
			windowedRect.bottom - windowedRect.top,
			SWP_FRAMECHANGED
		);

		// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE’†
		isFullscreen = false;
	}

	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEXV
	UpdateWindow(hWnd);

	// ƒoƒbƒtƒ@Ä¶¬
	ResizeBuffers();

	// ƒŠƒTƒCƒYI—¹
	isResizing = false;
}

// ƒoƒbƒtƒ@Ä¶¬
void Graphics::ResizeBuffers()
{
	// Œ»İƒNƒ‰ƒCƒAƒ“ƒgƒTƒCƒYæ“¾
	RECT rc;
	GetClientRect(hWnd, &rc);
	UINT w = rc.right - rc.left;
	UINT h = rc.bottom - rc.top;
	// Å¬‰»ƒXƒLƒbƒv
	if (w == 0 || h == 0) return;

	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg‰ğœ
	immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(0, nullptr, nullptr);

	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[‰ğ•ú
	renderTargetView.Reset();
	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹‰ğ•ú
	depthStencilView.Reset();

	// ƒXƒƒbƒvƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“Ä¶¬
	swapChain->ResizeBuffers(
		0,
		w, h,
		DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN,
		DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_FLAG_ALLOW_TEARING // í‚ÉƒeƒBƒAƒŠƒ“ƒO‹–‰Â
	);

	// ƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[Ä¶¬
	// VƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@æ“¾
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D> backBuffer;
	swapChain->GetBuffer(
		0,
		__uuidof(ID3D11Texture2D),
		reinterpret_cast<void**>(backBuffer.GetAddressOf())
	);
	// ƒoƒbƒNƒoƒbƒtƒ@—pƒŒƒ“ƒ_[ƒ^[ƒQƒbƒgƒrƒ…[¶¬
	device->CreateRenderTargetView(
		backBuffer.Get(),
		nullptr,
		renderTargetView.GetAddressOf()
	);

	// [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹ƒrƒ…[Ä¶¬
	// V[“xƒoƒbƒtƒ@¶¬
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11Texture2D> dsBuffer;
	D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC dsDesc{};
	dsDesc.Width = w;                   // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.Height = h;                  // ƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.MipLevels = 1;               // ƒ~ƒbƒv–³‚µ
	dsDesc.ArraySize = 1;               // ”z—ñƒTƒCƒY
	dsDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT; // Depth24 + Stencil8
	dsDesc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;        // MSAAİ’è
	dsDesc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT; // GPUg—p
	dsDesc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL; // [“xƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹—p“r

	// [“xƒoƒbƒtƒ@¶¬
	device->CreateTexture2D(
		&dsDesc,
		nullptr,
		dsBuffer.GetAddressOf()
	);

	// ƒfƒvƒXƒXƒeƒ“ƒVƒ‹ƒrƒ…[¶¬
	device->CreateDepthStencilView(
		dsBuffer.Get(),
		nullptr,
		depthStencilView.GetAddressOf()
	);

	// ‰æ–ÊƒTƒCƒYXV
	screenWidth = static_cast<float>(w);
	screenHeight = static_cast<float>(h);
	// ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgƒTƒCƒYXV
	viewport.Width = screenWidth;
	viewport.Height = screenHeight;
}

// I—¹ˆ—
void Graphics::Cleanup()
{
	if (immediateContext)
	{
		immediateContext->OMSetRenderTargets(0, nullptr, nullptr);
		immediateContext->ClearState();
	}

	renderTargetView.Reset();
	depthStencilView.Reset();
}

// PresentTexture—pƒŠƒ\[ƒX‚Ì‰Šú‰»
void Graphics::InitializePresentResources()
{
	// ’¸“_ƒVƒF[ƒ_[
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11InputLayout> dummyLayout;
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadVertexShader(
		device.Get(), "Data/Shader/Lighting_VS.cso",
		nullptr, 0, dummyLayout, presentVS
	);

	// ƒsƒNƒZƒ‹ƒVƒF[ƒ_[
	GpuResourceUtils::LoadPixelShader(
		device.Get(), "Data/Shader/Present_PS.cso", presentPS
	);

	// ƒTƒ“ƒvƒ‰[ƒXƒe[ƒg
	D3D11_SAMPLER_DESC samplerDesc{};
	samplerDesc.Filter = D3D11_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_LINEAR;
	samplerDesc.AddressU = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.AddressV = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
	samplerDesc.ComparisonFunc = D3D11_COMPARISON_NEVER;
	samplerDesc.MinLOD = 0;
	samplerDesc.MaxLOD = D3D11_FLOAT32_MAX;
	device->CreateSamplerState(&samplerDesc, presentSampler.GetAddressOf());

	// ƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ‰ƒCƒU[ƒXƒe[ƒg
	D3D11_RASTERIZER_DESC rasterizerDesc{};
	rasterizerDesc.FillMode = D3D11_FILL_SOLID;
	rasterizerDesc.CullMode = D3D11_CULL_NONE;
	rasterizerDesc.FrontCounterClockwise = FALSE;
	device->CreateRasterizerState(&rasterizerDesc, presentRasterizer.GetAddressOf());
}

#include "ImGuiManager.h"

ImGuiManager::~ImGuiManager()
{
	// I—¹‰»
	Shutdown();
}

// ‰Šú‰»
void ImGuiManager::Initialize(HWND hWnd, ID3D11Device* device, ID3D11DeviceContext* dc)
{
	IMGUI_CHECKVERSION();
	ImGui::CreateContext();

	ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();

	// ƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒO—LŒø‰»
	io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_DockingEnable;
	//ƒ}ƒ‹ƒ`ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg—LŒø‰»
	io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_ViewportsEnable;

	// “ú–{ŒêƒtƒHƒ“ƒgİ’è
	io.Fonts->AddFontFromFileTTF(
		"SystemData/Fonts/NotoSansJP-Regular.ttf",
		16.0f, nullptr, io.Fonts->GetGlyphRangesJapanese());

	ImGui_ImplWin32_Init(hWnd);
	ImGui_ImplDX11_Init(device, dc);

	ImGui::StyleColorsClassic(); // ƒNƒ‰ƒVƒbƒN
}

// ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŠJn
void ImGuiManager::BeginFrame()
{
	ImGui_ImplDX11_NewFrame();
	ImGui_ImplWin32_NewFrame();
	ImGui::NewFrame();

	// ƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒOƒXƒy[ƒXi‰æ–Ê‘S‘Ìj
	ImGuiWindowFlags dockFlags =
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDocking |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoTitleBar |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoResize |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBringToFrontOnFocus |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoNavFocus |
		ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBackground;

	ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
	ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(viewport->Pos);
	ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(viewport->Size);
	ImGui::SetNextWindowViewport(viewport->ID);

	ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowRounding, 0.0f);
	ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowBorderSize, 0.0f);
	ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowPadding, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));

	ImGui::Begin("DockSpace", nullptr, dockFlags);
	ImGui::PopStyleVar(3);

	// ƒhƒbƒLƒ“ƒOƒXƒy[ƒXID¶¬
	ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID("MainDockSpace");
	ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_PassthruCentralNode);

	ImGui::End();
}

// ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€I—¹/•`‰æ
void ImGuiManager::EndFrame()
{
	ImGui::Render();
	ImGui_ImplDX11_RenderDrawData(ImGui::GetDrawData());

	// ƒ}ƒ‹ƒ`ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒgXV
	ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
	if (io.ConfigFlags & ImGuiConfigFlags_ViewportsEnable)
	{
		ImGui::UpdatePlatformWindows();
		ImGui::RenderPlatformWindowsDefault();
	}
}

// I—¹‰»
void ImGuiManager::Shutdown()
{
	ImGui_ImplDX11_Shutdown();
	ImGui_ImplWin32_Shutdown();
	ImGui::DestroyContext();
}

// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEƒƒbƒZ[ƒWˆ—
bool ImGuiManager::HandleMessage(HWND hWnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wparam, LPARAM lparam)
{
	return ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(hWnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
}
 
 
===== PhysX.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\PhysX.cpp 
#include "PhysX.h"

using namespace physx;

// ‰Šú‰»
void PhysX::Initialize()
{
	// Foundatioì¬
	foundation = PxCreateFoundation(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, allocator, errorCallback);

#ifdef _DEBUG
	// PhysXVisualDebugger‚ÉÚ‘±
	pvd = PxCreatePvd(*foundation);
	pvdTransport = PxDefaultPvdSocketTransportCreate("127.0.0.1", 5425, 10);
	pvd->connect(*pvdTransport, physx::PxPvdInstrumentationFlag::eALL);
#endif

	// PxPhysics–{‘Ìì¬
	physics = PxCreatePhysics(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, *foundation, PxTolerancesScale(), true, pvd);

	// ƒVƒ~ƒ…ƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğCPUƒXƒŒƒbƒh‚ÉŠ„‚èU‚éƒfƒBƒXƒpƒbƒ`ƒƒ‚ğì¬
	dispatcher = PxDefaultCpuDispatcherCreate(1); // ƒXƒŒƒbƒh”‚P

	// ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgƒ}ƒeƒŠƒAƒ‹(Ã–€C, “®–€C, ”½”­ŒW”)ì¬
	defaultMaterial = physics->createMaterial(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.6f);
}
// I—¹ˆ—
void PhysX::Cleanup()
{
	// ì¬‚µ‚½‡‚Æ‹t‡‚É‰ğ•ú‚·‚é
	if (defaultMaterial) { defaultMaterial->release(); defaultMaterial = nullptr; }
	if (dispatcher)      { dispatcher->release();      dispatcher      = nullptr; }
	if (physics)         { physics->release();         physics         = nullptr; }
	if (pvd)             { pvd->disconnect();
				           pvd->release();             pvd             = nullptr; }
	if (pvdTransport)    { pvdTransport->release();    pvdTransport    = nullptr; }
	if (foundation)      { foundation->release();      foundation      = nullptr; }
}

// ƒƒbƒVƒ…ƒf[ƒ^‚©‚çPxTriangleMesh‚ğ¶¬
physx::PxTriangleMesh* PhysX::CreateTriangleMesh(
	const void* vertexData, 
	uint32_t vertexCount,
	uint32_t vertexStride,
	const uint32_t* indices,
	uint32_t triangleCount)
{
	// ƒƒbƒVƒ…î•ñ‘g‚İ—§‚Ä
	PxTriangleMeshDesc meshDesc;
	meshDesc.points.count = vertexCount;
	meshDesc.points.stride = vertexStride;
	meshDesc.points.data = vertexData;

	meshDesc.triangles.count = triangleCount;
	meshDesc.triangles.stride = 3 * sizeof(uint32_t);
	meshDesc.triangles.data = indices;

	// Cookingƒpƒ‰ƒ[ƒ^
	PxCookingParams cookingParams(physics->getTolerancesScale());

	// ƒƒbƒVƒ…¶¬
	return PxCreateTriangleMesh(
		cookingParams,
		meshDesc,
		physics->getPhysicsInsertionCallback()
	);
}
 
 
===== Scene.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\Scene.cpp 
#include "Scene.h"

// •¨—ƒV[ƒ“‚Ìì¬
void Scene::InitializePhysicsScene(PhysX& physX)
{
	// ƒV[ƒ“‘g‚İ—§‚Ä
	physx::PxSceneDesc sceneDesc(physX.GetPhysics()->getTolerancesScale());
	sceneDesc.gravity = physx::PxVec3(0.0f, -9.81f, 0.0f);
	sceneDesc.cpuDispatcher = physX.GetDispatcher();
	sceneDesc.filterShader = physx::PxDefaultSimulationFilterShader;

	pxScene = physX.GetPhysics()->createScene(sceneDesc);

	// PhysXVisualDebugger‚É‘—‚éî•ñ‚ğİ’è
	if (physx::PxPvdSceneClient* pvdClient = pxScene->getScenePvdClient())
	{
		pvdClient->setScenePvdFlag(physx::PxPvdSceneFlag::eTRANSMIT_CONSTRAINTS,  true);
		pvdClient->setScenePvdFlag(physx::PxPvdSceneFlag::eTRANSMIT_CONTACTS,     true);
		pvdClient->setScenePvdFlag(physx::PxPvdSceneFlag::eTRANSMIT_SCENEQUERIES, true);
	}

	// ƒLƒƒƒ‰ƒNƒ^[ƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[ƒ}ƒl[ƒWƒƒ[ì¬
	controllerManager = PxCreateControllerManager(*pxScene);
}

// •¨—ƒV[ƒ“‚Ì”jŠü
void Scene::FinalizePhysicsScene()
{
	if (controllerManager)
	{
		controllerManager->release();
		controllerManager = nullptr;
	}

	if (pxScene)
	{
		pxScene->release();
		pxScene = nullptr;
	}
}

// •¨—ƒV[ƒ“‚ÌXV
void Scene::UpdatePhysicsScene(float elapsedTime)
{
	pxScene->simulate(elapsedTime);
	pxScene->fetchResults(true);
} 
 
===== SceneManager.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Core\SceneManager.cpp 
#include "SceneManager.h"

// XV
void SceneManager::Update(float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	// ƒV[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦ˆ—
	if (nextScene)
	{
		// Œ»İƒV[ƒ“I—¹‰»
		if (currentScene)
		{
			currentScene->Finalize();
		}

		// ƒV[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦
		currentScene = std::move(nextScene);
		nextScene = nullptr;

		// ƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒg‚ğ“n‚µ‚Ä‰Šú‰»
		currentScene->Initialize(context);
	}

	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->Update(elapsedTime, context);
	}
}

// •`‰æ
void SceneManager::Render(const SceneContext& context)
{
	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->Render(context);
	}
}

// ImGui•`‰æ
void SceneManager::RenderImGui()
{
	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->RenderImGui();
	}
}

// ƒV[ƒ“Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦—\–ñ
void SceneManager::ChangeScene(std::unique_ptr<Scene> scene)
{
	// ŸƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ÅØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	nextScene = std::move(scene);
}

// ƒV[ƒ“ƒNƒŠƒA
void SceneManager::ClearScene()
{
	if (currentScene)
	{
		currentScene->Finalize();
		currentScene.reset();
	}
	nextScene.reset();
}

struct TransitionGraphData // ‘JˆÚ—pƒOƒ‰ƒt–{‘Ì
{

	// IDÌ”ÔƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[
	int* idCounter = nullptr;
	int GenerateId() { return (*idCounter)++; }

	// Linkì¬
	int CreateLink(int fromId, int toId);
	// Linkíœ
	void DeleteLink(int linkId);
};



class AnimatorGraphData

	// “Ç‚İ‚ñ‚¾ID‚ÉƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ’Ç]‚³‚¹‚é
	void SyncIdCounter(int id);

	// IDÌ”ÔƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ÌÀ‘Ì
	int idCounter = 1;
};

// ”ÍˆÍ : ŠJn‚©‚çI—¹‚ÌŠÔí‚Étrue‚ğ•Ô‚·
struct SequencerRange
{
	int id = -1;
	float start = 0.0f; // ŠJnˆÊ’u
	float end = 1.0f;   // I—¹ˆÊ’u
	SequencerFlag flag = SequencerFlag::AnimExit;
};

// uŠÔ : ’Ê‰ß‚µ‚½‚»‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯true
struct SequencerPoint
{
	int id = -1;
	float time = 0.0f; // 
	SequencerFlag flag = SequencerFlag::AnimExit;
};

// 1ƒNƒŠƒbƒv•ª‚ÌRange/Point‚ğ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚½‚à‚Ì
struct SequencerClipData
{
	std::string clipName;
	std::vector<SequencerRange> ranges;
	std::vector<SequencerPoint> points;

	int nextId = 1;
	int GenerateId() { return nextId++; }
};

// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹1‘Ì•ªA‘SƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ÌSequencerİ’è‚ğ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚½‚à‚Ì
class SequencerData

	SequencerClipData* FindOrCreateClip(const std::string& clipName);

===== AnimationSpeedData.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Data\AnimationSpeedData.cpp 
#include "AnimationSpeedData.h"
#include <algorithm>

// w’è‚µ‚½ƒNƒŠƒbƒv–¼‚Ìƒf[ƒ^‚ğ’T‚·
AnimationSpeedClipData* AnimationSpeedData::FindOrCreateClip(const std::string& clipName)
{
	for (auto& clip : clips)
	{
		if (clip.clipName == clipName) return &clip;
	}

	AnimationSpeedClipData newClip;
	newClip.clipName = clipName;

	// n’[/I’[‚ÌŒÅ’èƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğ1.0”{‚Å”z’u‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
	AnimationSpeedPoint startPoint;
	startPoint.id = newClip.GenerateId();
	startPoint.time = 0.0f;
	startPoint.speed = 1.0f;
	startPoint.isEndpoint = true;
	newClip.points.push_back(startPoint);

	AnimationSpeedPoint endPoint;
	endPoint.id = newClip.GenerateId();
	endPoint.time = 1.0f;
	endPoint.speed = 1.0f;
	endPoint.isEndpoint = true;
	newClip.points.push_back(endPoint);

	clips.push_back(newClip);
	return &clips.back();
}

// ³‹K‰»ŠÔ‚É‚¨‚¯‚é‘¬“x‚ğüŒ`•âŠ®‚Å‹‚ß‚é
float AnimationSpeedClipData::EvaluateSpeed(float normalizedTime) const
{
	if (points.empty()) return 1.0f;

	// ƒ\[ƒg—pˆê”z—ñ
	std::vector<const AnimationSpeedPoint*> sorted;
	sorted.reserve(points.size());
	for (const auto& p : points) sorted.push_back(&p);
	std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end(),
		[](const AnimationSpeedPoint* a, const AnimationSpeedPoint* b) { return a->time < b->time; });

	// —¼’[
	if (normalizedTime <= sorted.front()->time) return sorted.front()->speed;
	if (normalizedTime >= sorted.back()->time)  return sorted.back()->speed;

	// ‹²‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚é2“_‚ğ’T‚µ‚ÄüŒ`•âŠÔ
	for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < sorted.size(); ++i)
	{
		const AnimationSpeedPoint* a = sorted[i];
		const AnimationSpeedPoint* b = sorted[i + 1];
		if (normalizedTime >= a->time && normalizedTime <= b->time)
		{
			float range = b->time - a->time;
			float t = (range > 0.0f) ? (normalizedTime - a->time) / range : 0.0f;
			return a->speed + (b->speed - a->speed) * t;
		}
	}

	return 1.0f;
}

// •Û‘¶
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimationSpeedData::ToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json j;
	j["clips"] = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& clip : clips)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
		jc["clipName"] = clip.clipName;
		jc["nextId"] = clip.nextId;

		jc["points"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& p : clip.points)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jp;
			jp["id"] = p.id;
			jp["time"] = p.time;
			jp["speed"] = p.speed;
			jp["isEndpoint"] = p.isEndpoint;
			jc["points"].push_back(jp);
		}

		j["clips"].push_back(jc);
	}

	return j;
}

// “Ç‚İ‚İ
void AnimationSpeedData::FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	clips.clear();

	if (!j.contains("clips")) return;

	for (const auto& jc : j["clips"])
	{
		AnimationSpeedClipData clip;
		clip.clipName = jc.value("clipName", "");
		clip.nextId = jc.value("nextId", 1);

		if (jc.contains("points"))
		{
			for (const auto& jp : jc["points"])
			{
				AnimationSpeedPoint p;
				p.id = jp.value("id", -1);
				p.time = jp.value("time", 0.0f);
				p.speed = jp.value("speed", 1.0f);
				p.isEndpoint = jp.value("isEndpoint", false);
				clip.points.push_back(p);
			}
		}

		clips.push_back(clip);
	}
} 
 
===== AnimatorGraphData.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Data\AnimatorGraphData.cpp 
#include "AnimatorGraphData.h"
#include <algorithm>

// == enum‚Ì•¶š•ÏŠ· == //
namespace
{
	// ===== enum‚Ì•¶š•ÏŠ·(StateƒOƒ‰ƒtŠÖ˜A) =====

	// ChildStateType(qŠK‘w‚Ìí—Ş)
	std::string ToString(ChildStateType type)
	{
		switch (type)
		{
		case ChildStateType::Normal: return "Normal";
		case ChildStateType::Start:  return "Start";
		case ChildStateType::End:    return "End";
		}
		return "Normal";
	}
	ChildStateType ToChildStateType(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Start") return ChildStateType::Start;
		if (s == "End")   return ChildStateType::End;
		return ChildStateType::Normal;
	}


	// ParentStateType(eŠK‘w‚Ìí—Ş)
	std::string ToString(ParentStateType type)
	{
		switch (type)
		{
		case ParentStateType::Entry: return "Entry";
		case ParentStateType::State: return "State";
		}
		return "State";
	}
	ParentStateType ToParentStateType(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Entry")
		{
			return ParentStateType::Entry;
		}
		return ParentStateType::State;
	}


	// ===== enum‚Ì•¶š•ÏŠ·(ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒtŠÖ˜A) =====

	// ValueType(•Ï”ƒm[ƒh‚ª‚Â’l‚ÌŒ^)
	std::string ToString(ValueType type)
	{
		switch (type)
		{
		case ValueType::Int:    return "Int";
		case ValueType::Float:  return "Float";
		case ValueType::Bool:   return "Bool";

		case ValueType::Float2: return "Float2";
		case ValueType::Float3: return "Float3";
		case ValueType::Float4: return "Float4";
		default:                return "Float";
		}
	}
	ValueType ToValueType(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Int")    return ValueType::Int;
		if (s == "Bool")   return ValueType::Bool;
		if (s == "Float2") return ValueType::Float2;
		if (s == "Float3") return ValueType::Float3;
		if (s == "Float4") return ValueType::Float4;
		return ValueType::Float;
	}


	// FloatComponent(\‘¢‘Ì‚Ì‚Ç‚Ì¬•ª‚ğæ‚èo‚·‚©)
	std::string ToString(FloatComponent c)
	{
		switch (c)
		{
		case FloatComponent::X: return "X";
		case FloatComponent::Y: return "Y";
		case FloatComponent::Z: return "Z";
		case FloatComponent::W: return "W";
		default:                return "None";
		}
	}
	FloatComponent ToFloatComponent(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "X") return FloatComponent::X;
		if (s == "Y") return FloatComponent::Y;
		if (s == "Z") return FloatComponent::Z;
		if (s == "W") return FloatComponent::W;
		return FloatComponent::None;
	}

	// CompareOp(”äŠr‰‰Zq‚Ìí—Ş)
	std::string ToString(CompareOp op)
	{
		switch (op)
		{
		case CompareOp::Greater:      return "Greater";
		case CompareOp::Less:         return "Less";
		case CompareOp::Equal:        return "Equal";
		case CompareOp::NotEqual:     return "NotEqual";
		case CompareOp::GreaterEqual: return "GreaterEqual";
		case CompareOp::LessEqual:    return "LessEqual";
		default:                      return "Greater";
		}
	}
	CompareOp ToCompareOp(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Less")         return CompareOp::Less;
		if (s == "Equal")        return CompareOp::Equal;
		if (s == "NotEqual")     return CompareOp::NotEqual;
		if (s == "GreaterEqual") return CompareOp::GreaterEqual;
		if (s == "LessEqual")    return CompareOp::LessEqual;
		return CompareOp::Greater;
	}

	// LogicOp(˜_—‰‰Zq‚Ìí—Ş)
	std::string ToString(LogicOp op)
	{
		switch (op)
		{
		case LogicOp::And: return "And";
		case LogicOp::Or:  return "Or";
		default:           return "And";
		}
	}
	LogicOp ToLogicOp(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "Or") return LogicOp::Or;
		return LogicOp::And;
	}
}


// eŠK‘w‚ÌState‚ğì¬‚·‚é
int ParentStateGraph::CreateParentState(const std::string& name, float posX, float posY)
{
	ParentState state;
	state.id = GenerateId();
	state.name = name;
	state.posX = posX;
	state.posY = posY;
	state.type = ParentStateType::State;

	// V‚µ‚­ì‚Á‚½State‚ÌqŠK‘wƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÉAˆÄ“à”Â‚ğ”zü‚·‚é
	state.childGraph.idCounter = this->idCounter;
	// ”zü‚ªI‚í‚Á‚Ä‚©‚çAStart/End‚ğ•ÛØ‚·‚é
	state.childGraph.EnsureStartAndEndExist();

	states.push_back(state);

	return state.id;
}
// eŠK‘w‚ÌLink‚ğì¬‚·‚é
int ParentStateGraph::CreateParentLink(int fromId, int toId)
{
	AnimLink link;
	link.id = GenerateId();
	link.fromId = fromId;
	link.toId = toId;

	// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É‚àˆÄ“à”Â‚ğ”zü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çg‚¤
	link.condition.idCounter = this->idCounter;
	// ‚±‚ÌLinkê—p‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÉAŒÅ’è‚ÌReturnƒm[ƒh‚ğ1‚Â”z’u‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
	link.condition.returnNode.id = link.condition.GenerateId();
	link.condition.returnNode.posX = 500.0f;
	link.condition.returnNode.posY = 200.0f;

	stateLinks.push_back(link);
	return link.id;
}

// eŠK‘w‚ÌState‚ğíœ‚·‚é
void ParentStateGraph::DeleteParentState(int stateId)
{
	states.erase(
		std::remove_if(states.begin(), states.end(),
			[stateId](const ParentState& s)
			{
				// Entry‚Ííœ‚³‚¹‚È‚¢
				if (s.type == ParentStateType::Entry) return false;
				return s.id == stateId;
			}),
		states.end()
	);

	// ‚±‚ÌState‚ÉŒq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½ƒŠƒ“ƒN‚àˆê‚Éíœ‚·‚é
	stateLinks.erase(
		std::remove_if(stateLinks.begin(), stateLinks.end(),
			[stateId](const AnimLink& l)
			{
				return l.fromId == stateId || l.toId == stateId;
			}),
		stateLinks.end()
	);
}
// eŠK‘w‚ÌLink‚ğíœ‚·‚é
void ParentStateGraph::DeleteParentLink(int linkId)
{
	stateLinks.erase(
		std::remove_if(stateLinks.begin(), stateLinks.end(),
			[linkId](const AnimLink& l) { return l.id == linkId; }),
		stateLinks.end()
	);
}

// Entry‚ª‚È‚¯‚ê‚Îì¬‚·‚é
void ParentStateGraph::EnsureEntryExists()
{
	bool hasEntry = false;
	for (const auto& s : states)
	{
		if (s.type == ParentStateType::Entry)
		{
			hasEntry = true;
			break;
		}
	}

	if (!hasEntry)
	{
		ParentState entry;
		entry.id = GenerateId();
		entry.name = "Entry";
		entry.posX = 50.0f;
		entry.posY = 200.0f;
		entry.type = ParentStateType::Entry;

		// Entry‚ÌqŠK‘wƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É‚àˆÄ“à”Â‚¾‚¯‚Í”zü‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­(Start/End‚Í•ÛØ‚µ‚È‚¢)
		entry.childGraph.idCounter = this->idCounter;

		states.push_back(entry);
	}
}


// qŠK‘w‚ÌState‚ğì¬‚·‚é
int ChildStateGraph::CreateChildState(const std::string& name, float posX, float posY)
{
	ChildState state;
	state.id = GenerateId();
	state.name = name;
	state.posX = posX;
	state.posY = posY;
	state.type = ChildStateType::Normal;

	children.push_back(state);

	return state.id;
}
// qŠK‘w‚ÌLink‚ğì¬‚·‚é
int ChildStateGraph::CreateChildLink(int fromId, int toId)
{
	AnimLink link;
	link.id = GenerateId();
	link.fromId = fromId;
	link.toId = toId;

	// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É‚àˆÄ“à”Â‚ğ”zü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çg‚¤
	link.condition.idCounter = this->idCounter;
	// ‚±‚ÌLinkê—p‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÉAŒÅ’è‚ÌReturnƒm[ƒh‚ğ1‚Â”z’u‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
	link.condition.returnNode.id = link.condition.GenerateId();
	link.condition.returnNode.posX = 500.0f;
	link.condition.returnNode.posY = 200.0f;

	childLinks.push_back(link);

	return link.id;
}

// qŠK‘w‚ÌState‚ğíœ‚·‚é
void ChildStateGraph::DeleteChildState(int stateId)
{
	children.erase(
		std::remove_if(children.begin(), children.end(),
			[stateId](const ChildState& s)
			{
				// Start/End‚Ííœ‚³‚¹‚È‚¢
				if (s.type == ChildStateType::Start || s.type == ChildStateType::End) return false;
				return s.id == stateId;
			}),
		children.end()
	);

	// ‚±‚ÌState‚ÉŒq‚ª‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½Link‚àˆê‚Éíœ‚·‚é
	childLinks.erase(
		std::remove_if(childLinks.begin(), childLinks.end(),
			[stateId](const AnimLink& l)
			{
				return l.fromId == stateId || l.toId == stateId;
			}),
		childLinks.end()
	);
}
// qŠK‘w‚ÌLink‚ğíœ‚·‚é
void ChildStateGraph::DeleteChildLink(int linkId)
{
	childLinks.erase(
		std::remove_if(childLinks.begin(), childLinks.end(),
			[linkId](const AnimLink& l) { return l.id == linkId; }),
		childLinks.end()
	);
}

// Start/End‚ª‚È‚¯‚ê‚Îì¬‚·‚é
void ChildStateGraph::EnsureStartAndEndExist()
{
	bool hasStart = false;
	bool hasEnd = false;

	for (const auto& child : children)
	{
		if (child.type == ChildStateType::Start) hasStart = true;
		if (child.type == ChildStateType::End)   hasEnd = true;
	}

	if (!hasStart)
	{
		CreateChildState("Entry", 50.0f, 200.0f);
		children.back().type = ChildStateType::Start;
	}

	if (!hasEnd)
	{
		CreateChildState("Exit", 400.0f, 200.0f);
		children.back().type = ChildStateType::End;
	}
}


// ƒRƒ“ƒXƒgƒ‰ƒNƒ^
AnimatorGraphData::AnimatorGraphData()
{
	// idCounter‚ÌÀ‘Ì‚ğAparentGraph‚ÌˆÄ“à”Â‚É”zü‚·‚é
	parentGraph.idCounter = &idCounter;
	// “¯‚¶idCounter‚ğAtransitionGraph‘¤‚É‚à”z•z
	transitionGraph.idCounter = &idCounter;

	// ”zü‚ªI‚í‚Á‚Ä‚©‚çAEntry‚Ì•ÛØ‚ğs‚¤
	parentGraph.EnsureEntryExists();
}


// ƒOƒ‰ƒt“à—e‚ğJson‚É•ÏŠ·‚·‚é
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimatorGraphData::ToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json j;
	j["states"] = StatesToJson();
	j["stateLinks"] = StateLinksToJson();
	j["transitionLinks"] = TransitionLinksToJson();
	return j;
}
// Json‚©‚ç’†g‚ğ•ÏŠ·‚·‚é
void AnimatorGraphData::FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	parentGraph.states.clear();
	parentGraph.stateLinks.clear();
	transitionGraph.links.clear();

	if (j.contains("states")) StatesFromJson(j["states"]);
	if (j.contains("stateLinks")) StateLinksFromJson(j["stateLinks"]);
	if (j.contains("transitionLinks")) TransitionLinksFromJson(j["transitionLinks"]);

	// “Ç‚İ‚ñ‚¾Œ‹‰ÊEntry‚ª–³‚©‚Á‚½ê‡‚Ì•ÛŒ¯
	parentGraph.EnsureEntryExists();
	//SyncTransitionLinks();

}

// ParentState‚ğíœ‚·‚é
void AnimatorGraphData::DeleteParentState(int stateId)
{
	// eŠK‘w‚ÌState‚ğíœ‚·‚é
	parentGraph.DeleteParentState(stateId);

	// ‘JˆÚ—pƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÌLink‚àíœ‚·‚é
	transitionGraph.links.erase(
		std::remove_if(transitionGraph.links.begin(), transitionGraph.links.end(),
			[stateId](const TransitionLink& l)
			{
				return l.fromId == stateId || l.toId == stateId;
			}),
		transitionGraph.links.end()
	);
}

void AnimatorGraphData::SyncTransitionLinks()
{
	for (const auto& link : parentGraph.stateLinks)
	{
		bool exists = false;
		for (const auto& tLink : transitionGraph.links)
		{
			if (tLink.fromId == link.fromId && tLink.toId == link.toId)
			{
				exists = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		if (!exists)
		{
			transitionGraph.CreateLink(link.fromId, link.toId);
		}
	}
}

// States‚ğ•Û‘¶“à—e‚Ö•ÏŠ·
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimatorGraphData::StatesToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json jStates = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& state : parentGraph.states)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json js;
		js["id"] = state.id;
		js["name"] = state.name;
		js["posX"] = state.posX;
		js["posY"] = state.posY;
		js["type"] = ToString(state.type);
		js["color"] = { state.color[0], state.color[1], state.color[2] };

		js["children"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& node : state.childGraph.children)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jn;
			jn["id"] = node.id;
			jn["name"] = node.name;
			jn["posX"] = node.posX;
			jn["posY"] = node.posY;
			jn["type"] = ToString(node.type);
			jn["clipName"] = node.clipName;
			jn["loop"] = node.loop;
			jn["useRootMotion"] = node.useRootMotion;
			js["children"].push_back(jn);
		}

		js["childLinks"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& link : state.childGraph.childLinks)
		{
			js["childLinks"].push_back(AnimLinkToJson(link));
		}

		jStates.push_back(js);
	}

	return jStates;
}
// States‚ğ•Û‘¶“à—e‚©‚ç•œŒ³‚·‚é
void AnimatorGraphData::StatesFromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	for (const auto& js : j)
	{
		ParentState state;
		state.id = js.value("id", -1);
		state.name = js.value("name", "");
		state.posX = js.value("posX", 0.0f);
		state.posY = js.value("posY", 0.0f);
		state.type = ToParentStateType(js.value("type", "State"));

		if (js.contains("color") && js["color"].is_array() && js["color"].size() == 3)
		{
			state.color[0] = js["color"][0].get<float>();
			state.color[1] = js["color"][1].get<float>();
			state.color[2] = js["color"][2].get<float>();
		}

		// qŠK‘wƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÉˆÄ“à”Â‚ğ”zü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çAJSON‚Ì“à—e‚ğ“Ç‚İ‚Ş
		state.childGraph.idCounter = &idCounter;
		// state.childGraph ‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒXƒgƒ‰ƒNƒ^‚ª©“®¶¬‚µ‚½Start/End‚ğA
		// JSON“Ç‚İ‚İ‘O‚Éˆê’Uæ‚èœ‚¢‚Ä‚¨‚­(“ñd¶¬–h~)
		state.childGraph.children.clear();
		state.childGraph.childLinks.clear();

		if (js.contains("children"))
		{
			for (const auto& jn : js["children"])
			{
				ChildState node;
				node.id = jn.value("id", -1);
				node.name = jn.value("name", "");
				node.posX = jn.value("posX", 0.0f);
				node.posY = jn.value("posY", 0.0f);
				node.type = ToChildStateType(jn.value("type", "Normal"));
				node.clipName = jn.value("clipName", "");
				node.loop = jn.value("loop", true);
				node.useRootMotion = jn.value("useRootMotion", false);
				state.childGraph.children.push_back(node);

				SyncIdCounter(node.id);
			}
		}

		if (js.contains("childLinks"))
		{
			for (const auto& jl : js["childLinks"])
			{
				AnimLink link;
				AnimLinkFromJson(link, jl);
				state.childGraph.childLinks.push_back(link);
			}
		}

		// ŒÃ‚¢•Û‘¶ƒf[ƒ^‚ÉAqŠK‘w‚ÌStart/End‚ª–³‚©‚Á‚½ê‡‚Ì•ÛŒ¯
		state.childGraph.EnsureStartAndEndExist();

		SyncIdCounter(state.id);

		parentGraph.states.push_back(state);
	}
}

// StateLinks‚ğ•Û‘¶“à—e‚Ö•ÏŠ·
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimatorGraphData::StateLinksToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json jLinks = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& link : parentGraph.stateLinks)
	{
		jLinks.push_back(AnimLinkToJson(link));
	}

	return jLinks;
}
// StateLinks‚ğ•Û‘¶“à—e‚©‚ç•œŒ³‚·‚é
void AnimatorGraphData::StateLinksFromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	for (const auto& jl : j)
	{
		AnimLink link;
		AnimLinkFromJson(link, jl);
		parentGraph.stateLinks.push_back(link);
	}
}

// TransitionLinks‚Ì•Û‘¶“à—e‚Ö•ÏŠ·
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimatorGraphData::TransitionLinksToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json jLinks = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& link : transitionGraph.links)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jl;
		jl["id"] = link.id;
		jl["fromId"] = link.fromId;
		jl["toId"] = link.toId;

		nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
		jc["returnNode"]["id"] = link.condition.returnNode.id;
		jc["returnNode"]["posX"] = link.condition.returnNode.posX;
		jc["returnNode"]["posY"] = link.condition.returnNode.posY;

		jc["variableNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& v : link.condition.variableNodes)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jv;
			jv["id"] = v.id;
			jv["posX"] = v.posX;
			jv["posY"] = v.posY;
			jv["name"] = v.name;
			jv["type"] = ToString(v.type);
			jv["intValue"] = v.intValue;
			jv["floatValue"] = v.floatValue;
			jv["boolValue"] = v.boolValue;
			jc["variableNodes"].push_back(jv);
		}

		jc["operatorNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& o : link.condition.operatorNodes)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jo;
			jo["id"] = o.id;
			jo["posX"] = o.posX;
			jo["posY"] = o.posY;
			jo["op"] = ToString(o.op);
			jc["operatorNodes"].push_back(jo);
		}

		jc["logicNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& l : link.condition.logicNodes)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jln;
			jln["id"] = l.id;
			jln["posX"] = l.posX;
			jln["posY"] = l.posY;
			jln["op"] = ToString(l.op);
			jc["logicNodes"].push_back(jln);
		}

		jc["getterNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& g : link.condition.getterNodes)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jg;
			jg["id"] = g.id;
			jg["posX"] = g.posX;
			jg["posY"] = g.posY;
			jg["componentName"] = g.componentName;
			jg["fieldName"] = g.fieldName;
			jg["type"] = ToString(g.type);
			jg["selectedComponent"] = ToString(g.selectedComponent);
			jc["getterNodes"].push_back(jg);
		}

		jc["links"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& cl : link.condition.links)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jcl;
			jcl["id"] = cl.id;
			jcl["fromId"] = cl.fromId;
			jcl["toId"] = cl.toId;
			jcl["toPinB"] = cl.toPinB;
			jc["links"].push_back(jcl);
		}

		jl["condition"] = jc;
		jLinks.push_back(jl);
	}

	return jLinks;
}
// TransitionLinks‚Ì•Û‘¶“à—e‚©‚ç•œŒ³
void AnimatorGraphData::TransitionLinksFromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	for (const auto& jl : j)
	{
		TransitionLink link;
		link.id = jl.value("id", -1);
		link.fromId = jl.value("fromId", -1);
		link.toId = jl.value("toId", -1);

		link.condition.idCounter = &idCounter;

		if (jl.contains("condition"))
		{
			const auto& jc = jl["condition"];

			if (jc.contains("returnNode"))
			{
				link.condition.returnNode.id = jc["returnNode"].value("id", -1);
				link.condition.returnNode.posX = jc["returnNode"].value("posX", 500.0f);
				link.condition.returnNode.posY = jc["returnNode"].value("posY", 200.0f);
			}

			if (jc.contains("variableNodes"))
			{
				for (const auto& jv : jc["variableNodes"])
				{
					ConditionVariableNode v;
					v.id = jv.value("id", -1);
					v.posX = jv.value("posX", 0.0f);
					v.posY = jv.value("posY", 0.0f);
					v.name = jv.value("name", "");
					v.type = ToValueType(jv.value("type", "Float"));
					v.intValue = jv.value("intValue", 0);
					v.floatValue = jv.value("floatValue", 0.0f);
					v.boolValue = jv.value("boolValue", false);
					link.condition.variableNodes.push_back(v);

					SyncIdCounter(v.id);
				}
			}

			if (jc.contains("operatorNodes"))
			{
				for (const auto& jo : jc["operatorNodes"])
				{
					ConditionOperatorNode o;
					o.id = jo.value("id", -1);
					o.posX = jo.value("posX", 0.0f);
					o.posY = jo.value("posY", 0.0f);
					o.op = ToCompareOp(jo.value("op", "Greater"));
					link.condition.operatorNodes.push_back(o);

					SyncIdCounter(o.id);
				}
			}

			if (jc.contains("logicNodes"))
			{
				for (const auto& jln : jc["logicNodes"])
				{
					ConditionLogicNode l;
					l.id = jln.value("id", -1);
					l.posX = jln.value("posX", 0.0f);
					l.posY = jln.value("posY", 0.0f);
					l.op = ToLogicOp(jln.value("op", "And"));
					link.condition.logicNodes.push_back(l);

					SyncIdCounter(l.id);
				}
			}

			if (jc.contains("getterNodes"))
			{
				for (const auto& jg : jc["getterNodes"])
				{
					ConditionGetterNode g;
					g.id = jg.value("id", -1);
					g.posX = jg.value("posX", 0.0f);
					g.posY = jg.value("posY", 0.0f);
					g.componentName = jg.value("componentName", "");
					g.fieldName = jg.value("fieldName", "");
					g.type = ToValueType(jg.value("type", "Float"));
					g.selectedComponent = ToFloatComponent(jg.value("selectedComponent", "None"));
					link.condition.getterNodes.push_back(g);

					SyncIdCounter(g.id);
				}
			}

			if (jc.contains("links"))
			{
				for (const auto& jcl : jc["links"])
				{
					ConditionLink cl;
					cl.id = jcl.value("id", -1);
					cl.fromId = jcl.value("fromId", -1);
					cl.toId = jcl.value("toId", -1);
					cl.toPinB = jcl.value("toPinB", false);
					link.condition.links.push_back(cl);

					SyncIdCounter(cl.id);
				}
			}
		}

		if (link.condition.returnNode.id < 0)
		{
			link.condition.returnNode.id = link.condition.GenerateId();
			link.condition.returnNode.posX = 500.0f;
			link.condition.returnNode.posY = 200.0f;
		}
		SyncIdCounter(link.condition.returnNode.id);

		SyncIdCounter(link.id);

		transitionGraph.links.push_back(link);
	}
}


// AnimLink1–{•ª‚ğJson‚É•ÏŠ·‚·‚é
nlohmann::ordered_json AnimatorGraphData::AnimLinkToJson(const AnimLink& link) const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json jl;
	jl["id"] = link.id;
	jl["fromId"] = link.fromId;
	jl["toId"] = link.toId;
	jl["blend"] = link.blend;
	jl["blendDuration"] = link.blendDuration;

	nlohmann::ordered_json jc;

	jc["returnNode"]["id"] = link.condition.returnNode.id;
	jc["returnNode"]["posX"] = link.condition.returnNode.posX;
	jc["returnNode"]["posY"] = link.condition.returnNode.posY;

	jc["variableNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& v : link.condition.variableNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jv;
		jv["id"] = v.id;
		jv["posX"] = v.posX;
		jv["posY"] = v.posY;
		jv["name"] = v.name;
		jv["type"] = ToString(v.type);
		jv["intValue"] = v.intValue;
		jv["floatValue"] = v.floatValue;
		jv["boolValue"] = v.boolValue;
		jc["variableNodes"].push_back(jv);
	}

	jc["operatorNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& o : link.condition.operatorNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jo;
		jo["id"] = o.id;
		jo["posX"] = o.posX;
		jo["posY"] = o.posY;
		jo["op"] = ToString(o.op);
		jc["operatorNodes"].push_back(jo);
	}

	jc["logicNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& l : link.condition.logicNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jln;
		jln["id"] = l.id;
		jln["posX"] = l.posX;
		jln["posY"] = l.posY;
		jln["op"] = ToString(l.op);
		jc["logicNodes"].push_back(jln);
	}

	jc["getterNodes"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& g : link.condition.getterNodes)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jg;
		jg["id"] = g.id;
		jg["posX"] = g.posX;
		jg["posY"] = g.posY;
		jg["componentName"] = g.componentName;
		jg["fieldName"] = g.fieldName;
		jg["type"] = ToString(g.type);
		jg["selectedComponent"] = ToString(g.selectedComponent);
		jc["getterNodes"].push_back(jg);
	}

	jc["links"] = nlohmann::json::array();
	for (const auto& cl : link.condition.links)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jcl;
		jcl["id"] = cl.id;
		jcl["fromId"] = cl.fromId;
		jcl["toId"] = cl.toId;
		jcl["toPinB"] = cl.toPinB;
		jc["links"].push_back(jcl);
	}

	jl["condition"] = jc;

	return jl;
}
// AnimLink1–{•ª‚ğJson‚©‚ç•œŒ³‚·‚é
void AnimatorGraphData::AnimLinkFromJson(AnimLink& link, const nlohmann::json& jl)
{
	link.id = jl.value("id", -1);
	link.fromId = jl.value("fromId", -1);
	link.toId = jl.value("toId", -1);
	link.blend = jl.value("blend", true);
	link.blendDuration = jl.value("blendDuration", 0.2f);

	// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÉˆÄ“à”Â‚ğ”zü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çg‚¤
	link.condition.idCounter = &idCounter;

	if (jl.contains("condition"))
	{
		const auto& jc = jl["condition"];

		if (jc.contains("returnNode"))
		{
			link.condition.returnNode.id = jc["returnNode"].value("id", -1);
			link.condition.returnNode.posX = jc["returnNode"].value("posX", 500.0f);
			link.condition.returnNode.posY = jc["returnNode"].value("posY", 200.0f);
		}

		if (jc.contains("variableNodes"))
		{
			for (const auto& jv : jc["variableNodes"])
			{
				ConditionVariableNode v;
				v.id = jv.value("id", -1);
				v.posX = jv.value("posX", 0.0f);
				v.posY = jv.value("posY", 0.0f);
				v.name = jv.value("name", "");
				v.type = ToValueType(jv.value("type", "Float"));
				v.intValue = jv.value("intValue", 0);
				v.floatValue = jv.value("floatValue", 0.0f);
				v.boolValue = jv.value("boolValue", false);
				link.condition.variableNodes.push_back(v);

				SyncIdCounter(v.id);
			}
		}

		if (jc.contains("operatorNodes"))
		{
			for (const auto& jo : jc["operatorNodes"])
			{
				ConditionOperatorNode o;
				o.id = jo.value("id", -1);
				o.posX = jo.value("posX", 0.0f);
				o.posY = jo.value("posY", 0.0f);
				o.op = ToCompareOp(jo.value("op", "Greater"));
				link.condition.operatorNodes.push_back(o);

				SyncIdCounter(o.id);
			}
		}

		if (jc.contains("logicNodes"))
		{
			for (const auto& jln : jc["logicNodes"])
			{
				ConditionLogicNode l;
				l.id = jln.value("id", -1);
				l.posX = jln.value("posX", 0.0f);
				l.posY = jln.value("posY", 0.0f);
				l.op = ToLogicOp(jln.value("op", "And"));
				link.condition.logicNodes.push_back(l);

				SyncIdCounter(l.id);
			}
		}

		if (jc.contains("getterNodes"))
		{
			for (const auto& jg : jc["getterNodes"])
			{
				ConditionGetterNode g;
				g.id = jg.value("id", -1);
				g.posX = jg.value("posX", 0.0f);
				g.posY = jg.value("posY", 0.0f);
				g.componentName = jg.value("componentName", "");
				g.fieldName = jg.value("fieldName", "");
				g.type = ToValueType(jg.value("type", "Float"));
				g.selectedComponent = ToFloatComponent(jg.value("selectedComponent", "None"));
				link.condition.getterNodes.push_back(g);

				SyncIdCounter(g.id);
			}
		}

		if (jc.contains("links"))
		{
			for (const auto& jcl : jc["links"])
			{
				ConditionLink cl;
				cl.id = jcl.value("id", -1);
				cl.fromId = jcl.value("fromId", -1);
				cl.toId = jcl.value("toId", -1);
				cl.toPinB = jcl.value("toPinB", false);
				link.condition.links.push_back(cl);

				SyncIdCounter(cl.id);
			}
		}
	}

	// ŒÃ‚¢•Û‘¶ƒf[ƒ^A‚ ‚é‚¢‚Í"condition"ƒL[©‘Ì‚ª–³‚©‚Á‚½ê‡‚Ì•ÛŒ¯
	if (link.condition.returnNode.id < 0)
	{
		link.condition.returnNode.id = link.condition.GenerateId();
		link.condition.returnNode.posX = 500.0f;
		link.condition.returnNode.posY = 200.0f;
	}
	SyncIdCounter(link.condition.returnNode.id);

	SyncIdCounter(link.id);
}

// “Ç‚İ‚ñ‚¾ID‚ÉƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ’Ç]‚³‚¹‚é
void AnimatorGraphData::SyncIdCounter(int id)
{
	if (id >= idCounter) idCounter = id + 1;
}


// ‘JˆÚ—pLinkì¬
int TransitionGraphData::CreateLink(int fromId, int toId)
{
	TransitionLink link;
	link.id = GenerateId();
	link.fromId = fromId;
	link.toId = toId;

	// ğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚É‚àˆÄ“à”Â‚ğ”zü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çg‚¤
	link.condition.idCounter = this->idCounter;
	// ‚±‚ÌLinkê—p‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ÉAŒÅ’è‚ÌReturnƒm[ƒh‚ğ1‚Â”z’u‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
	link.condition.returnNode.id = link.condition.GenerateId();
	link.condition.returnNode.posX = 500.0f;
	link.condition.returnNode.posY = 200.0f;

	links.push_back(link);
	return link.id;
}
// ‘JˆÚ—pLinkíœ
void TransitionGraphData::DeleteLink(int linkId)
{
	links.erase(
		std::remove_if(links.begin(), links.end(),
			[linkId](const TransitionLink& l) { return l.id == linkId; }),
		links.end()
	);
} 
 
===== SequencerData.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\Data\SequencerData.cpp 
#include "SequencerData.h"

namespace
{
	std::string ToString(SequencerFlag flag)
	{
		switch (flag)
		{
		case SequencerFlag::AnimExit:   return "AnimExit";
		case SequencerFlag::SuperArmor: return "SuperArmor";
		case SequencerFlag::Attack:     return "Attack";
		case SequencerFlag::Trimming:   return "Trimming";
		}
		return "AnimExit";
	}
	SequencerFlag ToSequencerFlag(const std::string& s)
	{
		if (s == "SuperArmor") return SequencerFlag::SuperArmor;
		if (s == "Attack")     return SequencerFlag::Attack;
		if (s == "Trimming")   return SequencerFlag::Trimming;
		return SequencerFlag::AnimExit;
	}
}


// w’è‚µ‚½ƒNƒŠƒbƒv–¼‚Ìƒf[ƒ^‚ğ’T‚·
SequencerClipData* SequencerData::FindOrCreateClip(const std::string& clipName)
{
	for (auto& clip : clips)
	{
		if (clip.clipName == clipName) return &clip;
	}

	SequencerClipData newClip;
	newClip.clipName = clipName;
	clips.push_back(newClip);
	return &clips.back();
}


// •Û‘¶
nlohmann::ordered_json SequencerData::ToJson() const
{
	nlohmann::ordered_json j;
	j["clips"] = nlohmann::json::array();

	for (const auto& clip : clips)
	{
		nlohmann::ordered_json jc;
		jc["clipName"] = clip.clipName;
		jc["nextId"] = clip.nextId;

		jc["ranges"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& r : clip.ranges)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jr;
			jr["id"] = r.id;
			jr["start"] = r.start;
			jr["end"] = r.end;
			jr["flag"] = ToString(r.flag);
			jc["ranges"].push_back(jr);
		}

		jc["points"] = nlohmann::json::array();
		for (const auto& p : clip.points)
		{
			nlohmann::ordered_json jp;
			jp["id"] = p.id;
			jp["time"] = p.time;
			jp["flag"] = ToString(p.flag);
			jc["points"].push_back(jp);
		}

		j["clips"].push_back(jc);
	}

	return j;
}

// “Ç‚İ‚İ
void SequencerData::FromJson(const nlohmann::json& j)
{
	clips.clear();

	if (!j.contains("clips")) return;

	for (const auto& jc : j["clips"])
	{
		SequencerClipData clip;
		clip.clipName = jc.value("clipName", "");
		clip.nextId = jc.value("nextId", 1);

		if (jc.contains("ranges"))
		{
			for (const auto& jr : jc["ranges"])
			{
				SequencerRange r;
				r.id = jr.value("id", -1);
				r.start = jr.value("start", 0.0f);
				r.end = jr.value("end", 1.0f);
				r.flag = ToSequencerFlag(jr.value("flag", "AnimExit"));
				clip.ranges.push_back(r);
			}
		}

		if (jc.contains("points"))
		{
			for (const auto& jp : jc["points"])
			{
				SequencerPoint p;
				p.id = jp.value("id", -1);
				p.time = jp.value("time", 0.0f);
				p.flag = ToSequencerFlag(jp.value("flag", "AnimExit"));
				clip.points.push_back(p);
			}
		}

		clips.push_back(clip);
	}
}

enum class ShadingModel : uint8_t
{
    Default = 0,
    Basic,      
};

struct MaterialComponent
{
    ShadingModel shadingModel = ShadingModel::Default;

    // ƒVƒF[ƒ_[–¼‚É•ÏŠ·‚·‚éƒwƒ‹ƒp[

    {

        {
        case ShadingModel::Basic:   return "gbuffer_basic";
        default:                    return "gbuffer_default";
        }
    }
};

===== TransformComponent.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\ECS\Components\TransformComponent.h 
#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>

struct TransformComponent
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 position{ 0.0f,0.0f,0.0f }; // ˆÊ’u
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 rotation{ 0.0f,0.0f,0.0f }; // ‰ñ“](ƒIƒCƒ‰[Šp)
	DirectX::XMFLOAT3 scale   { 1.0f,1.0f,1.0f }; // ƒXƒP[ƒ‹


	// ƒ[ƒ‹ƒhs—ñæ“¾
	DirectX::XMMATRIX GetWorldMatrix() const
	{
		DirectX::XMMATRIX S = DirectX::XMMatrixScaling(scale.x, scale.y, scale.z);
		DirectX::XMMATRIX R = DirectX::XMMatrixRotationRollPitchYaw(rotation.x, rotation.y, rotation.z);
		DirectX::XMMATRIX T = DirectX::XMMatrixTranslation(position.x, position.y, position.z);
		return S * R * T;
	}
};

Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\ECS\Systems\AnimationSystem.h 
#pragma once
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include "entt.hpp"
#include "Engine/Resource/Model.h" 
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"

class AnimationSystem
{
public:
	// XViŠÔ‚ği‚ß‚Äƒ{[ƒ“s—ñ‚ğŒvZj
	void Update(
		entt::registry& registry,
		float elapsedTime);

	// ŠÔ‚ği‚ß‚¸‚ÉAw’è‚µ‚½‚Ìƒ|[ƒY‚¾‚¯ŒvZ‚·‚é
	void AnimationSystem::EvaluatePose(entt::registry& registry);

private:
	// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•âŠÔiˆÊ’uj
	static DirectX::XMFLOAT3 InterpolatePosition(
		const std::vector<Model::VectorKeyframe>& keyframes,
		float time);

	// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•âŠÔi‰ñ“]j
	static DirectX::XMFLOAT4 InterpolateRotation(
		const std::vector<Model::QuaternionKeyframe>& keyframes,
		float time);

	// ƒL[ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€•âŠÔiƒXƒP[ƒ‹j
	static DirectX::XMFLOAT3 InterpolateScale(
		const std::vector<Model::VectorKeyframe>& keyframes,
		float time);

	// ƒm[ƒh‚ÌƒOƒ[ƒoƒ‹s—ñ‚ğÄ‹AŒvZ‚µ‚Äƒ{[ƒ“s—ñ‚É‘‚«‚Ş
	static void CalcBoneTransforms(
		Model& model,
		const Model::Animation& animation,
		float time,
		std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outPositions,
		std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& outRotations,
		std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outScales,
		int rootMotionNodeIndex = -1);

	// 2‚Â‚Ìƒ|[ƒY‚ğrate‚Å•âŠÔ‚·‚é
	static void BlendPoses(
		const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& posA, const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& rotA, const std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& scaleA,

		float rate,
		std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outPositions,
		std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>& outRotations,
		std::vector<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>& outScales);

	// –¼‘O‚©‚çƒ‹[ƒgƒm[ƒhƒCƒ“ƒfƒbƒNƒX‚ğ’T‚·
	static int FindNodeIndex(const Model& model, const std::string& name);

	// w’è‚Ìƒ[ƒJƒ‹ˆÊ’u‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
	static DirectX::XMFLOAT3 CalcNodeLocalPosition(
		const Model::Animation& animation,
		int nodeIndex,
		const Model::Node& node,
		float time);

	// ƒ‹[ƒgƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ÌˆÚ“®—Ê‚ğŒvZ‚·‚é
	static DirectX::XMFLOAT3 CalcRootMotionDelta(
		const Model& model,
		const Model::Animation& animation,

struct CbScene
{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 view;             // ƒrƒ…[s—ñ
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 projection;       // ƒvƒƒWƒFƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“s—ñ
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4   cameraPosition;   // ƒJƒƒ‰ˆÊ’uiw–¢g—pj

};

#define MAX_BONES 256

{
	DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 boneTransforms[MAX_BONES];

class RenderSystem
{
public:
	// ‰Šú‰»
	void Initialize(ID3D11Device* device);

	// •`‰æ
	void Render(
		entt::registry& registry,
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtv,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv,
		ID3D11Device* device,
		UINT width,
		UINT height
	);

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€‚ğScene‘¤‚©‚çƒZƒbƒg
	void SetFrustum(
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection)
	{
		cachedFrustum = CalcFrustum(view, projection);
	}

	// IBLƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒƒZƒbƒg
	void SetIBLTextures(
		ID3D11ShaderResourceView* diffuseIem,
		ID3D11ShaderResourceView* specularPmrem,
		ID3D11ShaderResourceView* lutGgx)
	{
		iblDiffuseIem = diffuseIem;
		iblSpecularPmrem = specularPmrem;
		iblLutGgx = lutGgx;
	}

	// ’è”ƒoƒbƒtƒ@ƒQƒbƒ^[
	ID3D11Buffer* GetCbScene() { return cbScene.Get(); }
	ID3D11Buffer** GetCbSceneAddress() { return cbScene.GetAddressOf(); }

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€•½–Ê
	struct FrustumPlane
	{
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 normal;
		float distance;
	};

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€i6•½–Êj
	struct Frustum
	{
		FrustumPlane planes[6]; // Left, Right, Top, Bottom, Near, Far
	};

		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection
	);

		const Frustum& frustum,
		const Model::BoundingBox& bbox,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& world
	);

		entt::registry& registry,
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv,
		const Frustum& frustum
	);

		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtv,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv
	);

	// ”¼“§–¾ƒpƒX(ƒtƒHƒ[ƒhƒŒƒ“ƒ_ƒŠƒ“ƒO)
	void ForwardTransparentPass(
		entt::registry& registry,
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtv,
		ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv,
		const Frustum& frustum
	);


	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11VertexShader> staticVertexShader;   // Ã“IƒƒbƒVƒ…—p
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11VertexShader> skinnedVertexShader;  // ƒXƒLƒjƒ“ƒOƒƒbƒVƒ…—p
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11VertexShader> lightingVS;           // LightingƒpƒX—p’¸“_ƒVƒF[ƒ_[
	Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11PixelShader>  lightingPS;           // LightingƒpƒX—pƒsƒNƒZƒ‹ƒVƒF[ƒ_[

private:
	JsonFileCache<AnimatorGraphData> graphCache;

	// Œ»İState‚©‚ç‘JˆÚ‰Â”\‚ÈLink‚ğ’T‚·
	int FindTransition(const std::vector<TransitionLink>& links, int fromId, entt::registry& registry, entt::entity entity);
};

#pragma once
#include <DirectXMath.h>

// 2BoneIKƒ\ƒ‹ƒo[
// root-mid-end ‚Ì3ŠÖß‚ğAtarget(‘«ñ‚ğ’u‚«‚½‚¢ˆÊ’u)‚É“Í‚©‚¹‚é
namespace TwoBoneIKSolver
{
    struct Result
    {
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 midPosition;      // ‰ğ‚¢‚½Œã‚Ìmid(•G)‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒhˆÊ’u
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4 rootRotation;     // ‰ğ‚¢‚½Œã‚Ìroot(˜)‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‰ñ“]
        DirectX::XMFLOAT4 midRotation;      // ‰ğ‚¢‚½Œã‚Ìmid(•G)‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‰ñ“]
        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 endPosition;      // ÀÛ‚É’u‚©‚ê‚½end(‘«ñ)‚ÌˆÊ’u(ƒNƒ‰ƒ“ƒvŒã‚Ìtarget)
    };

    // root, mid, end : Œ»İ(IK“K—p‘O)‚Ì3ŠÖß‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒhˆÊ’u
    // rootRotation, midRotation : Œ»İ‚Ìroot, mid‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒh‰ñ“](ƒNƒH[ƒ^ƒjƒIƒ“)
    // target : end(‘«ñ)‚ğ“Í‚©‚¹‚½‚¢ƒ[ƒ‹ƒhÀ•W
    // poleVector : mid(•G)‚ğŒü‚¯‚½‚¢•ûŒü‚Ìƒ[ƒ‹ƒhÀ•W(ˆÊ’u‚Å‚Í‚È‚­•ûŒü‚ğ¦‚·1“_)
    Result Solve(
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& root,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& mid,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& end,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& rootRotation,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT4& midRotation,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& target,
        const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& poleVector);

    // fromƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğtoƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÉŒü‚¯‚ÄAÅ’Z‰ñ“]‚ÌƒNƒH[ƒ^ƒjƒIƒ“‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
    DirectX::XMVECTOR RotationFromToRotation(DirectX::FXMVECTOR from, DirectX::FXMVECTOR to);
} 
 
===== AnimationSpeedSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\ECS\Systems\AnimationSpeedSystem.cpp 
#include "AnimationSpeedSystem.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SkinnedMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimationSpeedComponent.h"


void AnimationSpeedSystem::Update(entt::registry& registry)
{
	auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent, AnimatorComponent, AnimationSpeedComponent>();

	for (auto entity : view)
	{
		auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(entity);
		auto& animator = view.get<AnimatorComponent>(entity);
		auto& animSpeed = view.get<AnimationSpeedComponent>(entity);

		// ƒpƒX‚ª–¢İ’è‚È‚çA‚±‚ÌƒGƒ“ƒeƒBƒeƒB‚Í‘¬“xƒJ[ƒu‚ğg‚í‚È‚¢
		if (animSpeed.animationSpeedPath.empty())
		{
			animator.speedCurveMultiplier = 1.0f;
			continue;
		}

		if (!skinned.model)
		{
			continue;
		}

		AnimationSpeedData& data = dataCache.GetOrLoad(animSpeed.animationSpeedPath,
			[](AnimationSpeedData& data, const nlohmann::json& j) { data.FromJson(j); });

		const AnimationSpeedClipData* clip = nullptr;
		for (const auto& c : data.clips)
		{
			if (c.clipName == animator.currentAnimation) { clip = &c; break; }
		}

		// ƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì’·‚³‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
		float clipLength = 0.0f;
		for (const auto& anim : skinned.model->GetAnimations())
		{
			if (anim.name == animator.currentAnimation)
			{
				clipLength = anim.secondsLength;
				break;
			}
		}

		if (clipLength <= 0.0001f)
		{
			animator.speedCurveMultiplier = 1.0f;
			continue;
		}

		float normalizedTime = animator.animationTime / clipLength;

		animator.speedCurveMultiplier = clip ? clip->EvaluateSpeed(normalizedTime) : 1.0f;
	}
} 
 
===== AnimationSystem.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Engine\ECS\Systems\AnimationSystem.cpp 
#include "AnimationSystem.h"
#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"
#include "Engine/Resource/SkeletonUtils.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"

// XV
void AnimationSystem::Update(
	entt::registry& registry,
	float elapsedTime)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	auto view = registry.view<AnimatorComponent, SkinnedMeshComponent>();
	view.each(
		[&](auto entity,
			AnimatorComponent& animator,
			SkinnedMeshComponent& skinned)
		{
			ZoneScopedN("AnimationSystem::Entity");

			// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ª–³Œø‚È‚ç‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢
			if (!skinned.model) return;
			if (!animator.isPlaying) return;

			const auto& animations = skinned.model->GetAnimations();
			if (animations.empty()) return;

			// VƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ÌŠÔ‚ği‚ß‚é
			int animIndex = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < (int)animations.size(); i++)
			{
				if (animations[i].name == animator.currentAnimation) { animIndex = i; break; }
			}
			if (animIndex < 0)
			{
				animIndex = 0;
				animator.currentAnimation = animations[0].name;
			}

			const auto& animation = animations[animIndex];
			
			// ƒgƒŠƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ğ•b”‚É•ÏŠ·
			if (!animator.trimResolved)
			{
				if (animator.trimEnd >= 0.0f)
				{
					animator.trimStart *= animation.secondsLength;
					animator.trimEnd *= animation.secondsLength;
					animator.animationTime = animator.trimStart;
				}
				animator.trimResolved = true;
			}

			const float effectiveEnd = (animator.trimEnd >= 0.0f) ? animator.trimEnd : animation.secondsLength;
			const float effectiveStart = (animator.trimEnd >= 0.0f) ? animator.trimStart : 0.0f;
			
			const float oldTime = animator.animationTime;

			animator.animationTime += elapsedTime * animator.playSpeed * animator.speedCurveMultiplier;
			// ƒ‹[ƒvˆ—
			if (animator.loop)
			{
				if (animator.animationTime > effectiveEnd)
				{
					animator.animationTime = effectiveStart + (animator.animationTime - effectiveEnd);
					animator.isEnd = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (animator.animationTime >= effectiveEnd)
				{
