{
	// ç‰©ç†ã‚·ãƒ¼ãƒ³çµ‚äº†å‡¦ç†
	FinalizePhysicsScene();
}

void SceneGame::Update(float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	// F2ã§ç‰©ç†ãƒ‡ãƒãƒƒã‚°æç”»ã®è¡¨ç¤ºåˆ‡ã‚Šæ›¿ãˆ
	static bool prevF2 = false;
	bool currF2 = (GetAsyncKeyState(VK_F2) & 0x8000) != 0;
	if (currF2 && !prevF2)
	{
		DrawDebug = !DrawDebug;
	}
	prevF2 = currF2;

	// ãƒ‡ãƒ¢æ›´æ–°
	waveDemo.Update(elapsedTime);

	// PhysXæ›´æ–°
	{
		ZoneScopedN("UpdatePhysX");
		UpdatePhysicsScene(elapsedTime);
	}

	UpdateGameplay(elapsedTime, context);
	UpdateAnimation(elapsedTime, context);
	UpdateResources(elapsedTime, context);
	UpdateCamera(elapsedTime, context);
}

void SceneGame::Render(const SceneContext& context)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	{
		ZoneScopedN("CbScene Update");

		// CbSceneæ›´æ–°
		CbScene cbSceneData{};
		cbSceneData.view = context.view;
		cbSceneData.projection = context.projection;

		// ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©ä½ç½®
		if (context.camera)
		{
			const auto& eye = context.camera->GetEye();
			cbSceneData.cameraPosition = { eye.x, eye.y, eye.z, 0.0f };
		}
		lightManager.Bind(context.deviceContext);

		context.deviceContext->UpdateSubresource(
			renderSystem.GetCbScene(), 0, nullptr, &cbSceneData, 0, 0
		);

		// é ‚ç‚¹ã‚·ã‚§ãƒ¼ãƒ€ãƒ¼ã«è¨­å®š
		context.deviceContext->VSSetConstantBuffers(
			0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress()
		);

		// ãƒ”ã‚¯ã‚»ãƒ«ã‚·ã‚§ãƒ¼ãƒ€ãƒ¼ã«ã‚‚è¨­å®š
		context.deviceContext->PSSetConstantBuffers(
			0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress()
		);
	}

	// ãƒ•ãƒ©ã‚¹ã‚¿ãƒ è¨ˆç®—
	renderSystem.SetFrustum(context.view, context.projection);


	// ã‚¹ã‚«ã‚¤ãƒœãƒƒã‚¯ã‚¹
	{
		ZoneScopedN("SkyBox::Render");
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 eye = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
		if (context.camera)
		{
			const auto& e = context.camera->GetEye();
			eye = { e.x, e.y, e.z };
		}
		skyBox.Render(context.deviceContext, context.view, context.projection, eye);
	}

	// æç”»
	{
		ZoneScopedN("RenderSystem::Render");
		renderSystem.Render(
			registry,
			context.deviceContext,
			context.renderTargetView,
			context.depthStencilView,
			context.device,
			context.screenWidth,
			context.screenHeight
		);
	}

#if WaveDemo && !defined(_DEBUG)
	// ãƒ‡ãƒ¢æç”»
	{
		ZoneScopedN("WaveDemo Loop+Render");

		const auto& lightDir4 = lightManager.GetDirectionalLight().direction;
		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 lightDir3 = { lightDir4.x, lightDir4.y, lightDir4.z };

		waveDemo.Render(
			registry,
			context.deviceContext,
			context.view,
			context.projection,
			lightDir3
		);
	}
#endif

	if (DrawDebug)
	{
		physicsDebugRenderer.Render(
			context.deviceContext,
			GetPhysicsScene(),
			GetControllerManager(),
			context.view,
			context.projection,
			lightManager.GetDirectionalLight().direction);
	}
}

// ã‚«ãƒ¡ãƒ©
void SceneGame::UpdateCamera(float elapsedTime, const SceneContext& context)
{
	ZoneScopedN("CameraSystem");
	if (context.cameraMode == CameraMode::Debug)
	{
		debugCameraSystem.Update(registry, elapsedTime, context.gameInputActive);
		debugCameraSystem.ApplyToCamera(registry, *context.camera);

		// ã‚¹ãƒ†ã‚£ãƒƒã‚¯å…¥åŠ›ãŒã‚ã‚Œã°Gameãƒ¢ãƒ¼ãƒ‰ã«æˆ»ã™
		if (context.editorContext)
		{
			auto inputView = registry.view<GameInputComponent>();
			for (auto entity : inputView)
			{
				auto& input = inputView.get<GameInputComponent>(entity);
				const float threshold = 0.2f;
				if (fabsf(input.moveStick.x) > threshold ||
					fabsf(input.moveStick.y) > threshold ||
					fabsf(input.cameraStick.x) > threshold ||
					fabsf(input.cameraStick.y) > threshold)
				{
					context.editorContext->cameraMode = CameraMode::Game;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else
	{
		gameCameraSystem.Update(registry, elapsedTime);
		gameCameraSystem.ApplyToCamera(registry, *context.camera);
	}
}
 
 
===== AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h 
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"

class AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow
{
public:
    void Draw(ControllerComponent& ctrl);

private:
    // ƒ][ƒ“‚Ìó‘Ô
    struct ZoneState
    {
        bool  exists = false;
        float start = 0.0f;
        float end = 1.0f;
        std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint> localPoints;
        int   selectedIndex = -1;
    };

    struct RangeDragState
    {
        enum class Mode { None, Left, Right, Move };
        Mode mode = Mode::None;
        float startMouseX = 0.0f;
        float startZoneStart = 0.0f;
        float startZoneEnd = 0.0f;
    };

    void DrawTriggerSection(const char* label, AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect& effect, ZoneState& zone, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue);
    void DrawRangeBar(ZoneState& zone, float width, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue);
    void DrawGraph(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex, int& otherSelectedIndex, float localCurrentValue, bool showIndicator);
    void DrawSelectedPointPanel(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex);

    ZoneState leftZone;
    ZoneState rightZone;
    RangeDragState leftRangeDrag;
    RangeDragState rightRangeDrag;
}; 
 
===== AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.cpp 
#include "AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace
{
    constexpr ImU32 AccentColor = IM_COL32(66, 150, 250, 255);   // ImGui‚ç‚µ‚¢Â
    constexpr ImU32 IndicatorColor = IM_COL32(255, 70, 70, 255); // Œ»İ’n‚ÌÔü
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::Draw(ControllerComponent& ctrl)
{
    if (!ImGui::Begin("Adaptive Trigger Tool"))
    {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    if (ImGui::BeginTable("##layout", 2, ImGuiTableFlags_None))
    {
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("main", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthStretch);
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("panel", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthFixed, 160.0f);
        ImGui::TableNextRow();

        ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
        DrawTriggerSection("L2", ctrl.leftTriggerEffect, leftZone, leftRangeDrag, ctrl.leftTrigger);
        ImGui::Spacing();
        DrawTriggerSection("R2", ctrl.rightTriggerEffect, rightZone, rightRangeDrag, ctrl.rightTrigger);

        ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
        if (leftZone.selectedIndex >= 0)
        {
            DrawSelectedPointPanel(leftZone.localPoints, leftZone.selectedIndex);
        }
        else if (rightZone.selectedIndex >= 0)
        {
            DrawSelectedPointPanel(rightZone.localPoints, rightZone.selectedIndex);
        }
        else
        {
            ImGui::TextDisabled(u8"ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚­‚¾‚³‚¢");
        }

        ImGui::EndTable();
    }

    ImGui::End();
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawTriggerSection(const char* label, AdaptiveTrigger::TriggerEffect& effect, ZoneState& zone, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue)
{
    ImGui::PushID(label);

    ImGui::Text(label);

    if (!zone.exists)
    {
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 80.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"+ ƒ][ƒ“’Ç‰Á"))
        {
            zone.exists = true;
            zone.start = 0.0f;
            zone.end = 1.0f;
            zone.localPoints = { {0.0f, 0.5f}, {1.0f, 0.5f} };
        }
    }
    else
    {
        ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f + ImGui::GetCursorPosX());
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ"))
        {
            zone.exists = false;
            zone.selectedIndex = -1;
        }
    }

    float width = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;

    DrawRangeBar(zone, width, rangeDrag, currentValue);

    ImGui::Spacing();

    if (zone.exists)
    {
        bool withinZone = (currentValue >= zone.start && currentValue <= zone.end && zone.end > zone.start);
        float localCurrent = withinZone ? (currentValue - zone.start) / (zone.end - zone.start) : 0.0f;

        int dummyOther = -1;
        DrawGraph(zone.localPoints, zone.selectedIndex, dummyOther, localCurrent, withinZone);
    }

    auto& points = effect.forcePoints;
    points.clear();
    if (zone.exists)
    {
        for (const auto& lp : zone.localPoints)
        {
            float absPos = zone.start + lp.position * (zone.end - zone.start);
            points.push_back({ absPos, lp.value });
        }
    }

    ImGui::PopID();
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawRangeBar(ZoneState& zone, float width, RangeDragState& rangeDrag, float currentValue)
{
    ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    const float height = 24.0f;
    ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

    drawList->AddRectFilled(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + width, pos.y + height), IM_COL32(60, 60, 60, 255), 4.0f);

    ImGui::InvisibleButton("##rangeBar", ImVec2(width, height));
    bool barClicked = ImGui::IsItemClicked();
    bool barActive = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();

    if (zone.exists)
    {
        float x1 = pos.x + zone.start * width;
        float x2 = pos.x + zone.end * width;

        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x1, pos.y), ImVec2(x2, pos.y + height), AccentColor);

        const float handleHalf = 3.0f;
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x1 - handleHalf, pos.y - 3.0f), ImVec2(x1 + handleHalf, pos.y + height + 3.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x2 - handleHalf, pos.y - 3.0f), ImVec2(x2 + handleHalf, pos.y + height + 3.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));

        const float pickRange = 8.0f;

        if (barClicked)
        {
            if (std::abs(mousePos.x - x1) <= pickRange)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Left;
            }
            else if (std::abs(mousePos.x - x2) <= pickRange)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Right;
            }
            else if (mousePos.x >= x1 && mousePos.x <= x2)
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::Move;
                rangeDrag.startMouseX = mousePos.x;
                rangeDrag.startZoneStart = zone.start;
                rangeDrag.startZoneEnd = zone.end;
            }
            else
            {
                rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::None;
            }
        }

        if (barActive)
        {
            float mouseNormX = std::clamp((mousePos.x - pos.x) / width, 0.0f, 1.0f);

            if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Left)
            {
                zone.start = std::clamp(mouseNormX, 0.0f, zone.end - 0.01f);
            }
            else if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Right)
            {
                zone.end = std::clamp(mouseNormX, zone.start + 0.01f, 1.0f);
            }
            else if (rangeDrag.mode == RangeDragState::Mode::Move)
            {
                float rawDelta = (mousePos.x - rangeDrag.startMouseX) / width;
                float zoneWidth = rangeDrag.startZoneEnd - rangeDrag.startZoneStart;
                float clampedDelta = std::clamp(rawDelta, -rangeDrag.startZoneStart, 1.0f - rangeDrag.startZoneEnd);
                zone.start = rangeDrag.startZoneStart + clampedDelta;
                zone.end = rangeDrag.startZoneStart + clampedDelta + zoneWidth;
            }
        }

        if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
        {
            rangeDrag.mode = RangeDragState::Mode::None;
        }
    }

    // Œ»İ‚ÌƒgƒŠƒK[‰Ÿ‚µ‚İˆÊ’uiÔüj
    float curX = pos.x + std::clamp(currentValue, 0.0f, 1.0f) * width;
    drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(curX, pos.y - 4.0f), ImVec2(curX, pos.y + height + 4.0f), IndicatorColor, 2.0f);

    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(width, height));

    ImGui::TextDisabled("0");
    ImGui::SameLine(width - 24.0f);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("255");
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawGraph(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex, int& otherSelectedIndex, float localCurrentValue, bool showIndicator)
{
    const float margin = 8.0f;

    ImVec2 graphSize(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x, 150.0f);
    ImVec2 graphPos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

    ImGui::InvisibleButton("##graphBg", graphSize);
    bool bgClicked = ImGui::IsItemClicked();
    bool bgActive = ImGui::IsItemActive();
    ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();

    ImDrawList* drawList = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    drawList->AddRect(graphPos, ImVec2(graphPos.x + graphSize.x, graphPos.y + graphSize.y), IM_COL32(150, 150, 150, 255));

    float innerX = graphPos.x + margin;
    float innerWidth = graphSize.x - margin * 2.0f;
    float innerY = graphPos.y + margin;
    float innerHeight = graphSize.y - margin * 2.0f;

    auto ToScreen = [&](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& p) -> ImVec2
        {
            return ImVec2(innerX + p.position * innerWidth, innerY + (1.0f - p.value) * innerHeight);
        };
    auto FromScreen = [&](ImVec2 screen) -> AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint
        {
            AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint p;
            p.position = std::clamp((screen.x - innerX) / innerWidth, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            p.value = std::clamp(1.0f - (screen.y - innerY) / innerHeight, 0.0f, 1.0f);
            return p;
        };

    for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
    {
        drawList->AddLine(ToScreen(points[i]), ToScreen(points[i + 1]), AccentColor, 2.0f);
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
    {
        ImVec2 p = ToScreen(points[i]);
        bool isSelected = (static_cast<int>(i) == selectedIndex);
        float radius = isSelected ? 7.0f : 5.0f;
        drawList->AddCircleFilled(p, radius, AccentColor);
        if (isSelected)
        {
            drawList->AddCircle(p, radius + 2.0f, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255), 0, 2.0f);
        }
    }

    // Œ»İ’n‚ÌÔüiƒ][ƒ““à‚É‚¢‚é‚¾‚¯•\¦j
    if (showIndicator)
    {
        float curX = innerX + std::clamp(localCurrentValue, 0.0f, 1.0f) * innerWidth;
        drawList->AddLine(ImVec2(curX, graphPos.y), ImVec2(curX, graphPos.y + graphSize.y), IndicatorColor, 2.0f);
    }

    const float pickRadius = 8.0f;
    const int lastIndex = static_cast<int>(points.size()) - 1;

    if (bgClicked)
    {
        int picked = -1;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            ImVec2 p = ToScreen(points[i]);
            float dx = mousePos.x - p.x;
            float dy = mousePos.y - p.y;
            if ((dx * dx + dy * dy) <= (pickRadius * pickRadius))
            {
                picked = static_cast<int>(i);
                break;
            }
        }

        if (picked == -1)
        {
            AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint newPoint = FromScreen(mousePos);

            // ––”ö‚Ì‹«ŠE“_(100%)‚Ìè‘O‚É‘}“ü‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çA’†ŠÔ•”•ª‚¾‚¯•À‚Ñ‘Ö‚¦‚é
            points.insert(points.end() - 1, newPoint);
            if (points.size() > 2)
            {
                std::sort(points.begin() + 1, points.end() - 1,
                    [](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& a, const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& b)
                    {
                        return a.position < b.position;
                    });
            }

            // ‘}“ü‚µ‚½ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ÌV‚µ‚¢index‚ğ’T‚·
            for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
            {
                if (points[i].position == newPoint.position && points[i].value == newPoint.value)
                {
                    picked = static_cast<int>(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        selectedIndex = picked;
        otherSelectedIndex = -1;
    }

    if (bgActive && selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < static_cast<int>(points.size()))
    {
        // æ“ªE––”öiƒ][ƒ“‚Ì‹«ŠEj‚ÍXŒÅ’èAY‚¾‚¯“®‚©‚·
        if (selectedIndex == 0)
        {
            points[selectedIndex].value = FromScreen(mousePos).value;
            points[selectedIndex].position = 0.0f;
        }
        else if (selectedIndex == lastIndex)
        {
            points[selectedIndex].value = FromScreen(mousePos).value;
            points[selectedIndex].position = 1.0f;
        }
        else
        {
            points[selectedIndex] = FromScreen(mousePos);
        }
    }

    if (!ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left) &&
        selectedIndex > 0 && selectedIndex < lastIndex) // ‹«ŠE“_‚Í•À‚Ñ‘Ö‚¦•s—v
    {
        AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint selected = points[selectedIndex];
        std::sort(points.begin() + 1, points.end() - 1,
            [](const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& a, const AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& b)
            {
                return a.position < b.position;
            });
        for (size_t i = 1; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            if (points[i].position == selected.position && points[i].value == selected.value)
            {
                selectedIndex = static_cast<int>(i);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

void AdaptiveTriggerToolWindow::DrawSelectedPointPanel(std::vector<AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint>& points, int& selectedIndex)
{
    if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= static_cast<int>(points.size()))
    {
        ImGui::TextDisabled(u8"ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğ‘I‘ğ‚µ‚Ä‚­‚¾‚³‚¢");
        return;
    }

    const int lastIndex = static_cast<int>(points.size()) - 1;
    const bool isBoundary = (selectedIndex == 0 || selectedIndex == lastIndex);

    ImGui::PushID(selectedIndex);
    ImGui::TextDisabled(u8"‘I‘ğ’†‚Ìƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg");
    ImGui::Spacing();

    AdaptiveTrigger::CurvePoint& p = points[selectedIndex];

    ImGui::Text(u8"X (ƒ][ƒ““àˆÊ’u)");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    if (isBoundary)
    {
        ImGui::BeginDisabled();
        float fixedX = p.position;
        ImGui::DragFloat("##posX", &fixedX, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
    }
    else
    {
        ImGui::DragFloat("##posX", &p.position, 0.005f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");
    }

    ImGui::Spacing();

    ImGui::Text(u8"Y (‹­‚³)");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    ImGui::DragFloat("##posY", &p.value, 0.005f, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.3f");

    ImGui::Spacing();

    if (isBoundary)
    {
        ImGui::BeginDisabled();
        ImGui::Button(u8"íœi‹«ŠE‚Ì‚½‚ß•s‰Âj", ImVec2(-1, 0));
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
    }
    else if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ", ImVec2(-1, 0)))
    {
        points.erase(points.begin() + selectedIndex);
        selectedIndex = -1;
        ImGui::PopID();
        return;
    }

    ImGui::PopID();
}

	// ƒGƒ“ƒeƒBƒeƒB‚ª‚ÂƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÌÀ‘Ô‚ğmeta_any‚Æ‚µ‚Äæ“¾‚·‚é

};

===== EditorContext.h =====

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include "entt.hpp"

// ƒJƒƒ‰ƒ‚[ƒh
enum class CameraMode
{
    Debug,  // ƒfƒoƒbƒOƒJƒƒ‰iƒ}ƒEƒXEƒL[ƒ{[ƒhj
    Game,   // ƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰  iƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[j
};

// StudioƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
struct StudioOpenRequest
{
    bool        pending = false;
    std::string assetPath;
    std::string graphPath;
};

struct EditorContext
{
    entt::entity selectedEntity = entt::null;
    std::unordered_map<entt::entity, std::string> entityNames;

    // ƒJƒƒ‰ƒ‚[ƒh
    CameraMode cameraMode = CameraMode::Debug;
    // ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚½‚©
    bool rightClickPressed = false;  

    // StudioƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒEŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
    StudioOpenRequest studioRequest;

    std::string GetName(entt::entity e) const
    {
        auto it = entityNames.find(e);
        if (it != entityNames.end()) return it->second;
        return "Entity " + std::to_string((uint32_t)e);
    }

    void SetName(entt::entity e, const std::string& name)
    {
        entityNames[e] = name;
    }

    void Select(entt::entity e) { selectedEntity = e; }
    void Deselect() { selectedEntity = entt::null; }

    bool IsSelected(entt::entity e) const
    {
        return selectedEntity == e;
    }
};

===== ComponentRegistry.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\Editor\ComponentRegistry.cpp 
#include "ComponentRegistry.h"

std::unordered_map<std::string, ComponentInfo> ComponentRegistry::registry;
std::vector<std::string> ComponentRegistry::names;
std::unordered_map<std::string, std::string> ComponentRegistry::typeNameToDisplayName;

void ComponentRegistry::Register(const std::string& name, ComponentInfo info)
{
    if (registry.find(name) != registry.end()) return;
    info.name = name;
    registry[name] = info;
    names.push_back(name);
}

const std::vector<std::string>& ComponentRegistry::GetNames()
{
    return names;
}

const ComponentInfo* ComponentRegistry::Get(const std::string& name)
{
    auto it = registry.find(name);
    if (it == registry.end()) return nullptr;
    return &it->second;
}

void ComponentRegistry::RegisterTypeName(const std::string& typeName, const std::string& displayName)
{
    typeNameToDisplayName[typeName] = displayName;
}

const ComponentInfo* ComponentRegistry::GetByTypeName(const std::string& typeName)
{
    auto it = typeNameToDisplayName.find(typeName);
    if (it == typeNameToDisplayName.end()) return nullptr;
    return Get(it->second);
} 
 
===== ComponentRegistryInitializer.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\Editor\ComponentRegistryInitializer.cpp 
#include "ComponentRegistryInitializer.h"
#include "Tool/Editor/ComponentRegistry.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <Windows.h>
#include <commdlg.h>
#include <filesystem>
#include <algorithm>

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MaterialComponent.h"

#include "Project/ECS/Components/GameInputComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/ControllerComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MovementComponent.h"

#include "Project/ECS/Components/PlayerIdleComponent.h"
#include "Project/ECS/Components/PlayerMoveComponent.h"
#include "Project/ECS/Components/PlayerDodgeComponent.h"
#include "Project/ECS/Components/PlayerSprintComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SkinnedMeshComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/FreeCameraComponent.h"
#include "Project/ECS/Components/GameCameraComponent.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/PhysicsComponents.h"

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SequencerComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimationSpeedComponent.h"

#include "MetaRegistry.h"

#define REGISTER_COMPONENT(Type, Name, DrawFunc)                                    \
    ComponentRegistry::Register(Name,                                               \
    {                                                                               \
        Name,                                                                       \
        [](auto& r, auto e){ if (!r.any_of<Type>(e)) r.emplace<Type>(e); },         \
        [](auto& r, auto e){ r.remove<Type>(e); },                                  \
        [](auto& r, auto e){ return r.any_of<Type>(e); },                           \
        DrawFunc,                                                                   \
        [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity e) -> entt::meta_any                     \
        {                                                                           \
            auto* c = r.try_get<Type>(e);                                           \
            if (!c) return entt::meta_any{};                                        \
            return entt::forward_as_meta(*c);                                       \
        }                                                                           \
    });                                                                             \
    ComponentRegistry::RegisterTypeName(#Type, Name)

#define DRAW_COMPONENT(Type, Label, body)                                           \
    [](auto& reg, auto e)                                                           \
    {                                                                               \
        auto* _c = reg.try_get<Type>(e);                                            \
        if (!_c) return;                                                            \
        if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader(Label, ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))         \
        { body }                                                                    \
    }

void ComponentRegistryInitializer::Initialize()
{
	// ƒgƒ‰ƒ“ƒXƒtƒH[ƒ€ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(TransformComponent, "Transform",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<TransformComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

			if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Transform", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
			{
				MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<TransformComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*_c));
			}
		});

	// ƒƒbƒVƒ…ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(MeshComponent, "Mesh",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<MeshComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

			if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Mesh", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
			{
				MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<MeshComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*_c));
			}
		});

	// ƒ}ƒeƒŠƒAƒ‹ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	ComponentRegistry::Register("Material",
		{
			"Material",
			[](auto& reg, auto e) { if (!reg.any_of<MaterialComponent>(e)) reg.emplace<MaterialComponent>(e); },
			[](auto& reg, auto e) { reg.remove<MaterialComponent>(e); },
			[](auto& reg, auto e) { return reg.any_of<MaterialComponent>(e); },
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				auto* mat = reg.try_get<MaterialComponent>(e);
				if (!mat) return;
				if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Material", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;

				const char* shaderNames[] = { "Default (PBR)", "Basic" };
				int current = (int)mat->shadingModel;
				if (ImGui::Combo("Shader", &current, shaderNames, 2))
				{
					mat->shadingModel = (ShadingModel)current;
				}
			}
		});

	// Player‘Ò‹@ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(PlayerIdleComponent, "Player Idle",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<PlayerIdleComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

			if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Player Idle", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
			{
				MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<PlayerIdleComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*_c));
			}
		});

	// PlayerˆÚ“®ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(PlayerMoveComponent, "Player Move",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<PlayerMoveComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

			if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Player Move", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
			{
				MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<PlayerMoveComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*_c));
			}
		});
	// DodgeƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(PlayerDodgeComponent, "Player Dodge",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<PlayerDodgeComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

	// SprintƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(PlayerSprintComponent, "Player Sprint",

	// ƒCƒ“ƒvƒbƒgƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(GameInputComponent, "GameInput",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* _c = reg.try_get<GameInputComponent>(e);
			if (!_c) return;

			if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Input", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
			{
				MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<GameInputComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*_c));
			}
		});

	// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒ^[ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(AnimatorComponent, "Animator",
		[](auto& reg, auto e)
		{
			auto* anim = reg.try_get<AnimatorComponent>(e);
			auto* skinned = reg.try_get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(e);
			if (!anim) return;

			if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Animator", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;

			if (!skinned || !skinned->model)
			{
				ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, "No SkinnedMesh found.");
				return;
			}
			const auto& animations = skinned->model->GetAnimations();
			if (animations.empty())
			{
				ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, "No animations found.");
				return;
			}

			if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Animation", anim->currentAnimation.c_str()))
			{
				for (const auto& animation : animations)
				{
					bool selected = (anim->currentAnimation == animation.name);
					if (ImGui::Selectable(animation.name.c_str(), selected))
					{
						anim->currentAnimation = animation.name;
						anim->animationTime = 0.0f;
						anim->isPlaying = true;
						anim->isEnd = false;
					}
					if (selected) ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
				}
				ImGui::EndCombo();
			}

			if (anim->isPlaying)
			{
				if (ImGui::Button("Stop")) anim->isPlaying = false;
			}
			else
			{
				if (ImGui::Button("Play")) { anim->isPlaying = true; anim->isEnd = false; }
			}
			MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<AnimatorComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*anim));
		});

	// ƒtƒŠ[ƒJƒƒ‰ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(FreeCameraComponent, "FreeCamera",
		DRAW_COMPONENT(FreeCameraComponent, "FreeCamera",
			{
				ImGui::DragFloat("Camera Speed", &_c->cameraSpeed, 0.1f, 0.1f, 100.0f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Yaw",          &_c->yaw,         0.01f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Pitch",        &_c->pitch,       0.01f, -1.5f, 1.5f);
				ImGui::Separator();
				ImGui::DragFloat3("Eye",    &_c->base.currentEye.x,    0.1f);
				ImGui::DragFloat3("Target", &_c->base.currentTarget.x, 0.1f);
			}));

	// ƒQ[ƒ€ƒJƒƒ‰ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	ComponentRegistry::Register("GameCamera",
		{
			"GameCamera",
			[](auto& reg, auto e) { if (!reg.any_of<GameCameraComponent>(e)) reg.emplace<GameCameraComponent>(e); },
			[](auto& reg, auto e) { reg.remove<GameCameraComponent>(e); },
			[](auto& reg, auto e) { return reg.any_of<GameCameraComponent>(e); },
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				auto* _c = reg.try_get<GameCameraComponent>(e);
				if (!_c) return;
				if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("GameCamera", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;

				const char* modeNames[] = { "Follow", "LookAt" };
				int mode = (int)_c->mode;
				if (ImGui::Combo("Mode", &mode, modeNames, 2))
					_c->mode = (Mode)mode;

				// ’Ç]‘ÎÛ(FollowTarget)
				std::string followTargetName = "None";
				if (_c->followTarget != entt::null && reg.valid(_c->followTarget))
					followTargetName = "Entity " + std::to_string((uint32_t)_c->followTarget);

				ImGui::Text("Follow Target"); ImGui::SameLine();
				ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f);
				ImGui::InputText("##followTarget", followTargetName.data(),
					followTargetName.size() + 1, ImGuiInputTextFlags_ReadOnly);

				if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
				{
					if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("ENTITY"))
						_c->followTarget = *(const entt::entity*)payload->Data;
					ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
				}
				ImGui::SameLine();
				if (ImGui::Button("Clear##followTarget")) _c->followTarget = entt::null;

				// ’‹‘ÎÛ(LookAtTargetA” ‚Ì‚İ)
				std::string lookAtTargetName = "None";
				if (_c->lookAtTarget != entt::null && reg.valid(_c->lookAtTarget))
					lookAtTargetName = "Entity " + std::to_string((uint32_t)_c->lookAtTarget);

				ImGui::Text("LookAt Target"); ImGui::SameLine();
				ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f);
				ImGui::InputText("##lookAtTarget", lookAtTargetName.data(),
					lookAtTargetName.size() + 1, ImGuiInputTextFlags_ReadOnly);

				if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
				{
					if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("ENTITY"))
						_c->lookAtTarget = *(const entt::entity*)payload->Data;
					ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
				}
				ImGui::SameLine();
				if (ImGui::Button("Clear##lookAtTarget")) _c->lookAtTarget = entt::null;

				ImGui::DragFloat("Pitch", &_c->pitch, 0.01f, -1.5f, 1.5f);
				ImGui::Separator();
				ImGui::Text("Follow Camera");
				ImGui::DragFloat("Distance",     &_c->followCameraDistance,  0.1f, 0.0f, 50.0f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Height",       &_c->followCameraHeight,    0.1f, 0.0f, 20.0f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Target Y Up",  &_c->followCameraTargetYUp, 0.1f, 0.0f, 10.0f);
				ImGui::DragFloat("Speed Ratio",  &_c->followCameraSpeedRatio, 0.5f, 0.0f, 100.0f);
				ImGui::Separator();
				ImGui::DragFloat3("Eye",    &_c->base.currentEye.x,    0.1f);
				ImGui::DragFloat3("Target", &_c->base.currentTarget.x, 0.1f);
			}
		});

	// ƒRƒ“ƒgƒ[ƒ‰[ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	REGISTER_COMPONENT(ControllerComponent, "Controller",
		DRAW_COMPONENT(ControllerComponent, "Controller",
			{
				if (_c->connected)
				{
					const char* typeName = _c->type == ControllerType::PS5 ? "PS5 DualSense" : "Xbox";
					ImGui::TextColored({ 0.3f, 1.0f, 0.3f, 1.0f }, u8"[%s] Ú‘±’†", typeName);
				}
				else
				{
					ImGui::TextColored({ 0.6f, 0.6f, 0.6f, 1.0f }, u8"–¢Ú‘±");
				}
			}));


	// •¨—ƒ^ƒCƒvƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
	ComponentRegistry::Register("Physics",
		{
			"Physics",
			// Add: PhysicsBodyTypeComponent‚Ì‚İ•t—^(í•Ê–¢‘I‘ğó‘Ô)
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				if (!reg.any_of<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e))
					reg.emplace<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);
			},
		// Remove: ‹¤’ÊƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Æ•R‚Ã‚¢‚½ŒÂ•Ê‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ğ—¼•ûíœ
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				if (reg.any_of<StaticBodyComponent>(e))
					reg.remove<StaticBodyComponent>(e);

				if (auto* ctrl = reg.try_get<CharacterControllerComponent>(e))
				{
					// ‚±‚ê‚Í1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€’x‰„‚³‚¹A‘¼‚ÌXV‚Ì“à‚Å‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚Äˆ—‚·‚é
					PhysicsDestroyQueue::Push(ctrl->controller);
					reg.remove<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);
				}

				// CharacterController‚ÆƒZƒbƒg‚Å¶‚Ü‚ê‚½MovementComponent‚àˆê‚ÉŠO‚·
				if (reg.any_of<MovementComponent>(e))
					reg.remove<MovementComponent>(e);

				reg.remove<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);		},
		// Has
		[](auto& reg, auto e) { return reg.any_of<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e); },
		// DrawImGui
				[](auto& reg, auto e)
				{
					auto* body = reg.try_get<PhysicsBodyTypeComponent>(e);
					if (!body) return;
					if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Physics", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;

					const char* typeNames[] = { "None", "Static", "Dynamic", "Character" };
					int current = (int)body->type;

					if (ImGui::Combo("Type", &current, typeNames, 4))
					{
						auto newType = (PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type)current;

						// Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦‘O‚ÌÀ‘Ì‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Îíœ—\–ñ‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çŠO‚·
						if (body->type == PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Static)
						{
							auto* staticBody = reg.try_get<StaticBodyComponent>(e);
							if (staticBody && staticBody->actor)
							{
								PhysicsDestroyQueue::Push(staticBody->actor);
							}
							reg.remove<StaticBodyComponent>(e);
						}
						else if (body->type == PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Character)
						{
							auto* ctrl = reg.try_get<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);
							if (ctrl && ctrl->controller)
							{
								PhysicsDestroyQueue::Push(ctrl->controller);
							}
							reg.remove<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);

							// CharacterController‚ÆƒZƒbƒg‚Å¶‚Ü‚ê‚½MovementComponent‚àˆê‚ÉŠO‚·
							if (reg.any_of<MovementComponent>(e))
								reg.remove<MovementComponent>(e);
						}

						body->type = newType;

						// Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦Œã‚ÌÀ‘Ì‚ğV‹Kì¬
						if (newType == PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Static)
						{
							reg.emplace<StaticBodyComponent>(e);
						}
						else if (newType == PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Character)
						{
							reg.emplace<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);

							// CharacterController‚ÆƒZƒbƒg‚ÅMovementComponent‚à¶¬
							if (!reg.any_of<MovementComponent>(e))
								reg.emplace<MovementComponent>(e);
						}
					}

					// ‘I‘ğ’†‚Ìí•Ê‚²‚Æ‚Ì€–Ú•\¦
					switch (body->type)
					{
					case PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Static:
					{
						auto* staticBody = reg.try_get<StaticBodyComponent>(e);
						if (staticBody)
						{
							ImGui::Text(staticBody->actor ? u8"ó‘Ô: ¶¬Ï‚İ" : u8"ó‘Ô: –¢¶¬");
						}
						break;
					}
					case PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Dynamic:
						ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, u8"Dynamic‚Í–¢À‘•‚Å‚·");
						break;
					case PhysicsBodyTypeComponent::Type::Character:
					{
						auto* ctrl = reg.try_get<CharacterControllerComponent>(e);
						if (ctrl)
						{
							bool created = (ctrl->controller != nullptr);

							ImGui::Text(created ? u8"ó‘Ô: ¶¬Ï‚İ" : u8"ó‘Ô: –¢¶¬");
						}
						break;
					}

					default:
						break;
					}
				} 
		});

		// IKƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
		REGISTER_COMPONENT(IKComponent, "IK",
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				auto* ik = reg.try_get<IKComponent>(e);
				auto* skinned = reg.try_get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(e);
				if (!ik) return;
				if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("IK", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) return;
				if (!skinned || !skinned->model)
				{
					ImGui::TextColored({ 1, 0.5f, 0, 1 }, "No SkinnedMesh found.");
					return;
				}
				ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled", &ik->enabled);
				int removeIndex = -1; // ‚±‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Åíœ‚µ‚½‚¢foot‚ÌƒCƒ“ƒfƒbƒNƒX(-1‚È‚çíœ‚È‚µ)
				for (size_t i = 0; i < ik->feet.size(); ++i)
				{
					ImGui::PushID((int)i);
					std::string label = "Foot " + std::to_string(i);
					if (ImGui::TreeNode(label.c_str()))
					{
						FootIKSettings& foot = ik->feet[i];
						auto& nodes = skinned->model->GetNodes();
						auto drawBoneCombo = [&](const char* comboLabel, int& boneIndex)
							{
								const char* previewName = (boneIndex >= 0 && boneIndex < (int)nodes.size())
									? nodes[boneIndex].name.c_str()
									: "(Invalid Index)";

								if (ImGui::BeginCombo(comboLabel, previewName))
								{
									for (int n = 0; n < (int)nodes.size(); ++n)
									{
										bool isSelected = (boneIndex == n);
										if (ImGui::Selectable(nodes[n].name.c_str(), isSelected))
										{
											boneIndex = n;
										}
										if (isSelected)
										{
											ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
										}
									}
									ImGui::EndCombo();
								}
							};
						drawBoneCombo("Root Bone", foot.rootBoneIndex);
						drawBoneCombo("Mid Bone", foot.midBoneIndex);
						drawBoneCombo("End Bone", foot.endBoneIndex);
						ImGui::DragFloat("Ray Start Offset", &foot.rayStartOffset, 0.01f);
						ImGui::DragFloat("Ray Max Distance", &foot.rayMaxDistance, 0.01f);
						ImGui::DragFloat("Ankle Height", &foot.ankleHeight, 0.01f);
						ImGui::Checkbox("Pole Vector Forward (Z-)", &foot.poleVectorForward);
						ImGui::DragFloat3("Target Pos", &foot.targetPos.x, 0.01f);
						if (ImGui::Button("Reset Target Pos"))
						{
							foot.hasInitializedTarget = false;
						}
						if (ImGui::Button("Remove This Foot"))
						{
							removeIndex = (int)i;
						}
						ImGui::TreePop();
					}
					ImGui::PopID();
				}
				if (removeIndex >= 0)
				{
					ik->feet.erase(ik->feet.begin() + removeIndex);
				}
				if (ImGui::Button("Add Foot"))
				{
					ik->feet.push_back(FootIKSettings{});
				}
			}); 


		// ƒV[ƒPƒ“ƒT[ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
		REGISTER_COMPONENT(SequencerComponent, "Sequencer",
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				auto* seq = reg.try_get<SequencerComponent>(e);
				if (!seq) return;

				if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Sequencer", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
				{
					MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<SequencerComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*seq));
				}
			}); 

		// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‘¬“xƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚Ì“o˜^
		REGISTER_COMPONENT(AnimationSpeedComponent, "Animation Speed",
			[](auto& reg, auto e)
			{
				auto* animSpeed = reg.try_get<AnimationSpeedComponent>(e);
				if (!animSpeed) return;

				if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Animation Speed", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
				{
					MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::resolve<AnimationSpeedComponent>(), entt::forward_as_meta(*animSpeed));
				}
			});
}

#include "HierarchyWindow.h"
#include "imgui_internal.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <filesystem>

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MaterialComponent.h"

#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"

void HierarchyWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    ZoneScoped;

    ImGui::Begin("Hierarchy");

    // ‹ó‚Ì Entity ‚ğ’Ç‰Á‚·‚éƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
    if (ImGui::Button("+ Add Entity"))
    {
        entt::entity e = registry.create();

        registry.emplace<TransformComponent>(e);

        // –¼‘O‚ğ©“®İ’è
        context.SetName(e, "Entity");

        // ì¬‚µ‚½ Entity ‚ğ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚É
        context.Select(e);
    }
    // ‘I‘ğ’†‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚¾‚¯ Delete ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ—×‚É•\¦
    if (context.selectedEntity != entt::null)
    {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::Button("Delete"))
        {
            registry.destroy(context.selectedEntity);
            context.Deselect();
        }
    }

    ImGui::Separator();
    // ƒZƒpƒŒ[ƒ^‚Ì‰º‚ÌÀ•W‚ğ‹L˜^
    ImVec2 dropAreaMin = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

    //// ‘S Entity ‚ğ—ñ‹“
    //auto view = registry.view<entt::entity>();
    //for (auto e : view)
    //{
    //    std::string name = context.GetName(e);

    //    // ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚ğƒnƒCƒ‰ƒCƒg
    //    bool isSelected = context.IsSelected(e);
    //    if (ImGui::Selectable(
    //        (name + "##" + std::to_string((uint32_t)e)).c_str(),
    //        isSelected))
    //    {
    //        context.Select(e);
    //    }

    //    // Entity ‚ğƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚Å‚«‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
    //    if (ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
    //    {
    //        // Entity ID ‚ğƒyƒCƒ[ƒh‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“n‚·
    //        ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
    //            "ENTITY",
    //            &e,
    //            sizeof(entt::entity)
    //        );
    //        // ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO’†‚ÌƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[
    //        ImGui::Text(name.c_str());
    //        ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
    //    }
    //}
    // ‘S Entity ‚ğ—ñ‹“(‰æ–Ê‚É‰f‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é”ÍˆÍ‚¾‚¯ˆ—‚·‚é)
    auto view = registry.view<entt::entity>();

    // entt::view ‚Íƒ‰ƒ“ƒ_ƒ€ƒAƒNƒZƒX‚Å‚«‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅAˆê’U”z—ñ‚ÉW‚ß‚é
    // (‚±‚ê©‘Ì‚ÍŒy‚¢ˆ—:ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒ^‘Š“–‚Ìentity’l‚ğƒRƒs[‚·‚é‚¾‚¯)
    std::vector<entt::entity> entities(view.begin(), view.end());

    ImGuiListClipper clipper;
    clipper.Begin(static_cast<int>(entities.size()));
    while (clipper.Step())
    {
        for (int i = clipper.DisplayStart; i < clipper.DisplayEnd; i++)
        {
            entt::entity e = entities[i];

            std::string name = context.GetName(e);
            // ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚ğƒnƒCƒ‰ƒCƒg
            bool isSelected = context.IsSelected(e);
            if (ImGui::Selectable(
                (name + "##" + std::to_string((uint32_t)e)).c_str(),
                isSelected))
            {
                context.Select(e);
            }
            // Entity ‚ğƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚Å‚«‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
            if (ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
            {
                // Entity ID ‚ğƒyƒCƒ[ƒh‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“n‚·
                ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
                    "ENTITY",
                    &e,
                    sizeof(entt::entity)
                );
                // ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO’†‚ÌƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[
                ImGui::Text(name.c_str());
                ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
            }
        }
    }

    // ƒZƒpƒŒ[ƒ^ˆÈ‰ºƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE––”ö‚Ü‚Å‚ğƒhƒƒbƒvƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg‚É
    ImVec2 dropAreaMax = ImGui::GetWindowPos();
    dropAreaMax.x += ImGui::GetWindowSize().x;
    dropAreaMax.y += ImGui::GetWindowSize().y;

    ImRect dropRect(dropAreaMin, dropAreaMax);

	// ƒhƒƒbƒvƒ^[ƒQƒbƒg
    if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTargetCustom(dropRect, ImGui::GetCurrentWindow()->ID))
    {
        if (const ImGuiPayload* payload =
            ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("MODEL_PATH"))
        {                
            // ƒpƒX‚ğó‚¯æ‚é
            std::string modelPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));

            // Entity ¶¬
            entt::entity e = registry.create();

            auto& t = registry.emplace<TransformComponent>(e);
            t.position = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
            t.scale = { 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f };

            registry.emplace<MeshComponent>(e, modelPath);
            registry.emplace<MaterialComponent>(e);

            // ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼‚©‚ç Entity –¼‚ğİ’è
            std::filesystem::path p(modelPath);
            context.SetName(e, p.stem().string());
            context.Select(e);
        }
        ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
    }
    ImGui::End();
} 
 
===== InspectorWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\Editor\InspectorWindow.cpp 
#include "InspectorWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include "imgui_internal.h"
#include "ComponentRegistry.h"

#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"

// •`‰æ
void InspectorWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    ZoneScoped;

    ImGui::Begin("Inspector");

    // ‰½‚à‘I‘ğ‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¯‚ê‚Î‰½‚à•\¦‚µ‚È‚¢
    if (context.selectedEntity == entt::null)
    {
        ImGui::Text("No entity selected.");
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    // –¼‘O•ÒW
    DrawNameField(registry, context);

    ImGui::Separator();

    // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒgˆê——
    DrawComponents(registry, context);

    ImGui::Separator();

    // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg’Ç‰Áƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
    DrawAddComponent(registry, context);

    ImGui::End();
}

// –¼‘O•ÒWUI
void InspectorWindow::DrawNameField(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    entt::entity e = context.selectedEntity;
    std::string name = context.GetName(e);

    char buf[256] = {};
    strncpy_s(buf, name.c_str(), sizeof(buf) - 1);

    if (ImGui::InputText("Name", buf, sizeof(buf)))
    {
        context.SetName(e, buf);
    }
}

// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒgˆê——UI
void InspectorWindow::DrawComponents(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    entt::entity e = context.selectedEntity;

    for (const auto& name : ComponentRegistry::GetNames())
    {
        const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(name);
        if (!info) continue;
        // ‚±‚ÌEntity‚ªƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ğ‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
        if (!info->has(registry, e)) continue;


        // íœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
        ImGui::PushID(name.c_str());
        if (ImGui::Button("x"))
        {
            info->remove(registry, e);
            ImGui::PopID();
            // íœŒã‚ÍƒCƒeƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğ’†’f
            break;
        }
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::PopID();


        // ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÌImGui‚ğ•`‰æ
        info->drawImGui(registry, e);
    }
}

// ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg’Ç‰ÁUI
void InspectorWindow::DrawAddComponent(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
    entt::entity e = context.selectedEntity;

    if (ImGui::Button("+ Add Component"))
    {
        ImGui::OpenPopup("AddComponentPopup");
    }

    if (ImGui::BeginPopup("AddComponentPopup"))
    {
        ImGui::Text("Select Component");
        ImGui::Separator();

        for (const auto& name : ComponentRegistry::GetNames())
        {
            const ComponentInfo* info = ComponentRegistry::Get(name);
            if (!info) continue;

            // ‚·‚Å‚É‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚éƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ÍƒOƒŒ[ƒAƒEƒg
            if (info->has(registry, e))
            {
                ImGui::TextDisabled(name.c_str());
            }
            else
            {
                if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str()))
                {
                    info->add(registry, e);
                    ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
                }
            }
        }
        ImGui::EndPopup();
    }
}

#include "MetaRegistry.h"
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include <filesystem>

using namespace entt::literals;

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"

}

// “o˜^Ï‚İƒƒ^î•ñ‚©‚çInspectorUI‚ğ©“®•`‰æ
void MetaRegistry::DrawMetaFields(entt::meta_type metaType, entt::meta_any instance)
{
	if (!metaType) return;

	for (auto&& [id, metaData] : metaType.data())
	{
		FieldInfo* fieldInfo = metaData.custom();
		std::string label = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->label : std::string(metaData.name());

		entt::meta_any value = metaData.get(instance);

		if (float* f = value.try_cast<float>())
		{
			if (fieldInfo && fieldInfo->readOnly)
			{
				ImGui::Text("%s: %.2f", label.c_str(), *f);
			}
			else
			{
				float minV = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->minValue : 0.0f;
				float maxV = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->maxValue : 0.0f;
				float speed = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->dragSpeed : 0.1f;

				if (ImGui::DragFloat(label.c_str(), f, speed, minV, maxV))
				{
					metaData.set(instance, *f);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (DirectX::XMFLOAT3* v = value.try_cast<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>())
		{
			float speed = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->dragSpeed : 0.1f;

			if (ImGui::DragFloat3(label.c_str(), &v->x, speed))
			{
				metaData.set(instance, *v);
			}
		}
		else if (bool* b = value.try_cast<bool>())
		{
			if (fieldInfo && fieldInfo->readOnly)
			{
				ImGui::Text("%s: %s", label.c_str(), *b ? "true" : "false");
			}
			else
			{
				if (ImGui::Checkbox(label.c_str(), b))
				{
					metaData.set(instance, *b);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (std::string* s = value.try_cast<std::string>())
		{
			if (fieldInfo && !fieldInfo->dragDropPayload.empty())
			{
				std::string displayName = s->empty()
					? "None"
					: std::filesystem::path(*s).stem().string();

				ImGui::InputText(label.c_str(), displayName.data(),
					displayName.size() + 1, ImGuiInputTextFlags_ReadOnly);

				if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
				{
					if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload(fieldInfo->dragDropPayload.c_str()))
					{
						std::string newPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));
						metaData.set(instance, newPath);
					}
					ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
				}
			}
			else if (fieldInfo && fieldInfo->readOnly)
			{
				ImGui::Text("%s: %s", label.c_str(), s->c_str());
			}
			else
			{
				char buf[256];
				strncpy_s(buf, s->c_str(), sizeof(buf) - 1);
				if (ImGui::InputText(label.c_str(), buf, sizeof(buf)))
				{
					metaData.set(instance, std::string(buf));
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			ImGui::TextDisabled("%s: (unsupported type)", label.c_str());
		}
	}
}

// ’l‚ÌŒ^‚ğ”»•Ê‚·‚éƒwƒ‹ƒp[
namespace
{
	ValueType ResolveValueType(entt::meta_type type)
	{
		if (type == entt::resolve<bool>())              return ValueType::Bool;
		if (type == entt::resolve<int>())               return ValueType::Int;
		if (type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>()) return ValueType::Float2;
		if (type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>()) return ValueType::Float3;
		if (type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>()) return ValueType::Float4;
		return ValueType::Float;
	}

	// leafŒ^‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
	bool IsLeafType(entt::meta_type type)
	{
		return type == entt::resolve<bool>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<int>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<float>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<std::string>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT2>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT3>()
			|| type == entt::resolve<DirectX::XMFLOAT4>();
	}
}

// “o˜^Ï‚İ‚ÌƒtƒB[ƒhˆê——‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
const std::vector<FieldEntry>& MetaRegistry::GetAllFields()
{
	static std::vector<FieldEntry> fieldEntries;

	if (fieldEntries.empty())
	{
		for (auto&& [id, metaType] : entt::resolve())
		{
			TypeInfo* typeInfo = metaType.custom();

			if (!typeInfo) continue;

			std::string category = typeInfo->category;
			std::string componentName = std::string(metaType.name());

			if (componentName.empty()) continue;

			for (auto&& [fieldId, metaData] : metaType.data())
			{
				FieldInfo* fieldInfo = metaData.custom();
				std::string label = fieldInfo ? fieldInfo->label : std::string(metaData.name());

				entt::meta_type fieldType = metaData.type();

				if (!IsLeafType(fieldType) && fieldType && fieldType.data().begin() != fieldType.data().end())
				{
					// ƒlƒXƒg‚µ‚½ƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒhF“à‘¤‚ÌŒ^‚ª‚ÂƒtƒB[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğA1’iŠK‚¾‚¯Œ@‚è‰º‚°‚Ä’Ç‰Á‚·‚é
					for (auto&& [subFieldId, subMetaData] : fieldType.data())
					{
						FieldInfo* subFieldInfo = subMetaData.custom();
						std::string subLabel = subFieldInfo ? subFieldInfo->label : std::string(subMetaData.name());

						ValueType valueType = ResolveValueType(subMetaData.type());
						std::string combinedLabel = label + "." + subLabel;

						fieldEntries.push_back(FieldEntry{
							metaType, fieldId, fieldType, subFieldId, componentName, category, combinedLabel, valueType
							});
					}
					continue;
				}

				ValueType valueType = ResolveValueType(fieldType);

				fieldEntries.push_back(FieldEntry{
					metaType, fieldId, entt::meta_type{}, 0, componentName, category, label, valueType
					});
			}
		}
	}

	return fieldEntries;
}

// Float2/3/4Œ^‚ÌA¬•ª”‚ğ•Ô‚·(‚»‚êˆÈŠO‚Í0)
int MetaRegistry::GetComponentCount(ValueType type)
{
	switch (type)
	{
	case ValueType::Float2: return 2;
	case ValueType::Float3: return 3;
	case ValueType::Float4: return 4;
	default: return 0;
	}
}

// ƒgƒ‰ƒ“ƒXƒtƒH[ƒ€ƒRƒ“ƒ|[ƒlƒ“ƒg‚ğ“o˜^
void MetaRegistry::RegisterTransformComponent()
{
	entt::meta_factory<TransformComponent>{}
	.type("TransformComponent")
		.custom<TypeInfo>(TypeInfo{ "Transform" })
		.data<&TransformComponent::position>("position"_hs)
		.custom<FieldInfo>(FieldInfo{ "Position", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f })
		.data<&TransformComponent::rotation>("rotation"_hs)
		.custom<FieldInfo>(FieldInfo{ "Rotation", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.01f })
		.data<&TransformComponent::scale>("scale"_hs)
		.custom<FieldInfo>(FieldInfo{ "Scale", 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.01f });
}

#include "ProjectWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>

#include "Engine/ECS/Components/TransformComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/MeshComponent.h"

#include <fstream>
#include <WICTextureLoader.h>
#include <json.hpp>

#include "tracy/Tracy.hpp"

void ProjectWindow::Initialize(const std::string& rootPath)
{
	this->rootPath = rootPath;
	// ‰Šú‘I‘ğƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Íƒ‹[ƒg
	itemState.selectedPath = rootPath;
}

// ƒAƒCƒRƒ““Ç‚İ‚İ
void ProjectWindow::LoadIcons(ID3D11Device* device)
{
	// Data/Sprite/System/folder.png ‚ğ“Ç‚İ‚Ş
	DirectX::CreateWICTextureFromFile(
		device,
		L"SystemData/Sprite/folder.png",
		nullptr,
		modelIconSRV.GetAddressOf()
	);
}

void ProjectWindow::Draw(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context, bool asDrawer)
{
	ZoneScoped;

	ImGui::Begin("Project");

	if (ImGui::BeginTable("ProjectLayout", 2, 
		ImGuiTableFlags_Resizable |
		ImGuiTableFlags_BordersInnerV))
	{
		// ¶‘¤‚Ì‰Šú•‚ğİ’è
		ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Folders", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthFixed, 200.0f);
		ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Files", ImGuiTableColumnFlags_WidthStretch);

		ImGui::TableNextRow();

		// ¶‘¤FƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[
		ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
		ImGui::BeginChild("FolderTree", ImVec2(0, 0), false);
		DrawFolderTree(rootPath);
		ImGui::EndChild();

		// ‰E‘¤Fƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ƒOƒŠƒbƒh
		ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
		ImGui::BeginChild("FileGrid", ImVec2(0, 0), false);
		DrawFileGrid(registry, context);
		ImGui::EndChild();

		ImGui::EndTable();
	}

	DrawDeleteConfirmPopup();

	ImGui::End();
}

// d•¡‚µ‚È‚¢–¼‘O‚Åì¬
std::string ProjectWindow::GenerateUniqueName(const std::filesystem::path& parentDir, const std::string& baseName) 
{
	std::filesystem::path candidate = parentDir / baseName;
	if (!std::filesystem::exists(candidate))
	{
		return baseName;
	}

	// Šg’£q‚Ìè‘O‚É˜A”Ô‚ğ‹²‚Ş
	std::filesystem::path basePath(baseName);
	std::string stem = basePath.stem().string();
	std::string ext = basePath.extension().string();

	int suffix = 2;
	while (true)
	{
		std::string name = stem + " (" + std::to_string(suffix) + ")" + ext;
		if (!std::filesystem::exists(parentDir / name))
		{
			return name;
		}
		++suffix;
	}
}

// ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_ƒcƒŠ[
void ProjectWindow::DrawFolderTree(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	if (!std::filesystem::is_directory(path)) return;

	std::string folderName = path.filename().string();
	bool isRenamingThis = (itemState.mode == ProjectItemMode::Renaming && itemState.targetPath == path);

	// ƒTƒuƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ª‚ ‚é‚©Šm”F
	bool hasSubFolder = false;
	for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(path))
	{
		if (entry.is_directory())
		{
			hasSubFolder = true;
			break;
		}
	}

	// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒcƒŠ[s‚Ì‘ã‚í‚è‚É“ü—Í—“‚ğ•\¦‚·‚é
	if (isRenamingThis)
	{
		ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(160.0f);
		ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();

		bool confirmedByEnter = ImGui::InputText("##rename", itemState.editBuffer, sizeof(itemState.editBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
		bool confirmedByBlur = ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && !confirmedByEnter;

		if (confirmedByEnter || confirmedByBlur)
		{
			CommitRename();
		}
		// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒTƒuƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ÌÄ‹A•`‰æ‚ğs‚í‚È‚¢
		return;
	}

	ImGuiTreeNodeFlags flags = ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_SpanAvailWidth;

	// ‘I‘ğ’†ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ğƒnƒCƒ‰ƒCƒg
	if (itemState.selectedPath == path)
	{
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_Selected;
	}

	// Å‰º‘w‚Ìê‡‚Í—tƒm[ƒh‚Æ‚µ‚Ä•\¦
	if (!hasSubFolder)
	{
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_Leaf;
		flags |= ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_NoTreePushOnOpen;
	}

	bool open = ImGui::TreeNodeEx(folderName.c_str(), flags);

	// ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Å‘I‘ğ
	if (ImGui::IsItemClicked())
	{
		itemState.selectedPath = path;
	}

	// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒgƒƒjƒ…[i‚±‚ÌƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Ì’†‚Éì‚éj
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem(path.string().c_str()))
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu(u8"V‹Kì¬"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_"))
			{
				std::string newName = GenerateUniqueName(path, "New Folder");
				std::filesystem::create_directory(path / newName);
			}

			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		
		ImGui::Separator();
		if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏX"))
		{
			BeginRename(path);
		}

		// ƒ‹[ƒgƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚Ííœ‚³‚¹‚È‚¢
		if (path != rootPath)
		{
			ImGui::Separator();
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"íœ"))
			{
				itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete;
				itemState.targetPath = path;
			}
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	// Å‰º‘w‚ÍTreePop‚ª•s—v
	if (open && hasSubFolder)
	{
		for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(path))
		{
			if (entry.is_directory())
			{
				DrawFolderTree(entry.path());
			}
		}
		ImGui::TreePop();
	}
}
// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹ˆê——
void ProjectWindow::DrawFileGrid(entt::registry& registry, EditorContext& context)
{
	if (itemState.selectedPath.empty()) return;
	if (!std::filesystem::exists(itemState.selectedPath)) return;

	// ƒAƒCƒRƒ“ƒTƒCƒY‚Æ—]”’
	const float iconSize = 64.0f;
	const float padding = 16.0f;
	const float cellSize = iconSize + padding;

	// ƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE•‚©‚ç—ñ”‚ğŒvZ
	float windowWidth = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
	int   columns = (std::max)(1, (int)(windowWidth / cellSize));

	int index = 0;

	//for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(itemState.selectedPath))

	//	// —ñ‚ÌÜ‚è•Ô‚µ
	//	if (index % columns != 0)
	//	{
	//		ImGui::SameLine();
	//	}

	//	// ƒOƒ‹[ƒv‰»‚µ‚ÄƒAƒCƒRƒ“{–¼‘O‚ğ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚é
	//	ImGui::BeginGroup();

	//	ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

	//	// ƒAƒCƒRƒ“•\¦
	//	if (modelIconSRV)
	//	{
	//		ImGui::Image(
	//			(ImTextureID)modelIconSRV.Get(),
	//			ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize)
	//		);
	//	}
	//	else
	//	{
	//		ImGui::GetWindowDrawList()->AddRectFilled(
	//			pos,
	//			ImVec2(pos.x + iconSize, pos.y + iconSize),
	//			IM_COL32(80, 120, 200, 255),
	//			8.0f
	//		);
	//		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));
	//	}

	//	// ‰æ‘œ‚Ìã‚É“§–¾ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğd‚Ë‚Äƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO”»’è
	//	ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(pos);
	//	ImGui::PushID(index);
	//	ImGui::InvisibleButton("##icon", ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));

	//	// ƒ_ƒuƒ‹ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅStudio‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒg
	//	if (ImGui::IsItemHovered() && ImGui::IsMouseDoubleClicked(0))
	//	{
	//		context.studioRequest.pending = true;
	//		context.studioRequest.assetPath = modelPath;
	//	}

	//	// ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒOŠJn
	//	if (ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
	//	{
	//		// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ƒpƒX‚ğƒyƒCƒ[ƒh‚Æ‚µ‚Ä“n‚·
	//		ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
	//			"MODEL_PATH",
	//			modelPath.c_str(),
	//			modelPath.size() + 1
	//		);
	//		if (modelIconSRV)
	//		{
	//			ImGui::Image(
	//				(ImTextureID)modelIconSRV.Get(),
	//				ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize)
	//			);
	//		}
	//		ImGui::Text(filename.c_str());
	//		ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
	//	}

	//	ImGui::PopID();

	//	// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼i’·‚¯‚ê‚ÎÈ—ªj
	//	std::string displayName = filename;
	//	if (displayName.size() > 10)
	//		displayName = displayName.substr(0, 9) + "...";
	//	ImGui::TextUnformatted(displayName.c_str());

	//	ImGui::EndGroup();
	//	index++;
	//}

	for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(itemState.selectedPath))
	{
		if (!entry.is_regular_file()) continue;

		bool isModel = IsModelFile(entry.path());
		bool isAnimGraph = IsAnimGraphFile(entry.path());
		bool isSequencer = IsSequencerFile(entry.path());
		bool isAnimSpeed = IsAnimSpeedFile(entry.path());
		if (!isModel && !isAnimGraph && !isSequencer && !isAnimSpeed) continue;

		std::string filename = entry.path().stem().string();
		std::string modelPath = entry.path().string();
		std::replace(modelPath.begin(), modelPath.end(), '\\', '/');

		// —ñ‚ÌÜ‚è•Ô‚µ
		if (index % columns != 0)
		{
			ImGui::SameLine();
		}

		// ƒOƒ‹[ƒv‰»‚µ‚ÄƒAƒCƒRƒ“{–¼‘O‚ğ‚Ü‚Æ‚ß‚é
		ImGui::BeginGroup();

		ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

		// ƒAƒCƒRƒ“•\¦iƒ‚ƒfƒ‹—pƒAƒCƒRƒ“‚ª–³‚¢/ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ˆÈŠO‚ÍF•t‚«‹éŒ`‚Å‘ã—pj
		if (isModel && modelIconSRV)
		{
			ImGui::Image(
				(ImTextureID)modelIconSRV.Get(),
				ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize)
			);
		}
		else
		{
			ImU32 color = isAnimGraph ? IM_COL32(200, 130, 60, 255)
				: isSequencer ? IM_COL32(120, 200, 130, 255)
				: isAnimSpeed ? IM_COL32(200, 90, 160, 255)
				: IM_COL32(80, 120, 200, 255);
			ImGui::GetWindowDrawList()->AddRectFilled(
				pos,
				ImVec2(pos.x + iconSize, pos.y + iconSize),
				color,
				8.0f
			);
			ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));
		}

		// ‰æ‘œ‚Ìã‚É“§–¾ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğd‚Ë‚Äƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO‚ğæ‚é
		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(pos);
		ImGui::PushID(index);
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("##icon", ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize));

		// ƒ_ƒuƒ‹ƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅStudio‚ğŠJ‚­ƒŠƒNƒGƒXƒgiƒ‚ƒfƒ‹^ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒOƒ‰ƒtj
		if (ImGui::IsItemHovered() && ImGui::IsMouseDoubleClicked(0))
		{
			if (isModel)
			{
				context.studioRequest.pending = true;
				context.studioRequest.assetPath = modelPath;
				context.studioRequest.graphPath = "";
			}
		}

		// ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒOŠJniƒ‚ƒfƒ‹^ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒOƒ‰ƒt/ƒV[ƒPƒ“ƒT[/ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‘¬“xj
		if ((isModel || isAnimGraph || isSequencer || isAnimSpeed) && ImGui::BeginDragDropSource(ImGuiDragDropFlags_SourceAllowNullID))
		{
			if (isModel)
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"MODEL_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}
			else if (isAnimGraph)
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"ANIMGRAPH_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}
			else if (isSequencer)
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"SEQUENCER_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}
			else // isAnimSpeed
			{
				ImGui::SetDragDropPayload(
					"ANIMSPEED_PATH",
					modelPath.c_str(),
					modelPath.size() + 1
				);
			}

			if (isModel && modelIconSRV)
			{
				ImGui::Image(
					(ImTextureID)modelIconSRV.Get(),
					ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize)
				);
			}
			ImGui::Text(filename.c_str());
			ImGui::EndDragDropSource();
		}

		// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚Åu–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏXvuíœv
		if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextItem("##fileContext"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"–¼‘O‚Ì•ÏX"))
			{
				BeginRename(entry.path());
			}
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"íœ"))
			{
				itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete;
				itemState.targetPath = entry.path();
			}
			ImGui::EndPopup();
		}

		ImGui::PopID();

		// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼•\¦ or ƒŠƒl[ƒ€’†‚ÍƒCƒ“ƒ‰ƒCƒ““ü—Í—“
		bool isRenamingThis = (itemState.mode == ProjectItemMode::Renaming && itemState.targetPath == entry.path());
		if (isRenamingThis)
		{
			std::string extension = entry.path().extension().string();

			// “ü—Í—“‚ÍƒAƒCƒRƒ“•‚¢‚Á‚Ï‚¢‚Ég‚¤iŠg’£q‚ÍŸ‚Ìs‚É•\¦‚·‚é‚½‚ß•‚ğ•ª‚¯‚é•K—v‚ª–³‚¢j
			ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(iconSize + padding);
			ImGui::SetKeyboardFocusHere();

			bool confirmedByEnter = ImGui::InputText("##rename", itemState.editBuffer, sizeof(itemState.editBuffer), ImGuiInputTextFlags_EnterReturnsTrue);
			bool confirmedByBlur = ImGui::IsItemDeactivated() && !confirmedByEnter;

			// Šg’£q‚Í“ü—Í—“‚Ì‰º‚Ìs‚ÉŒÅ’è•\¦i•ÒW•s‰Âj
			if (!extension.empty())
			{
				ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", extension.c_str());
			}

			if (confirmedByEnter || confirmedByBlur)
			{
				CommitRename();
			}
		}
		else
		{
			std::string displayName = filename;
			if (displayName.size() > 10)
				displayName = displayName.substr(0, 9) + "...";
			ImGui::TextUnformatted(displayName.c_str());
		}

		ImGui::EndGroup();
		index++;
	}

	// ‹ó”’•”•ª‚Ì‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN‚ÅV‹Kì¬ƒƒjƒ…[
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupContextWindow("FileGridContext", ImGuiPopupFlags_MouseButtonRight | ImGuiPopupFlags_NoOpenOverExistingPopup))
	{
		if (ImGui::BeginMenu(u8"V‹Kì¬"))
		{
			if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_"))
			{
				std::string newName = GenerateUniqueName(itemState.selectedPath, "New Folder");
				std::filesystem::create_directory(itemState.selectedPath / newName);
			}
			ImGui::EndMenu();
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
}

// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsModelFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();

	// ¬•¶š‚É•ÏŠ·‚µ‚Ä”äŠr
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".gltf" || ext == ".glb";
}
// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒOƒ‰ƒtƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsAnimGraphFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".animgraph";
}
// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒV[ƒPƒ“ƒT[ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsSequencerFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".sequencer";
}

// ƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚ªƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‘¬“xƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹‚©”»’è
bool ProjectWindow::IsAnimSpeedFile(const std::filesystem::path& path)
{
	std::string ext = path.extension().string();
	std::transform(ext.begin(), ext.end(), ext.begin(), ::tolower);

	return ext == ".animspeed";
}


// íœŠm”Fƒ|ƒbƒv
void ProjectWindow::DrawDeleteConfirmPopup()
{
	if (itemState.mode != ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete) return;

	ImGui::OpenPopup(u8"íœ‚ÌŠm”F");

	bool open = true;
	if (ImGui::BeginPopupModal(u8"íœ‚ÌŠm”F", &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize))
	{
		std::string name = itemState.targetPath.filename().string();
		ImGui::Text(u8"u%sv‚ğíœ‚µ‚Ü‚·‚©H", name.c_str());
		ImGui::Spacing();

		if (ImGui::Button(u8"íœ", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			std::error_code ec;
			std::filesystem::remove_all(itemState.targetPath, ec);

			if (itemState.selectedPath == itemState.targetPath)
			{
				itemState.selectedPath = rootPath;
			}

			itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
			itemState.targetPath.clear();
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}

		ImGui::SameLine();

		if (ImGui::Button(u8"ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹", ImVec2(120, 0)))
		{
			itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
			itemState.targetPath.clear();
			ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
		}

		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}

	// ~ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚Å•Â‚¶‚ç‚ê‚½ê‡‚àó‘Ô‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
	if (!open && itemState.mode == ProjectItemMode::ConfirmingDelete)
	{
		itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
		itemState.targetPath.clear();
	}
}

// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€ŠJn
void ProjectWindow::BeginRename(const std::filesystem::path& target)
{
	itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::Renaming;
	itemState.targetPath = target;

	std::string currentStem = target.stem().string();
	strncpy_s(itemState.editBuffer, currentStem.c_str(), sizeof(itemState.editBuffer) - 1);
}
// ƒŠƒl[ƒ€Šm’è
void ProjectWindow::CommitRename()
{
	std::string newStem = itemState.editBuffer;
	std::string extension = itemState.targetPath.extension().string(); // Šg’£q‚ÍŒÅ’èi•ÏX•s‰Âj
	std::string newName = newStem + extension;

	// ‹ó—“‚â•ÏX‚È‚µ‚Ìê‡‚Í‰½‚à‚¹‚¸I—¹
	if (!newStem.empty() && newName != itemState.targetPath.filename().string())
	{
		std::filesystem::path newPath = itemState.targetPath.parent_path() / newName;

		std::error_code ec;
		std::filesystem::rename(itemState.targetPath, newPath, ec);

		if (!ec)
		{
			// ‘I‘ğ’†ƒpƒX‚ªƒŠƒl[ƒ€‘ÎÛ‚¾‚Á‚½ê‡AV‚µ‚¢ƒpƒX‚É’Ç]‚³‚¹‚é
			if (itemState.selectedPath == itemState.targetPath)
			{
				itemState.selectedPath = newPath;
			}
		}
	}

	itemState.mode = ProjectItemMode::None;
	itemState.targetPath.clear();
}

    NodeEditorCanvas& operator=(const NodeEditorCanvas&) = delete;

    void End();

private:

===== NodeEditorCanvas.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\NodeEditor\NodeEditorCanvas.cpp 
#include "NodeEditorCanvas.h"

NodeEditorCanvas::~NodeEditorCanvas()
{
    if (editorContext)
    {
        ed::DestroyEditor(editorContext);
        editorContext = nullptr;
    }
}

void NodeEditorCanvas::Initialize()
{
    ed::Config config;
    config.SettingsFile = nullptr;
    editorContext = ed::CreateEditor(&config);
}

void NodeEditorCanvas::Begin(const char* canvasId)
{
    ed::SetCurrentEditor(editorContext);
    ed::Begin(canvasId);
}

void NodeEditorCanvas::End()
{
    ed::End();
    ed::SetCurrentEditor(nullptr);
}

#pragma once
#include "Engine/Data/AnimatorGraphData.h"

#include <imgui.h>
#include <functional>
#include <imgui_node_editor.h>

namespace ed = ax::NodeEditor;

class StudioConditionGraph
{
public:
	StudioConditionGraph() = default;
	~StudioConditionGraph() = default;

	void Initialize();

	// •ÒW‘ÎÛ‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt‚ğİ’è‚·‚é
	void SetTarget(ConditionGraph* condition);

	// ImGui•`‰æ
	void DrawGUI();
	// ‰E‘¤ƒƒjƒ…[
	void DrawInspector();

private:
	// == “à•”ó‘Ô == //
	NodeEditorCanvas canvas;        // ‚±‚ÌƒNƒ‰ƒXê—p‚Ìƒm[ƒhƒGƒfƒBƒ^ƒRƒ“ƒeƒLƒXƒg
	ConditionGraph* targetCondition = nullptr; // Œ»İ•ÒW’†‚ÌğŒƒOƒ‰ƒt
	ImVec2 pendingCreatePos{};
	bool needsApplyPositions = false;

	ConditionVariableNode* selectedVariable = nullptr;
	ConditionGetterNode* selectedGetter = nullptr;
	ConditionOperatorNode* selectedOperator = nullptr;
	ConditionLogicNode* selectedLogic = nullptr;
	ConditionLink* selectedConditionLink = nullptr;

	// == Šeƒm[ƒh•`‰æ == //
	void DrawReturnNode();    // ƒŠƒ^[ƒ“ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
	void DrawVariableNodes(); // •Ï”ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
	void DrawOperatorNodes(); // ”äŠr‰‰Zƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
	void DrawLogicNodes();    // ˜_—‰‰Zƒm[ƒh•`‰æ
	void DrawGetterNodes();	  // ƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒh•`‰æ

	void DrawUnassignedGetterNode(ConditionGetterNode& g); // –¢İ’èƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒh
	void DrawAssignedGetterNode(ConditionGetterNode& g);   // İ’èÏ‚İƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒh

	// == ƒsƒ“Ú‘± == //
	enum class PinRole // ƒsƒ“‚Ì–ğŠ„
	{
		VariableIn, VariableOut,
		OperatorA, OperatorB, OperatorOut,
		LogicA, LogicB, LogicOut,
		GetterOut,
		ReturnIn,
		Unknown
	};
	enum class NodeKind { Variable, Getter, Operator, Logic, Return, Unknown }; // ƒm[ƒh‚Ìí—Ş

	// ƒsƒ“IDŒvZ—p‚Ìƒx[ƒXƒIƒtƒZƒbƒg
	static constexpr int64_t VariableOffset = 1000000;
	static constexpr int64_t OperatorOffset = 2000000;
	static constexpr int64_t GetterOffset = 3000000;
	static constexpr int64_t ReturnOffset = 4000000;
	static constexpr int64_t LogicOffset = 5000000;

	static void ResolvePinRole(int64_t rawPinId, PinRole& outRole, int& outNodeId); // ¶‚Ìƒsƒ“ID(int64_t)‚©‚çA–ğŠ„‚Æƒm[ƒhID‚ğ‹tZ‚·‚é
	NodeKind GetNodeKind(int id) const; // ¶‚Ìƒm[ƒhID(int)‚©‚çA‚±‚ÌLink“à‚Å‚Ç‚Ìí—Ş‚Ìƒm[ƒh‚©‚ğ’²‚×‚é

	void DrawLinkCreation();    // ƒsƒ“Ú‘±(ƒhƒ‰ƒbƒO)‚Ìˆ—
	void DrawConditionLinks();  // Ú‘±Ï‚İConditionLink‚Ì•`‰æ

	// fromId/toId‚©‚çed::Link‚ğˆø‚­‚½‚ß‚ÌAÀÛ‚Ìƒsƒ“ID‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
	ed::PinId GetOutputPinIdOf(int nodeId, NodeKind kind) const;
	ed::PinId GetInputPinIdOf(int nodeId, NodeKind kind, bool toPinB) const;

	ValueType GetNodeValueType(NodeKind kind, int nodeId) const;        // ƒm[ƒh‚Ì’l‚ÌŒ^‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
	ValueType GetOutputValueType(PinRole outRole, int outNodeId) const; // o—Íƒsƒ“‚ÌÀ¿“I‚ÈŒ^‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
	static bool IsOrderCompareOp(CompareOp op);                         // ‘å¬Œn‰‰Zq‚©‚ğ”»’è‚·‚é

	// == ƒm[ƒh‚Ìì¬ == //		
	void DrawCreateNodeMenu();               // ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒNƒƒjƒ…[
	void CreateVariableNode(ValueType type); // •Ï”ƒm[ƒhì¬
	void CreateOperatorNode(CompareOp op);   // ”äŠr‰‰Zƒm[ƒhì¬
	void CreateLogicNode(LogicOp op);        // ˜_—‰‰Zƒm[ƒhì¬
	void CreateGetterNode();                 // ƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒhì¬

	// == ƒm[ƒh‚Ìíœ == //
	void DrawDeleteButton(const char* label, int targetId, const std::function<void(int)>& onDelete);
	void DeleteVariableNode(int id);  // •Ï”ƒm[ƒhíœ
	void DeleteGetterNode(int id);    // ƒQƒbƒ^[ƒm[ƒhíœ
	void DeleteOperatorNode(int id);  // ”äŠr‰‰Zƒm[ƒhíœ
	void DeleteLogicNode(int id);     // ˜_—‰‰Zƒm[ƒhíœ
	void DeleteConditionLink(int id); // ConditionLinkíœ

	// == ‘I‘ğó‘Ô == //
	void UpdateSelection();
	ConditionLink* GetSelectedConditionLink();

	// == •\¦—pƒwƒ‹ƒp[ == //
	static void CenterNextItem(float totalWidth, float itemWidth); // ƒm[ƒh“à‚Å’†‰›Šñ‚¹‚É‚·‚é
	static ImColor GetValueTypeColor(ValueType type);	           // ’l‚ÌŒ^‚²‚Æ‚Ì•\¦F‚ğ•Ô‚·
	static const char* GetValueTypeName(ValueType type);           // ’l‚ÌŒ^–¼‚ğ•¶š‚É‚µ‚Ä•Ô‚·
	static const char* GetCompareOpSymbol(CompareOp op);	       // ”äŠr‰‰Zq‚Ì•\¦—p‹L†
	static CompareOp InvertCompareOp(CompareOp op);                // ”äŠr‰‰Zq‚ÌŒü‚«‚ğ”½“]‚·‚é
	static const char* GetLogicOpSymbol(LogicOp op);               // ˜_—‰‰Zq‚Ì•\¦—p‹L†

	// == ƒsƒ“ID == //
	static ed::PinId GetVariableInPinId(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(VariableOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 2); }
	static ed::PinId GetVariableOutPinId(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(VariableOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 2 + 1); }
	static ed::PinId GetOperatorPinA(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(OperatorOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 3); }
	static ed::PinId GetOperatorPinB(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(OperatorOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 3 + 1); }
	static ed::PinId GetOperatorOutPinId(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(OperatorOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 3 + 2); }
	static ed::PinId GetGetterOutPinId(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(GetterOffset + (int64_t)nodeId); }
	static ed::PinId GetReturnInPinId(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(ReturnOffset + (int64_t)nodeId); }
	static ed::PinId GetLogicPinA(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(LogicOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 3); }
	static ed::PinId GetLogicPinB(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(LogicOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 3 + 1); }
	static ed::PinId GetLogicOutPinId(int nodeId) { return ed::PinId(LogicOffset + (int64_t)nodeId * 3 + 2); }
};

    // •`‰æ

    void End();

    Microsoft::WRL::ComPtr<ID3D11DepthStencilView>   dsv;

    StudioCamera camera;

    // ƒAƒ^ƒbƒ`ƒƒ“ƒgŠÔ‹¤—Lƒf[ƒ^
