// ‰æ–Ê‘JˆÚ‚ª—v‹‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚ê‚Îæ‚èo‚·
NavigationRequest StudioParentStateGraph::ConsumeNavigationRequest(AnimLink*& outLink, ParentState*& outState)
{
	NavigationRequest request = pendingNavigation;
	outLink = (request == NavigationRequest::EditCondition) ? selectedLink : nullptr;
	outState = (request == NavigationRequest::EditChildGraph) ? selectedState : nullptr;

	pendingNavigation = NavigationRequest::None;
	return request;
}


// State•`‰æ
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawStates()
{
	stateScreenRects.clear();

	for (auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states) 
	{
		ed::NodeId nodeId(state.id);

		if (needsApplyPositions)
		{
			ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(state.posX, state.posY));
		}

		PushStateStyle(state);

		ed::BeginNode(nodeId);

		float nodeWidth = (state.type == ParentStateType::Entry) ? 100.0f : ImGui::CalcTextSize("New State").x;
		float textWidth = ImGui::CalcTextSize(state.name.c_str()).x;

		CenterNextItem(nodeWidth, textWidth);
		ImGui::Text("%s", state.name.c_str());

		ImGui::SameLine(nodeWidth);
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));

		ed::EndNode();

		stateScreenRects[state.id] = { ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), ImGui::GetItemRectMax() };

		PopStateStyle(state);

		ImVec2 currentPos = ed::GetNodePosition(nodeId);
		state.posX = currentPos.x;
		state.posY = currentPos.y;
	}
	needsApplyPositions = false;
}
// State‚Ìí—Ş‚É‰‚¶‚½Œ©‚½–Ú‚ÌƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹‚ğ“K—p‚·‚é
void StudioParentStateGraph::PushStateStyle(const ParentState& state)
{
	switch (state.type)
	{
	case ParentStateType::Entry:
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(46, 125, 50, 255));
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, ImColor(27, 94, 32, 255));
		ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_NodeRounding, 4.0f);
		break;

	case ParentStateType::State:
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(state.color[0], state.color[1], state.color[2], 1.0f));
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, ImColor(state.color[0] * 0.6f, state.color[1] * 0.6f, state.color[2] * 0.6f, 1.0f));
		ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_NodeRounding, 4.0f);
		break;

	default:
		break;
	}
}
// PushStateStyle‚Å“K—p‚µ‚½ƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
void StudioParentStateGraph::PopStateStyle(const ParentState& state)
{
	switch (state.type)
	{
	case ParentStateType::Entry:
		ed::PopStyleVar(1);
		ed::PopStyleColor(2);
		break;

	case ParentStateType::State:
		ed::PopStyleVar(1);
		ed::PopStyleColor(2);
		break;

	default:
		break;
	}
}


// Link•`‰æ
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawLinks()
{
	const float arrowTrim = 10.0f; // –îˆó‚Ì’ê•Ó‚ÌˆÊ’u‚Ü‚ÅAü‚ğè‘O‚Éˆø‚­—Ê

	for (const auto& link : graph->parentGraph.stateLinks) 
	{
		ImVec2 startPoint, endPoint;
		CalcLinkPoints(link, startPoint, endPoint);

		if (startPoint.x == 0.0f && startPoint.y == 0.0f &&
			endPoint.x == 0.0f && endPoint.y == 0.0f)
		{
			continue;
		}

		ImVec2 dir(endPoint.x - startPoint.x, endPoint.y - startPoint.y);
		float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
		ImVec2 trimmedEnd = endPoint;
		if (len > arrowTrim)
		{
			trimmedEnd.x = endPoint.x - dir.x / len * arrowTrim;
			trimmedEnd.y = endPoint.y - dir.y / len * arrowTrim;
		}

		DrawLinkPins(link, startPoint, trimmedEnd);

		ImDrawList* drawList = ed::GetNodeBackgroundDrawList(ed::NodeId(link.fromId));
		if (drawList)
		{
			const ImU32 linkColor = IM_COL32(210, 210, 210, 255);
			DrawArrowHead(drawList, startPoint, endPoint, linkColor);
		}
	}
}
// ƒŠƒ“ƒN1–{•ª‚ÌÚ‘±“_‚ğŒvZ‚·‚é
void StudioParentStateGraph::CalcLinkPoints(const AnimLink& link, ImVec2& outStart, ImVec2& outEnd)
{
	auto fromIt = stateScreenRects.find(link.fromId);
	auto toIt = stateScreenRects.find(link.toId);
	if (fromIt == stateScreenRects.end() || toIt == stateScreenRects.end())
	{
		outStart = ImVec2();
		outEnd = ImVec2();
		return;
	}

	const ImVec2& fromMin = fromIt->second.first;
	const ImVec2& fromMax = fromIt->second.second;
	const ImVec2& toMin = toIt->second.first;
	const ImVec2& toMax = toIt->second.second;

	ImVec2 fromCenter((fromMin.x + fromMax.x) * 0.5f, (fromMin.y + fromMax.y) * 0.5f);
	ImVec2 toCenter((toMin.x + toMax.x) * 0.5f, (toMin.y + toMax.y) * 0.5f);

	ImVec2 startPoint = GetEdgePoint(fromMin, fromMax, toCenter);
	ImVec2 endPoint = GetEdgePoint(toMin, toMax, fromCenter);

	if (HasReverseLink(link.fromId, link.toId))
	{
		bool fromIsSmaller = link.fromId < link.toId;
		const ImVec2& baseCenter = fromIsSmaller ? fromCenter : toCenter;
		const ImVec2& otherCenter = fromIsSmaller ? toCenter : fromCenter;

		ImVec2 dir(otherCenter.x - baseCenter.x, otherCenter.y - baseCenter.y);
		float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
		if (len > 0.0001f)
		{
			ImVec2 perp(-dir.y / len, dir.x / len);

			const float offsetAmount = 8.0f;
			float sign = fromIsSmaller ? 1.0f : -1.0f;

			ImVec2 offset(perp.x * offsetAmount * sign, perp.y * offsetAmount * sign);

			startPoint.x += offset.x;
			startPoint.y += offset.y;
			endPoint.x += offset.x;
			endPoint.y += offset.y;
		}
	}

	outStart = startPoint;
	outEnd = endPoint;
}
// ŒvZÏ‚İ‚ÌÀ•W‚Éƒsƒ“‚ğ”z’u‚·‚é
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawLinkPins(const AnimLink& link, const ImVec2& start, const ImVec2& end)
{
	const int64_t idOffset = 10000000;
	ed::NodeId outNodeId(idOffset + (int64_t)link.id * 2);
	ed::NodeId inNodeId(idOffset + (int64_t)link.id * 2 + 1);

	ed::PinId outPinId = GetPinId(link.id, true);
	ed::PinId inPinId = GetPinId(link.id, false);

	ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_NodePadding, ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0));
	ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(0, 0, 0, 0));
	ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, ImColor(0, 0, 0, 0));

	ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_LinkStrength, 0.0f);

	ed::SetNodePosition(outNodeId, start);
	ed::BeginNode(outNodeId);
	ed::BeginPin(outPinId, ed::PinKind::Output);
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(1, 1));
	ed::EndPin();
	ed::EndNode();

	ed::SetNodePosition(inNodeId, end);
	ed::BeginNode(inNodeId);
	ed::BeginPin(inPinId, ed::PinKind::Input);
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(1, 1));
	ed::EndPin();
	ed::EndNode();

	ed::PopStyleVar(2);
	ed::PopStyleColor(2);

	ed::Link(ed::LinkId(link.id), outPinId, inPinId);
}
// •Ó‚©‚çƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğL‚Î‚·
ImVec2 StudioParentStateGraph::GetEdgePoint(const ImVec2& rectMin, const ImVec2& rectMax, const ImVec2& target)
{
	ImVec2 center((rectMin.x + rectMax.x) * 0.5f, (rectMin.y + rectMax.y) * 0.5f);
	float halfW = (rectMax.x - rectMin.x) * 0.5f;
	float halfH = (rectMax.y - rectMin.y) * 0.5f;

	ImVec2 dir(target.x - center.x, target.y - center.y);
	if (dir.x == 0.0f && dir.y == 0.0f) return center;

	float scaleX = (dir.x != 0.0f) ? halfW / fabsf(dir.x) : FLT_MAX;
	float scaleY = (dir.y != 0.0f) ? halfH / fabsf(dir.y) : FLT_MAX;
	float scale = (scaleX < scaleY) ? scaleX : scaleY;

	return ImVec2(center.x + dir.x * scale, center.y + dir.y * scale);
}
// ‘o•ûŒü‚ÌLink‚ª‚ ‚é‚©Šm”F‚·‚é
bool StudioParentStateGraph::HasReverseLink(int fromId, int toId) const
{
	for (const auto& link : graph->parentGraph.stateLinks) 
	{
		if (link.fromId == toId && link.toId == fromId)
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	return false;
}
// –îˆó•`‰æ
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawArrowHead(ImDrawList* drawList, const ImVec2& start, const ImVec2& end, ImU32 color)
{
	ImVec2 dir(end.x - start.x, end.y - start.y);
	float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
	if (len < 0.0001f) return;

	dir.x /= len;
	dir.y /= len;

	ImVec2 perp(-dir.y, dir.x);

	const float arrowLength = 18.0f;
	const float arrowWidth = 13.0f;

	ImVec2 tip = end;
	ImVec2 baseCenter(end.x - dir.x * arrowLength, end.y - dir.y * arrowLength);
	ImVec2 baseLeft(baseCenter.x + perp.x * arrowWidth * 0.5f, baseCenter.y + perp.y * arrowWidth * 0.5f);
	ImVec2 baseRight(baseCenter.x - perp.x * arrowWidth * 0.5f, baseCenter.y - perp.y * arrowWidth * 0.5f);

	drawList->AddTriangleFilled(tip, baseLeft, baseRight, color);
}


// ‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒNƒƒjƒ…[
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawCreateStateMenu()
{
	ed::Suspend();
	if (ed::ShowBackgroundContextMenu())
	{
		pendingCreatePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();
		ImGui::OpenPopup("CreateNewState");
	}
	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("CreateNewState"))
	{
		if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"State"))
		{
			ImVec2 canvasPos = ed::ScreenToCanvas(pendingCreatePos);
			int newId = graph->parentGraph.CreateParentState("New State", canvasPos.x, canvasPos.y);
			ed::SetNodePosition(ed::NodeId(newId), canvasPos);
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
	ed::Resume();
}
// Linkì¬‚Ì‹N“_‘I‘ğ(State‰EƒNƒŠƒbƒN)
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawStateContextMenu()
{
	static ed::NodeId contextNodeId;

	ed::Suspend();
	if (ed::ShowNodeContextMenu(&contextNodeId))
	{
		ImGui::OpenPopup("NodeContextMenu");
	}
	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("NodeContextMenu"))
	{
		if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"Link"))
		{
			pendingLinkFromId = (int)contextNodeId.Get();
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
	ed::Resume();
}
// Linkì¬ƒ‚[ƒh’†‚Ì“ü—Íˆ—
void StudioParentStateGraph::UpdateLinkCreation()
{
	if (pendingLinkFromId == -1) return;

	if (ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
	{
		ed::NodeId hovered = ed::GetHoveredNode();

		if (hovered && (int)hovered.Get() != pendingLinkFromId)
		{
			int targetId = (int)hovered.Get();

			bool targetIsEntry = false;
			for (const auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
			{
				if (state.id == targetId && state.type == ParentStateType::Entry)
				{
					targetIsEntry = true;
					break;
				}
			}

			if (!targetIsEntry)
			{
				graph->parentGraph.CreateParentLink(pendingLinkFromId, targetId);
				graph->transitionGraph.CreateLink(pendingLinkFromId, targetId);
			}
		}

		pendingLinkFromId = -1;
	}
}
// Linkì¬’†‚ÌƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[ü•`‰æ
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawPendingLink()
{
	if (pendingLinkFromId == -1) return;

	auto it = stateScreenRects.find(pendingLinkFromId);
	if (it == stateScreenRects.end()) return;

	ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();
	ImVec2 startPoint = GetEdgePoint(it->second.first, it->second.second, mousePos);

	ImDrawList* drawList = ed::GetNodeBackgroundDrawList(ed::NodeId(pendingLinkFromId));
	if (!drawList) return;

	const ImU32 previewColor = IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255);

	drawList->AddLine(startPoint, mousePos, previewColor, 2.0f);
}

// ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚ğXV
void StudioParentStateGraph::UpdateSelection()
{
	selectedState = nullptr;
	selectedLink = nullptr;

	if (ed::GetSelectedObjectCount() == 0) return;

	ed::NodeId selectedNodeId;
	if (ed::GetSelectedNodes(&selectedNodeId, 1) > 0)
	{
		for (auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
		{
			if (state.id == (int)selectedNodeId.Get())
			{
				selectedState = &state;
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	ed::LinkId selectedLinkId;
	if (ed::GetSelectedLinks(&selectedLinkId, 1) > 0)
	{
		for (auto& link : graph->parentGraph.stateLinks)
		{
			if (link.id == (int)selectedLinkId.Get())
			{
				selectedLink = &link;
				return;
			}
		}
	}
}

// State‘I‘ğ‚ÌInspector
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawStateInspector(ParentState* state)
{
	if (state->type == ParentStateType::Entry)
	{
		ImGui::Text("Entry");
		ImGui::TextDisabled("This is a unique node and cannot be edited.");
		return;
	}

	char nameBuf[256];
	strncpy_s(nameBuf, state->name.c_str(), sizeof(nameBuf) - 1);
	if (ImGui::InputText("Name", nameBuf, sizeof(nameBuf)))
	{
		state->name = nameBuf;
	}

	ImGui::ColorEdit3("Color", state->color);

	// qŠK‘w‚ğ•ÒW‚·‚é‰æ–Ê‚Ö
	if (ImGui::Button("Edit Child States", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		pendingNavigation = NavigationRequest::EditChildGraph;
	}

	int stateId = state->id;
	DrawDeleteButton("Delete State", [this, stateId]() { graph->DeleteParentState(stateId); });
}
// Link‘I‘ğ‚ÌInspector
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawLinkInspector(AnimLink* link)
{
	const ParentState* fromState = nullptr;
	const ParentState* toState = nullptr;
	for (const auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
	{
		if (state.id == link->fromId) fromState = &state;
		if (state.id == link->toId)   toState = &state;
	}

	std::string fromName = fromState ? fromState->name : "?";
	std::string toName = toState ? toState->name : "?";

	ImGui::TextWrapped("%s -> %s", fromName.c_str(), toName.c_str());

	ImGui::Checkbox("Blend", &link->blend);
	if (link->blend)
	{
		ImGui::DragFloat("Blend Duration", &link->blendDuration, 0.01f, 0.0f, 5.0f, "%.2f s");
	}

	if (ImGui::Button("Edit Condition", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		pendingNavigation = NavigationRequest::EditCondition;
	}

	int linkId = link->id;
	int fromId = link->fromId;
	int toId = link->toId;
	DrawDeleteButton("Delete Link", [this, linkId, fromId, toId]()
		{
			graph->parentGraph.DeleteParentLink(linkId);

			// “¯‚¶fromId/toId‚Ì‘g‚Åì‚ç‚ê‚½‘JˆÚ—pƒŠƒ“ƒN‚àˆê‚ÉÁ‚·
			auto& tLinks = graph->transitionGraph.links;
			tLinks.erase(
				std::remove_if(tLinks.begin(), tLinks.end(),
					[fromId, toId](const TransitionLink& l)
					{
						return l.fromId == fromId && l.toId == toId;
					}),
				tLinks.end()
			);
		});
}

// ƒm[ƒh“à‚Å’†‰›Šñ‚¹‚É‚·‚é
void StudioParentStateGraph::CenterNextItem(float totalWidth, float itemWidth)
{
	float pad = (totalWidth - itemWidth) * 0.5f;
	if (pad > 0.0f)
	{
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(pad, 0.0f));
		ImGui::SameLine(0.0f, 0.0f);
	}
}
// ‹¤’Ê‚Ìíœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
void StudioParentStateGraph::DrawDeleteButton(const char* label, const std::function<void()>& onDelete)
{
	ImGui::Spacing();
	ImGui::Spacing();

	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Button, ImVec4(0.7f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered, ImVec4(0.85f, 0.2f, 0.2f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonActive, ImVec4(0.6f, 0.1f, 0.1f, 1.0f));

	bool pressed = ImGui::Button(label, ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f));

	ImGui::PopStyleColor(3);

	if (pressed)
	{
		onDelete();
	}
} 
 
===== StudioSequencer.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\Studio\StudioSequencer.cpp 
#include "StudioSequencer.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/SkinnedMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/ECS/Components/AnimatorComponent.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <filesystem>

// ImGui•`‰æ
void StudioSequencer::DrawGUI(entt::registry& registry, StudioContext& context)
{
	if (!context.showSequencer) return;

	std::string panelName = "Sequencer##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	ImGui::Begin(panelName.c_str());

	RefreshAvailableAnimations(registry);

	auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent, AnimatorComponent>();
	if (view.begin() == view.end())
	{
		ImGui::Text("No skinned model.");
		ImGui::End();
		return;
	}

	auto entity = *view.begin();
	auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(entity);
	auto& animator = view.get<AnimatorComponent>(entity);

	if (!skinned.model)
	{
		ImGui::End();
		return;
	}

	// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ªØ‚è‘Ö‚í‚Á‚½‚çA‚»‚Ìƒ‚ƒfƒ‹—p‚ÌSequencerƒf[ƒ^‚ğ“Ç‚İ‚İ’¼‚·
	if (loadedModelPath != context.currentModelPath)
	{
		sequencerData = SequencerData();

		std::string loadPath = GetSequencerFilePath(context.currentModelPath);
		std::ifstream ifs(loadPath);
		if (ifs.is_open())
		{
			try
			{
				nlohmann::json j;
				ifs >> j;
				sequencerData.FromJson(j);
			}
			catch (const std::exception&) {}
		}

		loadedModelPath = context.currentModelPath;
	}

	if (animator.currentAnimation.empty())
	{
		ImGui::TextDisabled("Select an animation from the Animator panel.");
		ImGui::End();
		return;
	}

	// ‘I‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚éƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ªØ‚è‘Ö‚í‚Á‚½‚çA‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
	if (selectedClipName != animator.currentAnimation)
	{
		selectedClipName = animator.currentAnimation;
		selectedType = SelectionType::None;
		selectedId = -1;
	}

	// ‚±‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚Ì’·‚³‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
	float clipLength = 0.0f;
	for (const auto& anim : skinned.model->GetAnimations())
	{
		if (anim.name == selectedClipName)
		{
			clipLength = anim.secondsLength;
			break;
		}
	}

	// ‚±‚ÌƒNƒŠƒbƒv‚ª¡Ä¶’†‚È‚çAƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[‚Æ‚µ‚Äg‚¤
	float previewTime = (animator.currentAnimation == selectedClipName) ? animator.animationTime : 0.0f;

	SequencerClipData* clip = sequencerData.FindOrCreateClip(selectedClipName);

	const float detailPanelWidth = 220.0f;

	ImGui::BeginChild("SequencerTracks", ImVec2(-detailPanelWidth, 0.0f));
	DrawPointTrack(*clip, previewTime, clipLength);
	DrawRangeTrack(*clip, previewTime, clipLength);
	ImGui::EndChild();

	ImGui::SameLine();

	ImGui::BeginChild("SequencerDetail", ImVec2(detailPanelWidth, 0.0f), true);
	DrawDetailPanel(*clip, context.currentModelPath);
	ImGui::EndChild();

	ImGui::End();
}

// ƒc[ƒ‹ƒo[
void StudioSequencer::DrawToolbar(SequencerClipData& clip, float previewTime)
{
	ImGui::TextDisabled("Clip: %s", clip.clipName.c_str());
	ImGui::TextDisabled("Ranges: %d  Points: %d", (int)clip.ranges.size(), (int)clip.points.size());
	ImGui::Separator();
}
// Point‚Ìƒgƒ‰ƒbƒN•`‰æ
void StudioSequencer::DrawPointTrack(SequencerClipData& clip, float previewTime, float clipLength)
{
	ImGui::Text("Points");
	ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f);
	if (ImGui::Button("+ Add##Point"))
	{
		SequencerPoint p;
		p.id = clip.GenerateId();
		p.time = TimeToNormalized(previewTime, clipLength);
		clip.points.push_back(p);

		selectedType = SelectionType::Point;
		selectedId = p.id;
	}

	ImVec2 trackMin = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
	float trackWidth = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
	const float trackHeight = 24.0f;
	ImVec2 trackMax(trackMin.x + trackWidth, trackMin.y + trackHeight);

	ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
	dl->AddRectFilled(trackMin, trackMax, IM_COL32(40, 40, 40, 255));

	auto timeToX = [&](float normalized) { return trackMin.x + normalized * trackWidth; };

	// ƒvƒŒƒCƒwƒbƒh
	float phX = timeToX(TimeToNormalized(previewTime, clipLength));
	dl->AddLine(ImVec2(phX, trackMin.y), ImVec2(phX, trackMax.y), IM_COL32(29, 158, 117, 200), 1.5f);

	ImGui::PushID("PointTrack");
	for (auto& p : clip.points)
	{
		float x = timeToX(p.time);
		ImVec2 markerMin(x - 3.0f, trackMin.y);
		ImVec2 markerMax(x + 3.0f, trackMax.y);

		bool isSelected = (selectedType == SelectionType::Point && selectedId == p.id);
		ImU32 color = isSelected ? IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255) : IM_COL32(120, 170, 255, 255);
		dl->AddRectFilled(markerMin, markerMax, color);

		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(markerMin);
		ImGui::PushID(p.id);
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("marker", ImVec2(6.0f, trackHeight));

		if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
		{
			float mouseX = ImGui::GetMousePos().x;
			float normalized = (mouseX - trackMin.x) / trackWidth;
			p.time = std::clamp(normalized, 0.0f, 1.0f);
		}

		if (ImGui::IsItemClicked())
		{
			selectedType = SelectionType::Point;
			selectedId = p.id;
		}

		ImGui::PopID();
	}
	ImGui::PopID();

	ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(trackMin.x, trackMax.y + 4.0f));
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));
}
// Range‚Ìƒgƒ‰ƒbƒN•`‰æ
void StudioSequencer::DrawRangeTrack(SequencerClipData& clip, float previewTime, float clipLength)
{
	ImGui::Spacing();
	ImGui::Text("Ranges");
	ImGui::SameLine(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x - 60.0f);
	if (ImGui::Button("+ Add##Range"))
	{
		SequencerRange r;
		r.id = clip.GenerateId();
		r.start = TimeToNormalized(previewTime, clipLength);
		r.end = (std::min)(1.0f, r.start + 0.1f);
		clip.ranges.push_back(r);

		selectedType = SelectionType::Range;
		selectedId = r.id;
	}

	float trackWidth = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x;
	const float rowHeight = 24.0f;
	const float rowSpacing = 4.0f;

	auto timeToX = [&](float normalized, float originX) { return originX + normalized * trackWidth; };

	ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();

	ImGui::PushID("RangeTrack");
	for (auto& r : clip.ranges)
	{
		ImVec2 rowMin = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
		ImVec2 rowMax(rowMin.x + trackWidth, rowMin.y + rowHeight);

		dl->AddRectFilled(rowMin, rowMax, IM_COL32(40, 40, 40, 255));

		float startX = timeToX(r.start, rowMin.x);
		float endX = timeToX(r.end, rowMin.x);

		bool isSelected = (selectedType == SelectionType::Range && selectedId == r.id);
		ImU32 barColor = isSelected ? IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 180) : IM_COL32(120, 170, 255, 180);
		ImU32 handleColor = isSelected ? IM_COL32(255, 200, 80, 255) : IM_COL32(120, 170, 255, 255);

		dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(startX, rowMin.y), ImVec2(endX, rowMax.y), barColor);
		dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(startX - 3.0f, rowMin.y), ImVec2(startX + 3.0f, rowMax.y), handleColor);
		dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(endX - 3.0f, rowMin.y), ImVec2(endX + 3.0f, rowMax.y), handleColor);
		dl->AddText(ImVec2(startX + 6.0f, rowMin.y + 4.0f), IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255), GetSequencerFlagName(r.flag));

		// ƒvƒŒƒCƒwƒbƒh
		float phX = timeToX(TimeToNormalized(previewTime, clipLength), rowMin.x);
		dl->AddLine(ImVec2(phX, rowMin.y), ImVec2(phX, rowMax.y), IM_COL32(29, 158, 117, 200), 1.5f);

		ImGui::PushID(r.id);

		// ’†‰›(ƒo[‘S‘Ì)
		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(startX + 6.0f, rowMin.y));
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("body", ImVec2((std::max)(1.0f, endX - startX - 12.0f), rowHeight));
		if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
		{
			float deltaNorm = ImGui::GetIO().MouseDelta.x / trackWidth;
			float width = r.end - r.start;
			r.start = std::clamp(r.start + deltaNorm, 0.0f, 1.0f - width);
			r.end = r.start + width;
		}
		if (ImGui::IsItemClicked())
		{
			selectedType = SelectionType::Range;
			selectedId = r.id;
		}

		// ¶’[(ŠJnˆÊ’u)
		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(startX - 4.0f, rowMin.y));
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("startHandle", ImVec2(8.0f, rowHeight));
		if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
		{
			float mouseX = ImGui::GetMousePos().x;
			float normalized = (mouseX - rowMin.x) / trackWidth;
			r.start = std::clamp(normalized, 0.0f, r.end);
		}

		// ‰E’[(I—¹ˆÊ’u)
		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(endX - 4.0f, rowMin.y));
		ImGui::InvisibleButton("endHandle", ImVec2(8.0f, rowHeight));
		if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
		{
			float mouseX = ImGui::GetMousePos().x;
			float normalized = (mouseX - rowMin.x) / trackWidth;
			r.end = std::clamp(normalized, r.start, 1.0f);
		}

		ImGui::PopID();

		ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(rowMin.x, rowMax.y + rowSpacing));
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));
	}
	ImGui::PopID();
}
// Ú×ƒpƒlƒ‹
void StudioSequencer::DrawDetailPanel(SequencerClipData& clip, const std::string& modelPath)
{
	if (ImGui::Button("Save", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		Save(modelPath);
	}

	if (selectedType == SelectionType::Range)
	{
		SequencerRange* range = nullptr;
		for (auto& r : clip.ranges)
		{
			if (r.id == selectedId) { range = &r; break; }
		}

		if (!range)
		{
			selectedType = SelectionType::None;
			selectedId = -1;
			ImGui::TextDisabled("No selection.");
			return;
		}

		ImGui::Text("Range");
		ImGui::Separator();

		if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Flag", GetSequencerFlagName(range->flag)))
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < (int)SequencerFlag::Count; i++) 
			{
				SequencerFlag f = (SequencerFlag)i;
				bool isSelected = (range->flag == f);
				if (ImGui::Selectable(GetSequencerFlagName(f), isSelected))
				{
					range->flag = f;
				}
			}
			ImGui::EndCombo();
		}

		float startPct = range->start * 100.0f;
		float endPct = range->end * 100.0f;

		if (ImGui::DragFloat("Start %", &startPct, 0.1f, 0.0f, endPct))
		{
			range->start = startPct * 0.01f;
		}
		if (ImGui::DragFloat("End %", &endPct, 0.1f, startPct, 100.0f))
		{
			range->end = endPct * 0.01f;
		}

		int rangeId = range->id;
		DrawDeleteButton("Delete Range", [this, &clip, rangeId]() { DeleteRange(clip, rangeId); });
		return;
	}

	if (selectedType == SelectionType::Point)
	{
		SequencerPoint* point = nullptr;
		for (auto& p : clip.points)
		{
			if (p.id == selectedId) { point = &p; break; }
		}

		if (!point)
		{
			selectedType = SelectionType::None;
			selectedId = -1;
			ImGui::TextDisabled("No selection.");
			return;
		}

		ImGui::Text("Point");
		ImGui::Separator();

		if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Flag", GetSequencerFlagName(point->flag)))
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < (int)SequencerFlag::Count; i++) 
			{
				SequencerFlag f = (SequencerFlag)i;
				bool isSelected = (point->flag == f);
				if (ImGui::Selectable(GetSequencerFlagName(f), isSelected))
				{
					point->flag = f;
				}
			}
			ImGui::EndCombo();
		}

		float timePct = point->time * 100.0f;
		if (ImGui::DragFloat("Time (%)", &timePct, 0.1f, 0.0f, 100.0f))
		{
			point->time = timePct * 0.01f;
		}

		int pointId = point->id;
		DrawDeleteButton("Delete Point", [this, &clip, pointId]() { DeletePoint(clip, pointId); });
		return;
	}

	ImGui::TextDisabled("No selection.");
}

// Rangeíœ
void StudioSequencer::DeleteRange(SequencerClipData& clip, int id)
{
	clip.ranges.erase(
		std::remove_if(clip.ranges.begin(), clip.ranges.end(),
			[id](const SequencerRange& r) { return r.id == id; }),
		clip.ranges.end()
	);
	selectedType = SelectionType::None;
	selectedId = -1;
}
// Pointíœ
void StudioSequencer::DeletePoint(SequencerClipData& clip, int id)
{
	clip.points.erase(
		std::remove_if(clip.points.begin(), clip.points.end(),
			[id](const SequencerPoint& p) { return p.id == id; }),
		clip.points.end()
	);
	selectedType = SelectionType::None;
	selectedId = -1;
}

// íœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
void StudioSequencer::DrawDeleteButton(const char* label, const std::function<void()>& onDelete)
{
	ImGui::Spacing();
	ImGui::Spacing();

	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Button, ImVec4(0.7f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered, ImVec4(0.85f, 0.2f, 0.2f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonActive, ImVec4(0.6f, 0.1f, 0.1f, 1.0f));

	bool pressed = ImGui::Button(label, ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f));

	ImGui::PopStyleColor(3);

	if (pressed)
	{
		onDelete();
	}
}

// •b”‚ğ0.0`1.0‚É
float StudioSequencer::TimeToNormalized(float time, float clipLength)
{
	if (clipLength <= 0.0001f) return 0.0f;
	return time / clipLength;
}
// 0.0`1.0‚ğ•b”‚É
float StudioSequencer::NormalizedToTime(float normalized, float clipLength)
{
	return normalized * clipLength;
}

// •Û‘¶
void StudioSequencer::Save(const std::string& modelPath)
{
	if (modelPath.empty()) return;

	std::string savePath = GetSequencerFilePath(modelPath);

	// ƒtƒHƒ‹ƒ_‚ª–³‚¯‚ê‚Îì¬‚·‚é
	std::filesystem::create_directories("Data/Json/Sequencer");

	std::ofstream ofs(savePath);
	if (ofs.is_open())
	{
		ofs << sequencerData.ToJson().dump(4);
	}
}
// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ƒpƒX‚©‚ç•Û‘¶æ‚ÌƒpƒX‚ğ‘g‚İ—§‚Ä‚é
std::string StudioSequencer::GetSequencerFilePath(const std::string& modelPath)
{
	std::filesystem::path p(modelPath);
	std::string modelName = p.stem().string(); // Šg’£q‚ğœ‚¢‚½Aƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒ‹–¼

	return "Data/Json/Sequencer/" + modelName + ".sequencer";
}

// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ˆê——‚ğXV‚·‚é
void StudioSequencer::RefreshAvailableAnimations(entt::registry& registry)
{
	availableAnimations.clear();

	auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent>();
	if (view.begin() == view.end()) return;

	auto entity = *view.begin();
	auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(entity);

	if (!skinned.model) return;

	for (const auto& anim : skinned.model->GetAnimations())
	{
		availableAnimations.push_back(anim.name);
	}
}
// ƒV[ƒPƒ“ƒT[ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚Ì•\¦–¼‚ğ•Ô‚·
const char* StudioSequencer::GetSequencerFlagName(SequencerFlag flag)

{
	// í—Ş‚ª‘‚¦‚½‚ç‚±‚±‚É‚à’Ç‰Á‚·‚é
	static const char* names[] =
	{
		"AnimExit",
		"SuperArmor",
		"Attack",
		"Trimming"
	};

	// names‚Ì—v‘f”‚ÆASequencerFlag‚ÌŒÂ”‚ªH‚¢ˆá‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çAƒrƒ‹ƒh‚ÉƒGƒ‰[‚É‚·‚é
	static_assert(sizeof(names) / sizeof(names[0]) == (size_t)SequencerFlag::Count,
		u8"SequencerFlag ‚Æ names ‚Ì”‚ªˆê’v‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚Ü‚¹‚ñB’Ç‰ÁEíœ‚Í—¼•û’¼‚µ‚Ä‚­‚¾‚³‚¢B");

	int index = (int)flag;
	if (index < 0 || index >= (int)SequencerFlag::Count) return "?";

	return names[index];
}
 
 
===== StudioTimeline.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\Studio\StudioTimeline.cpp 
ï»¿#include "StudioTimeline.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <string>

void StudioTimeline::DrawGUI(entt::registry& registry, StudioContext& context)
{
    if (!context.showTimeline) return;

    std::string panelName = "Timeline##" + std::to_string(windowId);
    ImGui::Begin(panelName.c_str());

    auto view = registry.view<SkinnedMeshComponent, AnimatorComponent>();

    if (view.begin() == view.end())
    {
        ImGui::Text("No skinned model.");
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    auto entity = *view.begin();
    auto& skinned = view.get<SkinnedMeshComponent>(entity);
    auto& animator = view.get<AnimatorComponent>(entity);

    if (!skinned.model)
    {
        ImGui::End();
        return;
    }

    // ã‚¢ãƒ‹ãƒ¡ãƒ¼ã‚·ãƒ§ãƒ³é•·ã‚’å–å¾—
    float animLength = 0.0f;
    const auto& animations = skinned.model->GetAnimations();
    for (const auto& anim : animations)
    {
        if (anim.name == animator.currentAnimation)
        {
            animLength = anim.secondsLength;
            break;
        }
    }

    // å…ˆé ­ã«æˆ»ã‚‹
    if (ImGui::Button("<<"))
    {
        animator.animationTime = 0.0f;
        animator.isPlaying = false;
        animator.isEnd = false;
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // å†ç”Ÿ/åœæ­¢
    if (animator.isPlaying)
    {
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"â– "))
        {
            animator.isPlaying = false;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        if (ImGui::Button(u8"â–¶"))
        {
            if (animator.animationTime >= animLength)
                animator.animationTime = 0.0f;
            animator.isPlaying = true;
            animator.isEnd = false;
        }
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // ãƒ«ãƒ¼ãƒ—
    ImGui::Checkbox("Loop", &animator.loop);
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // ç¾åœ¨ã®ã‚¢ãƒ‹ãƒ¡ãƒ¼ã‚·ãƒ§ãƒ³åï¼ˆç·‘è‰²ï¼‰
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f),
        animator.currentAnimation.c_str());

    // æ™‚åˆ»è¡¨ç¤º
    ImGui::Text("%.2f / %.2f s", animator.animationTime, animLength);
    ImGui::SameLine();

    // å†ç”Ÿæ™‚åˆ»ã‚¹ãƒ©ã‚¤ãƒ€ãƒ¼ï¼ˆå…¨å¹…ï¼‰
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(-1);
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("##animTime",
        &animator.animationTime, 0.0f, animLength))
    {
        // ã‚¹ã‚¯ãƒ©ãƒ–ä¸­ã¯åœæ­¢ã—ã¦ãƒãƒ¼ã‚ºã‚’ç¢ºèª
        animator.isPlaying = false;
        animator.isEnd = false;
        context.requestPoseUpdate = true;
    }

    ImGui::End();
}

// ‰E‘¤ƒƒjƒ…[
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawInspector()
{
	if (selectedState)
	{
		// State‚Í“Ç‚İæ‚èê—p(–¼‘OEF‚Ì•ÒWAíœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚Í‚½‚È‚¢)
		ImGui::Text("%s", selectedState->name.c_str());
		ImGui::TextDisabled("State name/color can only be edited from the Animator Graph.");
		return;
	}

	if (selectedLink)
	{
		DrawLinkInspector(selectedLink);
		return;
	}

	ImGui::TextDisabled("No link selected.");
}

// ğŒ®•ÒW‰æ–Ê‚Ö‚Ì‘JˆÚ—v‹
bool StudioTransitionGraph::ConsumeConditionEditRequest(TransitionLink*& outLink)
{
	if (!pendingConditionEdit)
	{
		outLink = nullptr;
		return false;
	}

	outLink = selectedLink;
	pendingConditionEdit = false;
	return true;
}

// State•`‰æ
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawStates()
{
	stateScreenRects.clear();

	// ‡Šî’²‚ÌƒXƒ^ƒCƒ‹
	const ImColor bgColor(90, 60, 110, 255);
	const ImColor borderColor(140, 90, 170, 255);

	for (auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
	{
		ed::NodeId nodeId(state.id);

		if (needsApplyPositions)
		{
			ed::SetNodePosition(nodeId, ImVec2(state.posX, state.posY));
		}

		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, bgColor);
		ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, borderColor);
		ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_NodeRounding, 4.0f);

		ed::BeginNode(nodeId);

		float nodeWidth = (state.type == ParentStateType::Entry) ? 100.0f : ImGui::CalcTextSize("New State").x;
		float textWidth = ImGui::CalcTextSize(state.name.c_str()).x;

		CenterNextItem(nodeWidth, textWidth);
		ImGui::Text("%s", state.name.c_str());

		ImGui::SameLine(nodeWidth);
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));

		ed::EndNode();

		stateScreenRects[state.id] = { ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), ImGui::GetItemRectMax() };

		ed::PopStyleVar(1);
		ed::PopStyleColor(2);

		ImVec2 currentPos = ed::GetNodePosition(nodeId);
		state.posX = currentPos.x;
		state.posY = currentPos.y;
	}
	needsApplyPositions = false;
}

// Link•`‰æ
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawLinks()
{
	const float arrowTrim = 10.0f;

	for (const auto& link : graph->transitionGraph.links)
	{
		ImVec2 startPoint, endPoint;
		CalcLinkPoints(link, startPoint, endPoint);

		if (startPoint.x == 0.0f && startPoint.y == 0.0f &&
			endPoint.x == 0.0f && endPoint.y == 0.0f)
		{
			continue;
		}

		ImVec2 dir(endPoint.x - startPoint.x, endPoint.y - startPoint.y);
		float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
		ImVec2 trimmedEnd = endPoint;
		if (len > arrowTrim)
		{
			trimmedEnd.x = endPoint.x - dir.x / len * arrowTrim;
			trimmedEnd.y = endPoint.y - dir.y / len * arrowTrim;
		}

		DrawLinkPins(link, startPoint, trimmedEnd);

		ImDrawList* drawList = ed::GetNodeBackgroundDrawList(ed::NodeId(link.fromId));
		if (drawList)
		{
			// ‘JˆÚ—pƒŠƒ“ƒN‚Í‡F‚Å•`‰æ‚·‚é
			const ImU32 linkColor = IM_COL32(180, 130, 220, 255);
			DrawArrowHead(drawList, startPoint, endPoint, linkColor);
		}
	}
}
// ƒŠƒ“ƒN1–{‚ÌÚ‘±“_‚ğŒvZ
void StudioTransitionGraph::CalcLinkPoints(const TransitionLink& link, ImVec2& outStart, ImVec2& outEnd)
{
	auto fromIt = stateScreenRects.find(link.fromId);
	auto toIt = stateScreenRects.find(link.toId);
	if (fromIt == stateScreenRects.end() || toIt == stateScreenRects.end())
	{
		outStart = ImVec2();
		outEnd = ImVec2();
		return;
	}

	const ImVec2& fromMin = fromIt->second.first;
	const ImVec2& fromMax = fromIt->second.second;
	const ImVec2& toMin = toIt->second.first;
	const ImVec2& toMax = toIt->second.second;

	ImVec2 fromCenter((fromMin.x + fromMax.x) * 0.5f, (fromMin.y + fromMax.y) * 0.5f);
	ImVec2 toCenter((toMin.x + toMax.x) * 0.5f, (toMin.y + toMax.y) * 0.5f);

	ImVec2 startPoint = GetEdgePoint(fromMin, fromMax, toCenter);
	ImVec2 endPoint = GetEdgePoint(toMin, toMax, fromCenter);

	if (HasReverseLink(link.fromId, link.toId))
	{
		bool fromIsSmaller = link.fromId < link.toId;
		const ImVec2& baseCenter = fromIsSmaller ? fromCenter : toCenter;
		const ImVec2& otherCenter = fromIsSmaller ? toCenter : fromCenter;

		ImVec2 dir(otherCenter.x - baseCenter.x, otherCenter.y - baseCenter.y);
		float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
		if (len > 0.0001f)
		{
			ImVec2 perp(-dir.y / len, dir.x / len);

			const float offsetAmount = 8.0f;
			float sign = fromIsSmaller ? 1.0f : -1.0f;

			ImVec2 offset(perp.x * offsetAmount * sign, perp.y * offsetAmount * sign);

			startPoint.x += offset.x;
			startPoint.y += offset.y;
			endPoint.x += offset.x;
			endPoint.y += offset.y;
		}
	}

	outStart = startPoint;
	outEnd = endPoint;
}
// ŒvZÏ‚İ‚ÌÀ•W‚Éƒsƒ“‚ğ”z’u
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawLinkPins(const TransitionLink& link, const ImVec2& start, const ImVec2& end)
{
	const int64_t idOffset = 10000000;
	ed::NodeId outNodeId(idOffset + (int64_t)link.id * 2);
	ed::NodeId inNodeId(idOffset + (int64_t)link.id * 2 + 1);

	ed::PinId outPinId = GetPinId(link.id, true);
	ed::PinId inPinId = GetPinId(link.id, false);

	ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_NodePadding, ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0));
	ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBg, ImColor(0, 0, 0, 0));
	ed::PushStyleColor(ed::StyleColor_NodeBorder, ImColor(0, 0, 0, 0));

	ed::PushStyleVar(ed::StyleVar_LinkStrength, 0.0f);

	ed::SetNodePosition(outNodeId, start);
	ed::BeginNode(outNodeId);
	ed::BeginPin(outPinId, ed::PinKind::Output);
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(1, 1));
	ed::EndPin();
	ed::EndNode();

	ed::SetNodePosition(inNodeId, end);
	ed::BeginNode(inNodeId);
	ed::BeginPin(inPinId, ed::PinKind::Input);
	ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(1, 1));
	ed::EndPin();
	ed::EndNode();

	ed::PopStyleVar(2);
	ed::PopStyleColor(2);

	ed::Link(ed::LinkId(link.id), outPinId, inPinId);
}
// •Ó‚©‚çƒŠƒ“ƒN‚ğˆø‚«o‚·“_‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
ImVec2 StudioTransitionGraph::GetEdgePoint(const ImVec2& rectMin, const ImVec2& rectMax, const ImVec2& target)
{
	ImVec2 center((rectMin.x + rectMax.x) * 0.5f, (rectMin.y + rectMax.y) * 0.5f);
	float halfW = (rectMax.x - rectMin.x) * 0.5f;
	float halfH = (rectMax.y - rectMin.y) * 0.5f;

	ImVec2 dir(target.x - center.x, target.y - center.y);
	if (dir.x == 0.0f && dir.y == 0.0f) return center;

	float scaleX = (dir.x != 0.0f) ? halfW / fabsf(dir.x) : FLT_MAX;
	float scaleY = (dir.y != 0.0f) ? halfH / fabsf(dir.y) : FLT_MAX;
	float scale = (scaleX < scaleY) ? scaleX : scaleY;

	return ImVec2(center.x + dir.x * scale, center.y + dir.y * scale);
}
// ‹tŒü‚«‚ÌLink‚ª‚ ‚é‚©Šm”F
bool StudioTransitionGraph::HasReverseLink(int fromId, int toId) const
{
	for (const auto& link : graph->transitionGraph.links)
	{
		if (link.fromId == toId && link.toId == fromId)
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	return false;
}
// –îˆó•`‰æ
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawArrowHead(ImDrawList* drawList, const ImVec2& start, const ImVec2& end, ImU32 color)
{
	ImVec2 dir(end.x - start.x, end.y - start.y);
	float len = sqrtf(dir.x * dir.x + dir.y * dir.y);
	if (len < 0.0001f) return;

	dir.x /= len;
	dir.y /= len;

	ImVec2 perp(-dir.y, dir.x);

	const float arrowLength = 18.0f;
	const float arrowWidth = 13.0f;

	ImVec2 tip = end;
	ImVec2 baseCenter(end.x - dir.x * arrowLength, end.y - dir.y * arrowLength);
	ImVec2 baseLeft(baseCenter.x + perp.x * arrowWidth * 0.5f, baseCenter.y + perp.y * arrowWidth * 0.5f);
	ImVec2 baseRight(baseCenter.x - perp.x * arrowWidth * 0.5f, baseCenter.y - perp.y * arrowWidth * 0.5f);

	drawList->AddTriangleFilled(tip, baseLeft, baseRight, color);
}

// Linkì¬‚Ì‹N“_‘I‘ğ
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawStateContextMenu()
{
	static ed::NodeId contextNodeId;

	ed::Suspend();
	if (ed::ShowNodeContextMenu(&contextNodeId))
	{
		ImGui::OpenPopup("TransitionNodeContextMenu");
	}
	if (ImGui::BeginPopup("TransitionNodeContextMenu"))
	{
		if (ImGui::MenuItem(u8"Link"))
		{
			pendingLinkFromId = (int)contextNodeId.Get();
		}
		ImGui::EndPopup();
	}
	ed::Resume();
}
// Linkì¬ƒ‚[ƒh‚Ì“ü—Íˆ—
void StudioTransitionGraph::UpdateLinkCreation()
{
	if (pendingLinkFromId == -1) return;

	if (ImGui::IsMouseClicked(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
	{
		ed::NodeId hovered = ed::GetHoveredNode();

		if (hovered && (int)hovered.Get() != pendingLinkFromId)
		{
			int targetId = (int)hovered.Get();

			bool targetIsEntry = false;
			for (const auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
			{
				if (state.id == targetId && state.type == ParentStateType::Entry)
				{
					targetIsEntry = true;
					break;
				}
			}

			if (!targetIsEntry)
			{
				graph->transitionGraph.CreateLink(pendingLinkFromId, targetId);
			}
		}

		pendingLinkFromId = -1;
	}
}
// Linkì¬’†‚ÌƒvƒŒƒrƒ…[•`‰æ
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawPendingLink()
{
	if (pendingLinkFromId == -1) return;

	auto it = stateScreenRects.find(pendingLinkFromId);
	if (it == stateScreenRects.end()) return;

	ImVec2 mousePos = ImGui::GetMousePos();
	ImVec2 startPoint = GetEdgePoint(it->second.first, it->second.second, mousePos);

	ImDrawList* drawList = ed::GetNodeBackgroundDrawList(ed::NodeId(pendingLinkFromId));
	if (!drawList) return;

	const ImU32 previewColor = IM_COL32(200, 150, 255, 255);

	drawList->AddLine(startPoint, mousePos, previewColor, 2.0f);
}

// ‘I‘ğó‘Ô‚ğXV
void StudioTransitionGraph::UpdateSelection()
{
	selectedState = nullptr;
	selectedLink = nullptr;

	if (ed::GetSelectedObjectCount() == 0) return;

	ed::NodeId selectedNodeId;
	if (ed::GetSelectedNodes(&selectedNodeId, 1) > 0)
	{
		for (auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
		{
			if (state.id == (int)selectedNodeId.Get())
			{
				selectedState = &state;
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	ed::LinkId selectedLinkId;
	if (ed::GetSelectedLinks(&selectedLinkId, 1) > 0)
	{
		for (auto& link : graph->transitionGraph.links)
		{
			if (link.id == (int)selectedLinkId.Get())
			{
				selectedLink = &link;
				return;
			}
		}
	}
}
// Link‚ÌInspector
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawLinkInspector(TransitionLink* link)
{
	const ParentState* fromState = nullptr;
	const ParentState* toState = nullptr;
	for (const auto& state : graph->parentGraph.states)
	{
		if (state.id == link->fromId) fromState = &state;
		if (state.id == link->toId)   toState = &state;
	}

	std::string fromName = fromState ? fromState->name : "?";
	std::string toName = toState ? toState->name : "?";

	ImGui::TextWrapped("%s -> %s", fromName.c_str(), toName.c_str());

	if (ImGui::Button("Edit Condition", ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f)))
	{
		pendingConditionEdit = true;
	}

	int linkId = link->id;
	DrawDeleteButton("Delete Link", [this, linkId]() { graph->transitionGraph.DeleteLink(linkId); });
}

// ƒm[ƒh“à‚Å’†‰›Šñ‚¹‚É‚·‚é
void StudioTransitionGraph::CenterNextItem(float totalWidth, float itemWidth)
{
	float pad = (totalWidth - itemWidth) * 0.5f;
	if (pad > 0.0f)
	{
		ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(pad, 0.0f));
		ImGui::SameLine(0.0f, 0.0f);
	}
}
// ‹¤’Ê‚Ìíœƒ{ƒ^ƒ“
void StudioTransitionGraph::DrawDeleteButton(const char* label, const std::function<void()>& onDelete)
{
	ImGui::Spacing();
	ImGui::Spacing();

	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Button, ImVec4(0.7f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered, ImVec4(0.85f, 0.2f, 0.2f, 1.0f));
	ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_ButtonActive, ImVec4(0.6f, 0.1f, 0.1f, 1.0f));

	bool pressed = ImGui::Button(label, ImVec2(-1.0f, 0.0f));

	ImGui::PopStyleColor(3);

	if (pressed)
	{
		onDelete();
	}
} 
 
===== StudioWindow.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Tool\Studio\StudioWindow.cpp 
#include "StudioWindow.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>
#include <cassert>

void StudioWindow::Initialize(ID3D11Device* device, StudioSharedContext* shared, UINT width, UINT height)
{
	renderWidth = width;
	renderHeight = height;
	context.device = device;
	this->shared = shared;

	renderSystem.Initialize(device);
	lightManager.Initialize(device);

	// IBLƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒƒZƒbƒg
	renderSystem.SetIBLTextures(
		shared->skyBox.GetDiffuseIem(),
		shared->skyBox.GetSRV(),
		shared->skyBox.GetLutGgx()
	);

	stadoiModelViewer.Initialize(device, &shared->resourceManager);
	animatorGraph.Initialize();

	ResizeRenderTarget();
}

// XV
void StudioWindow::Update(float elapsedTime)
{
	// ƒJƒƒ‰XV
	camera.Update(viewportHovered, elapsedTime);

	// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹ƒrƒ…ƒ[XV
	stadoiModelViewer.Update(elapsedTime, context);

	// ƒAƒjƒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‘¬“xXV
	animationSpeedSystem.Update(studioRegistry);

	if (context.requestPoseUpdate)
	{
		// ƒXƒNƒ‰ƒu‘€ì‚ÍŠÔ‚ği‚ß‚¸ƒ|[ƒY‚¾‚¯XV
		animationSystem.EvaluatePose(studioRegistry);
		context.requestPoseUpdate = false;
	}
	else
	{
		animationSystem.Update(studioRegistry, elapsedTime);
	}

	// ƒAƒ^ƒbƒ`ƒƒ“ƒgXV
	for (auto& attachment : attachments)
	{
		attachment->Update(elapsedTime, context);
	}
}

// •`‰æ
void StudioWindow::Render()
{
	// ƒzƒo[’†‚Í–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€A—£‚ê‚½‚ç1/10
	if (!isHovered)
	{
		renderSkipCount++;
		if (renderSkipCount < 10) return;
		renderSkipCount = 0;
	}
	else
	{
		renderSkipCount = 0;
	}

	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// •`‰ææ‚ğ©‘ORTV‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	Begin();

	// CbSceneXV
	CbScene cbSceneData{};
	cbSceneData.view = camera.GetView();
	cbSceneData.projection = camera.GetProjection();
	const auto& eye = camera.GetEye();
	cbSceneData.cameraPosition = { eye.x, eye.y, eye.z, 0.0f };

	dc->UpdateSubresource(renderSystem.GetCbScene(), 0, nullptr, &cbSceneData, 0, 0);
	dc->VSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress());
	dc->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, renderSystem.GetCbSceneAddress());

	// ƒ‰ƒCƒg‚ğGPU‚É“]‘—
	lightManager.Bind(dc);

	// ƒtƒ‰ƒXƒ^ƒ€XV
	renderSystem.SetFrustum(camera.GetView(), camera.GetProjection());

	// ƒXƒJƒCƒ{ƒbƒNƒX•`‰æ
	shared->skyBox.Render(
		dc,
		camera.GetView(),
		camera.GetProjection(),
		camera.GetEye()
	);

	// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚ª‚ ‚é‚¾‚¯RenderSystem‚ğ‘–‚ç‚¹‚é
	if (!studioRegistry.storage<TransformComponent>().empty())
	{
		// •`‰æ
		renderSystem.Render(
			studioRegistry,
			dc,
			rtv.Get(),
			dsv.Get(),
			context.device,
			renderWidth,
			renderHeight
		);
	}

	// ƒAƒ^ƒbƒ`ƒƒ“ƒg‚Ì3D•`‰æ
	for (auto& attachment : attachments)
	{
		attachment->OnRender(
			context.deviceContext,
			camera.GetView(),
			camera.GetProjection(),
			studioRegistry,
			context
		);
	}

	// •`‰ææ‚ğƒƒCƒ“‚É–ß‚·
	End();
}

// ImGui•`‰æ

{
	bool open = true;
	ImGui::Begin(windowName, &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_MenuBar);
	if (!open) closeRequested = true;

	// StudioƒEƒBƒ“ƒhƒE‘S‘Ì‚Åƒzƒo[”»’è
	isHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered(ImGuiHoveredFlags_ChildWindows | ImGuiHoveredFlags_DockHierarchy);

		}
		ImGui::EndMenuBar();

	// ‚±‚ÌStudioê—p‚ÌDockSpace

	ImGuiID dockSpaceId = ImGui::GetID(dockSpaceName.c_str());
	ImGui::DockSpace(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);

	// ‰‰ñƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯ƒfƒtƒHƒ‹ƒgƒŒƒCƒAƒEƒg‚ğ‘g‚Ş
	if (firstDockSetup)
	{
		firstDockSetup = false;

		ImGui::DockBuilderRemoveNode(dockSpaceId);
		ImGui::DockBuilderAddNode(dockSpaceId, ImGuiDockNodeFlags_None);
		ImGui::DockBuilderSetNodeSize(dockSpaceId, ImVec2(1280, 720));

		ImGuiID dockMain = dockSpaceId;
		ImGuiID dockLeft = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Left, 0.2f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockRight = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Right, 0.7f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockBottom = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockMain, ImGuiDir_Down, 0.2f, nullptr, &dockMain);
		ImGuiID dockSequencer = ImGui::DockBuilderSplitNode(
			dockBottom, ImGuiDir_Down, 0.5f, nullptr, &dockBottom);

		std::string viewportName = "Viewport##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animatorName = "Animator##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string timelineName = "Timeline##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string sequencerName = "Sequencer##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animatorGraphName = "AnimatorGraph##" + std::to_string(windowId);
		std::string animationSpeedName = "Animation Speed##" + std::to_string(windowId);

		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(viewportName.c_str(), dockMain);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animatorName.c_str(), dockLeft);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(timelineName.c_str(), dockBottom);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(sequencerName.c_str(), dockSequencer);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animatorGraphName.c_str(), dockRight);
		ImGui::DockBuilderDockWindow(animationSpeedName.c_str(), dockSequencer);

		ImGui::DockBuilderFinish(dockSpaceId);
	}

	// Viewportƒpƒlƒ‹
	std::string viewportName = "Viewport##" + std::to_string(windowId);
	ImGui::Begin(viewportName.c_str());

	// ‰æ¿Šm”F
	// ImGui::Text("Render Resolution: %ux%u", renderWidth, renderHeight);

	ImVec2 region = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
	if (region.x > 1 && region.y > 1)
	{
		camera.UpdateAspect(region.x, region.y);
	}

	ImGui::Image(reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(srv.Get()), region);

	// ƒ‚ƒfƒ‹‚Ìƒhƒƒbƒv‚ğó‚¯æ‚é
	if (ImGui::BeginDragDropTarget())
	{
		if (const ImGuiPayload* payload = ImGui::AcceptDragDropPayload("MODEL_PATH"))
		{
			std::string droppedModelPath(static_cast<const char*>(payload->Data));
			LoadAsset(droppedModelPath);
		}
		ImGui::EndDragDropTarget();
	}

	// ƒJƒƒ‰‘€ì‚ÌŠJn”»’è‚ÍViewport’P‘Ì‚Ìƒzƒo[‚¾‚¯‚ğŒ©‚é
	viewportHovered = ImGui::IsWindowHovered();

	ImGui::End(); // Viewport

	// Animatorƒpƒlƒ‹
	if (context.showAnimator)
	{
		studioAnimator.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// Timelineƒpƒlƒ‹
	if (context.showTimeline)
	{
		studioTimeline.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// Sequencerƒpƒlƒ‹
	if (context.showSequencer)
	{
		studioSequencer.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// AnimatorGraphƒpƒlƒ‹
	if (context.showAnimatorGraph)
	{
		animatorGraph.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	// AnimationSpeedƒpƒlƒ‹
	if (context.showAnimationSpeed)
	{
		studioAnimationSpeed.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);
	}

	stadoiModelViewer.DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);

	for (auto& attachment : attachments)
		attachment->DrawGUI(studioRegistry, context);

	ImGui::End(); // Studio
}

void StudioWindow::Begin()
{
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// Œ»İ‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğ•Û‘¶
	UINT numVP = 1;
	dc->RSGetViewports(&numVP, &savedViewport);

	// ©‘O‚Ìƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğƒZƒbƒg
	D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{};
	vp.Width = static_cast<float>(renderWidth);
	vp.Height = static_cast<float>(renderHeight);
	vp.MinDepth = 0.0f;
	vp.MaxDepth = 1.0f;
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);

	// •`‰ææ‚ğ©‘ORTV‚ÉØ‚è‘Ö‚¦
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, rtv.GetAddressOf(), dsv.Get());

	// ƒNƒŠƒA
	float clear[4] = { 0.15f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1.0f };
	dc->ClearRenderTargetView(rtv.Get(), clear);
	dc->ClearDepthStencilView(dsv.Get(), D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH | D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL, 1.0f, 0);
}
void StudioWindow::End()
{
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc = context.deviceContext;
	if (!dc) return;

	// ƒrƒ…[ƒ|[ƒg‚ğŒ³‚É–ß‚·
	dc->RSSetViewports(1, &savedViewport);

	// •`‰ææ‚ğƒƒCƒ“‚É–ß‚·
	ID3D11RenderTargetView* nullRTV = nullptr;
	dc->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &nullRTV, nullptr);
}
void StudioWindow::ResizeRenderTarget()
{
	ID3D11Device* device = context.device;
	if (!device) return;
	if (renderWidth == 0 || renderHeight == 0) return;

	// ŒÃ‚¢ƒŠƒ\[ƒX‚ğ‰ğ•ú
	srv.Reset();
	rtv.Reset();
	renderTexture.Reset();
	dsv.Reset();
	depthTexture.Reset();

	HRESULT hr;

	// ƒJƒ‰[ƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒÄ¶¬
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_RENDER_TARGET | D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, renderTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateRenderTargetView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, rtv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateShaderResourceView(renderTexture.Get(), nullptr, srv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}

	// [“xƒeƒNƒXƒ`ƒƒÄ¶¬
	{
		D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC desc{};
		desc.Width = renderWidth;
		desc.Height = renderHeight;
		desc.MipLevels = 1;
		desc.ArraySize = 1;
		desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT;
		desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
		desc.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
		desc.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_DEPTH_STENCIL;

		hr = device->CreateTexture2D(&desc, nullptr, depthTexture.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
		hr = device->CreateDepthStencilView(depthTexture.Get(), nullptr, dsv.GetAddressOf());
		assert(SUCCEEDED(hr));
	}
}
 
 
===== AdaptiveTrigger.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Unsorted\Controller\AdaptiveTrigger.h 
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    struct CurvePoint
    {
        // ‰Ÿ‚µ‚İ—Ê
        float position = 0.0f; // (0,0`1.0)
        // ’ïR‚Ì‹­‚³
        float value = 0.0f;    // (0.0`1.0)
    };

    struct TriggerEffect
    {
        // o—ÍƒŠƒXƒg
        std::vector<CurvePoint> forcePoints;
    };

    // Œ»İ’n(‰Ÿ‚µ‚İ—Ê)‚©‚ç‘OŒã‚É‘¶İ‚·‚éƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğüŒ`•âŠ®‚µ‚ÄŒ»İ‚Ì’ïR’l‚ğ‹‚ß‚é
    inline float SampleCurve(const std::vector<CurvePoint>& points, float position)
    {
        // –³’ïR
        if (points.empty())
        {
            return 0.0f;
        }
        // “_‚ª1‚Â‚¾‚¯A‚Ü‚½‚Í position ‚ªÅ‰‚Ì“_‚æ‚èè‘O‚Ì‚ÍÅ‰‚Ì“_‚Ì’l‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Üg‚¤
        if (points.size() == 1 || position <= points.front().position)
        {
            return points.front().value;
        }
        // position ‚ªÅŒã‚Ì“_‚æ‚èŒã‚ë‚È‚çÅŒã‚Ì“_‚Ì’l‚ğ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Üg‚¤
        if (position >= points.back().position)
        {
            return points.back().value;
        }

        // ŠY“–‚·‚é2“_‚ğ’T‚µ‚Ä‚»‚ÌŠÔ‚ğüŒ`•âŠÔ‚·‚é
        for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            const CurvePoint& a = points[i];
            const CurvePoint& b = points[i + 1];
            if (position >= a.position && position <= b.position)
            {
                float t = (position - a.position) / (b.position - a.position);
                return a.value + (b.value - a.value) * t;
            }
        }
        return points.back().value;
    }
}

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    struct Zone
    {
        // ‚±‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚É‘®‚·‚éƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
        std::vector<CurvePoint> points;
    };

    // ƒŒƒ“ƒW“à‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚ğŒŸo‚·‚é
    inline std::vector<Zone> DetectZones(const std::vector<CurvePoint>& points)
    {
        // ŒŸo‚µ‚½ƒ][ƒ“‚ÌƒŠƒXƒg
        std::vector<Zone> zones;
		// Œ»İ‚Ìƒ][ƒ“
        Zone current;

        // ƒŒƒ“ƒW“à‚Ìƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğæ“ª‚©‚ç‡‚É‘–¸‚·‚é
        for (const auto& p : points)
        {
			// ’ïR’l‚ª0‚æ‚è‘å‚«‚¯‚ê‚ÎŒ»İ‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚É’Ç‰Á‚·‚é
            if (p.value > 0.0f)
            {
                current.points.push_back(p);
            }
            else
            {
				// ’ïR’l‚ª0‚È‚ç’ïR‚ª‚È‚¢êŠ‚Ö—ˆ‚½
                if (!current.points.empty())
                {
                    // ˆê‹æØ‚è‚Æ‚·‚é
                    zones.push_back(current);
					// Œ»İ‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
                    current.points.clear();
                }
            }
        }

		// ‰Ÿ‚µ‚İØ‚Á‚½‚É’ïR’l0ˆÈã‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î‚»‚Ì’n“_‚ğÅŒã‚Ìƒ][ƒ“‚Æ‚·‚é
        if (!current.points.empty())
        {
            zones.push_back(current);
        }

        return zones;
    }
}

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    // “¯‚¶’l‚Æ‚İ‚È‚·Œë·‚Ì”ÍˆÍ
    constexpr float ValueEpsilon = 0.05f;

    // ƒ][ƒ“‚Ì”»’èŒ‹‰Ê
    enum class ResistanceType
    {
        Constant, // ˆê’è’ïR
        Changing, // •Ï‰»’ïR
        Complex,  // •¡G‚ÈƒJ[ƒu
    };

    // ƒ][ƒ“‚Ìí—Ş‚ğ”»’è‚·‚é
    inline ResistanceType ClassifyZone(const Zone& zone)
    {
		// ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ª‚R“_ˆÈã‚È‚ç•¡G‚ÈƒJ[ƒu‚Æ‚İ‚È‚·
        if (zone.points.size() >= 3)
        {
            return ResistanceType::Complex;
        }

		// 2“_ˆÈ‰º‚È‚çAÅ‰‚ÆÅŒã‚Ì’l‚Ì·‚ğŒ©‚Äˆê’è’ïR‚©”»’f‚·‚é
        float diff = std::abs(zone.points.front().value - zone.points.back().value);
        if (diff < ValueEpsilon)
        {
            return ResistanceType::Constant;
        }
        return ResistanceType::Changing;
    }
}

namespace AdaptiveTrigger
{
    inline float ComputeForce(const TriggerEffect& effect, float currentPosition)
    {
        // Œ»İ’n‚Ì’ïR’l‚ğæ“¾
        float force = SampleCurve(effect.forcePoints, currentPosition);
        // ”ÍˆÍŠO•ÛŒ¯
        return std::clamp(force, 0.0f, 1.0f); 
    }
} 
 
===== WaveDemo.h ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Unsorted\Demo\WaveDemo.h 
#pragma once
#include <entt.hpp>
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <DirectXMath.h>
#include "Engine/Rendering/ShapeRenderer.h"

class WaveDemo
{
public:
	void Initialize(
		ID3D11Device* device,
		entt::registry& registry,
		int gridSize,
		float spacing);

	void Update(float elapsedTime) { time += elapsedTime; }

	void Render(
		entt::registry& registry,
		ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection,
		const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& lightDirection);

private:
	struct Tag
	{
		float baseX = 0.0f;
		float baseZ = 0.0f;
		float phase = 0.0f;
	};

	ShapeRenderer renderer;
	float time = 0.0f;
	float spacing = 1.0f;
}; 
 
===== WaveDemo.cpp ===== 
Path: C:\Users\2240140\Desktop\New-Library\Source\Unsorted\Demo\WaveDemo.cpp 
#include "WaveDemo.h"

void WaveDemo::Initialize(
	ID3D11Device* device,
	entt::registry& registry,
	int gridSize,
	float spacingValue)
{
	renderer = ShapeRenderer(device);
	spacing = spacingValue;

	for (int x = 0; x < gridSize; x++)
	{
		for (int z = 0; z < gridSize; z++)
		{
			entt::entity e = registry.create();

			Tag tag;
			tag.baseX = (x - gridSize * 0.5f) * spacing;
			tag.baseZ = (z - gridSize * 0.5f) * spacing;
			tag.phase = (tag.baseX + tag.baseZ) * 0.3f;

			registry.emplace<Tag>(e, tag);
		}
	}
}

void WaveDemo::Render(
	entt::registry& registry,
	ID3D11DeviceContext* dc,
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& view, 
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4& projection, 
	const DirectX::XMFLOAT3& lightDirection)
{
	auto view_ = registry.view<Tag>();
	for (auto entity : view_)
	{
		auto& tag = view_.get<Tag>(entity);

		float y = sinf(time * 2.0f + tag.phase) * 0.5f;

		DirectX::XMMATRIX m = DirectX::XMMatrixTranslation(tag.baseX, y, tag.baseZ);
		DirectX::XMFLOAT4X4 transform;
		DirectX::XMStoreFloat4x4(&transform, m);

		DirectX::XMFLOAT3 size = { spacing, spacing, spacing };

		float seed = sinf(tag.baseX * 12.9898f + tag.baseZ * 78.233f) * 43758.5453f;
		float variation = seed - floorf(seed);

		DirectX::XMFLOAT4 color = {
			0.75f + variation * 0.12f,
			0.88f - variation * 0.06f,
			1.0f - variation * 0.03f,
			1.0f
		};

		renderer.DrawBox(transform, size, color);
	}

	renderer.Render(dc, view, projection, lightDirection, false);
}
